## Batch 86 — The master Discipline search found empty or declined (Z-main, 2026-10-19, tracing harvested records; owner, 2026-10-19: 86.1 confirmed)

**86.1** 81.1's search taking nothing (no master Discipline card the diablerist could take in library, hand or ash heap) or declined (its Pass): the library is shuffled all the same: the controller goes through the library either way (advanced rules 4: "may go through … and then shuffle"); a seat with no card to take is not asked, its library shuffled. Confirmed (owner, 2026-10-19). Found tracing two harvested records whose games diverge at that shuffle (05d1a4123dfe22af, 81e9bf16b18eb65c; 7d7d339). (Test a_diablerist_of_an_older_vampire_takes_a_master_discipline, the younger victim's case aside.)

## Batch 87 — Round 19 (the Imbued, their powers and convictions): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-21)

Thirty-seven cards (20 imbued, 14 powers, 3 convictions) and the rules of the rulebook's "Appendix: Imbued Rules" (an imbued a mortal ally, life its cost, creeds and virtues; convictions; powers; the incapacitated region; monster and mortal), graded per the add-cards skill: four blind second readers read the cards and the appendix, their reads diffed against the code. Red: 87.1–87.7, 87.42 and 87.43, answered by the owner (2026-10-22): 87.42 and 87.43 corrected.

**87.1** An incapacitated imbued's printed text and powers stay usable ("controlled but not ready", as a vampire's text works in torpor; Abjure's ruling, LSJ 20081016): Béatrice's damage and Earl's burn each unlock phase, Anna's look, Paul's move, Maman's 3 votes against for a conviction. A reader agreed for the riders and the phase abilities but would not let an incapacitated Maman vote (a torpid vampire casts no votes). Question (implemented reading): Maman incapacitated may still burn a conviction for her 3 votes. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Tests maman_buys_three_votes_against_with_a_conviction, beatrice_damages_another_of_her_imbued_each_unlock_phase.)

**87.2** An effect sending an imbued to torpor (a strike's "send to torpor", Touch of Oblivion [OBL]) incapacitates it, the appendix's analogue of torpor (advanced rules §5.3: "incapacitation is the imbued analogue"); an ally sent to torpor is burned (advanced rules 5.3.3). No ruling. Question (implemented reading): incapacitated, not burned. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Code path; `Engine::minions_to_torpor`.)

**87.3** Illuminate: "any monster may enter combat with this imbued as a Ⓓ action" — another Methuselah's monster only (the Ⓓ, and rushes at another's minion: Haven Uncovered's shape). A reader would let the imbued's own controller's monster take it, undirected. Question (implemented reading): another Methuselah's only. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test illuminate_gives_imbued_intercept_and_invites_a_rush.)

**87.4** A power's [ACTION] with "[1 CONVICTION]" (Inspire, Rejuvenate, Respire's mend): the conviction named as the action is announced, burned as it resolves, the action fizzling if it is gone by then — as Travis's "action that costs 1 conviction" (RTR 20111202) and every action's cost (3.6). A reader read the appendix's "lock the power card (and pay whatever cost the effect requires)" as paid as the action is announced, lost on a block. Question (implemented reading): paid at resolution. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test inspire_moves_the_conviction_it_paid_to_another_imbued.)

**87.5** Rejuvenate's and Respire's "add 1 blood to a vampire or 1 life to any other ally": "other" than this imbued — the imbued itself not a target. A reader read "other" than a vampire, the imbued a target. Question (implemented reading): not itself. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Code path; `MEND_ACTION`'s `OtherThanActor`.)

**87.6** Surge: "burn all surge counters on this imbued at the end of the action" — counters gained in a combat outside any action stay until an action ends (`Hook::ActionEnds`). A reader would burn them as that combat ends. Question (implemented reading): until an action ends. Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-22): "in theory I suppose, but there is no combat outside of an action" — the reading stands, its case unmet. (Test surge_counters_prevent_damage_and_go_with_the_action.)

**87.7** Xian: "if Xian successfully blocks a monster and is still ready at the end of the action" — the monster read as the action ends (the gain's moment). A reader would read it as the block succeeds. Question (implemented reading): at the end. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test xian_gains_a_conviction_after_blocking_a_monster.)

**87.8** An imbued is a mortal ally (`MinionKind::Imbued`, `Minion::is_ally`, `is_imbued`): no vampire (no clan, sect, capacity, title, votes), its creed and virtues printed as clan and Disciplines in the crypt table (vocabularies of clans and Disciplines leave them out), 1 strength and 1 bleed, its cost its starting life; the "mortal ally" readers (`GameState::ally_of`: Mortal) read it so; a monster is a vampire or an ally neither mortal nor animal (`is_monster`). Influenced like a vampire, life from the counters. Green (rulebook "Appendix: Imbued Rules" 1, 4). (Test an_imbued_is_a_mortal_ally_its_cost_its_life.)

**87.9** Convictions: played in their player's unlock phase on an imbued of theirs, one each imbued each phase (`Minion::conviction_played`), from hand or ash heap (the ash heap's in the virtual hand, `ASH_UNLOCK`), an incapacitated imbued included; above five, burned; an imbued entering play with none may gain one from library, hand or ash heap (`Interrupt::GainConviction`), the hand's replaced (AllySearch's precedent); "gaining" one is no play (no unlock-phase use spent). Liz gains none. Green; the incapacitated receiver amber. (Tests convictions_are_played_in_the_unlock_phase_one_each_imbued, a_sixth_conviction_is_burned, an_imbued_influenced_out_gains_a_conviction.)

**87.10** "[n CONVICTION]" on an ability: n conviction cards of its imbued burned as it is used, its player choosing which when they differ (`Interrupt::BurnConvictions`); none to pay, not offered. Green (rulebook "Appendix: Imbued Rules" 2). (Tests peter_burns_a_conviction_to_maneuver_against_a_monster, maman_buys_three_votes_against_with_a_conviction.)

**87.11** Powers: an imbued's +1 stealth action (`ActionKind::Power`), requiring the power's virtue, the power on it if successful, the imbued then unlocked; no second copy (masked, fizzling if one arrived since). An iconed ability locks the power card and pays its cost; "always on" text works locked; a [REACTION] is usable as reactions are — the imbued ready, unlocked or woken, in another Methuselah's action (`Requirement::HostMayReact`; LSJ 20070403), Vigilance's "by a locked imbued" without the unlock (`HostMayReactLocked`). Green. (Tests a_power_is_gained_by_a_plus_one_stealth_action_the_imbued_unlocking, discern_gives_intercept_against_an_acting_monster, vigilance_unlocks_a_locked_imbued_as_a_reaction.)

**87.12** The incapacitated region: an imbued with no life is incapacitated, not burned — controlled, not ready, its cards with it; a "burn" effect still burns it; it leaves for 3 conviction in its controller's unlock phase (every imbued's shared ability), gaining 1 life up to its starting life, as leaving by any effect; any minion may burn one as a Ⓓ action (`ActionKind::BurnImbued`, undirected at one's own as a diablerie), taking its equipment; then each ready imbued, any Methuselah's, may burn a conviction for 1 unpreventable damage to the actor (`Interrupt::AvengeImbued`). Incapacitation is neither burn nor torpor: no "burned or sent to torpor" rider fires (advanced rules §5.7.5). Its region is the protocol's own (`Zone::Incapacitated`, `SeatView.incapacitated`, the `incapacitated` event; Z-main, 2026-10-21). Green. (Tests an_imbued_with_no_life_is_incapacitated_and_recovers_for_three_convictions, an_incapacitated_imbued_is_burned_by_an_action_and_avenged.)

**87.13** Crypt cards met by earlier cards (the appendix's card rulings): "a vampire in your uncontrolled region" never names an imbued (`uncontrolled_target_qualifies` gates it to a filter naming one: Paul, Travis, Inspire); Lázár takes from a prey's imbued (a blind target) but gives to a vampire only, his "vampire in your uncontrolled region" one too; the Soul Gem of Etrius brings an imbued younger than its bearer in with no life, incapacitated; cards naming "a vampire" in an ash heap skip an imbued (`state::vampire_card`). Green. (Tests lazar_takes_from_an_imbued_but_gives_to_vampires_only, the_soul_gem_brings_an_imbued_in_with_no_life.)

**87.14** Anna: her action's vampire chosen as announced, any Methuselah's, directed when another's; the look a phase ability, the cards told to her alone (the `looked` event's `zone: library`). Green. (Test anna_gives_two_blood_from_the_bank_to_any_vampire.)

**87.15** Béatrice: the damaged imbued chosen by her controller, another than her, ready; none, nothing. Green. (Test beatrice_damages_another_of_her_imbued_each_unlock_phase.)

**87.16** Earl: his stealth on any action not to enter combat; his count of ready imbued includes him. Amber (both reads). (Test earl_burns_a_conviction_with_more_than_two_ready_imbued.)

**87.17** Erick: a combat from his action's success (not a block), first round before range, the opponent a monster, once; the conviction from hand or ash heap. Amber ("before combat begins" taken as before range in its first round). (Test erick_gains_a_conviction_rushing_a_monster.)

**87.18** François: another Methuselah's action naming him as its target costs its acting minion 1 blood or life as it resolves (with the action's cost, Ambrosio's precedent), unpayable it fizzles; once whatever else it targets. Amber. (Test actions_at_francois_cost_another_blood.)

**87.19** Inez: a power whose requirement includes innocence, from library (shuffled) or hand (replaced), put on her, no action; before any contest (LSJ 20080526). Amber. (Test inez_searches_a_power_requiring_innocence_as_she_enters.)

**87.20** Jack, Marion, Pedro, Jennifer: printed modifiers (a first-round maneuver; a press; +1 strength, no maneuver to long range, no press to end, no "combat ends" strike; a weapon 1 pool or blood less, +1 bleed, -1 stealth). Green. (Tests jack_gets_a_maneuver_in_the_first_round, marion_gets_a_press_each_combat, pedro_cannot_maneuver_to_long_range, jennifer_pays_a_pool_less_for_weapons.)

**87.21** Jennie: any Methuselah's incapacitated imbued may leave for 2 conviction in any Methuselah's unlock phase while she is in play, gaining 1 life (the rule's, not 2). Amber (both reads). (Test jennie_lets_any_incapacitated_imbued_recover_for_two_convictions.)

**87.22** John: another Methuselah's ready monster, the conviction named as announced and burned as the action resolves (Travis's ruling). Amber. (Test john_rushes_a_monster_for_a_conviction.)

**87.23** Leaf: locking him the cost, the search offered with no conviction to find (a legal no-op, the library shuffled). Amber. (Test leaf_locks_to_gain_a_conviction_from_the_library.)

**87.24** Lupe: one use each action for stealth or intercept together, each only when needed. Amber (D15). (Test lupe_with_a_vehicle_buys_stealth_with_a_conviction.)

**87.25** Paul: outside the transfer rules, the last pool ousting; usable as he arrives (RTR 20180303). Amber. (Tests paul_moves_a_pool_counter_to_an_uncontrolled_imbued, paul_moves_a_counter_in_the_influence_phase_he_arrives.)

**87.26** Peter: "while" (not "during", 79.3): again each time paid. Amber. (Test peter_burns_a_conviction_to_maneuver_against_a_monster.)

**87.27** Travis: the conviction named as the action is announced (RTR 20111202), burned at resolution, the action fizzling if it is gone. Green. (Test travis_names_his_conviction_and_pays_it_at_resolution.)

**87.28** Abjure: any combat between a monster and a mortal, its imbued in it or not, incapacitated too (LSJ 20081016); locking both the imbued and the power; the conviction only when the mortal is another minion. Green. Any Methuselah's combat, a third one's imbued polled after the combatants' Methuselahs (98.1). (Tests abjure_ends_a_combat_between_a_monster_and_a_mortal, abjure_is_used_by_an_incapacitated_imbued, a_third_methuselahs_abjure_ends_another_pairs_combat.)

**87.29** Champion: the action fails at once and the acting monster fights its imbued, the action going on until the combat ends (LSJ 20070217), failed, not blocked (LSJ 20070219), never during a block attempt (ANK 20260316). Green. (Tests champion_turns_a_monsters_action_into_a_combat_with_its_imbued, champion_is_not_used_during_a_block_attempt.)

**87.30** Donate: each mode burns the card; its +1 bleed limited and before resolution (ANK 20231124); its strength for the combatant's remaining combat. Green. (Test donate_gives_another_mortal_a_bleed.)

**87.31** Foresee: the opposing minion's controller's hand looked at, then the combat may be canceled; the imbued blocking, the action continues as if unblocked (rules-qa 3.4; RTR 19941109). Green. (Test foresee_looks_and_cancels_the_combat_the_bleed_going_on.)

**87.32** Hide: its modifier's -1 intercept for monsters, as announced; its reaction failing a monster's action at its imbued; neither with an Illuminate on it (only their use barred). Green. (Test hide_fails_a_monsters_action_at_its_imbued.)

**87.33** Illuminate: +1 intercept for every imbued blocking a monster, once whatever the copies, none on top of The Unmasking's; burned in its controller's next unlock phase or as its imbued is incapacitated. Green. (Tests illuminate_gives_imbued_intercept_and_invites_a_rush, illuminate_burns_as_its_imbued_is_incapacitated.)

**87.34** Project: "monster allies who cost less than 4 pool" read narrowly: an ally printed with a pool cost below 4 (one costing blood is not barred). Green (default reading).

**87.35** Respire: its damage environmental, out of combat; its "if successful" gain after a fizzle too (56.1, 78.38). Amber. (Test respire_damages_a_small_vampire_and_gains_a_conviction.)

**87.36** Shame: the opposing monster's strength as it is now, environmental damage (RTR 19970630), then the combat ends; the card burns. Green. (Test shame_turns_a_monsters_strength_on_it_and_ends_combat.)

**87.37** React with Conviction: a card taking control of its imbued, an action card requiring one of the five Disciplines aimed at it, or such a strike by its opponent, canceled as played, no cost paid; the canceled actor stays unlocked (LSJ 20060425). Green. (Test react_with_conviction_cancels_a_steal_of_its_imbued.)

**87.38** Second Sight: one per imbued per action whichever mode; its intercept only when needed. Green. (Test second_sight_gives_intercept_against_a_monster.)

**87.39** Strike with Conviction: burned as the strike's cost, the strike made (a card in play burned for its strike leaves nothing to lose, unlike a weapon gone: spec §4.10.4); its bleed burns it as it resolves, kept if blocked (LSJ 20060421). Green. (Tests strike_with_conviction_hits_a_monster_harder, strike_with_conviction_bleeds_and_burns_only_as_it_resolves, strike_with_convictions_blocked_bleed_keeps_it.)

**87.40** Found by the tests: allies' Discipline checks read an imbued's virtues as its own (`cards::has_disciplines`); Béatrice's pick was optional (a missing mandatory purpose); a conviction moved to another Methuselah's imbued changed owner (copies conservation, the fuzz). Green.

**87.41** Strike with Conviction's "weapon strike" is any weapon's (guns too) and is the weapon's own strike: the weapon's requirements and limit hold (Sniper Rifle at long range only, Sawed-Off once each combat), its riders stand (Waxen Poetica burned, AK-47's additional strike), and the +1 is damage of the weapon's kind (ranged, aggravated: RTR 19970630); every pool weapon's strike deals damage, so RTR 19960221 has no case. Amber: the precedent is Lucky Blow's and Lam Into's "use a melee weapon strike", one routine for both (`Engine::weapon_strike_plus`), the target's filter choosing the weapons; found by the tests (a gun panicked the old melee-only path). (Test strike_with_convictions_added_damage_is_the_weapons.)

**87.42** A gun's maneuver commits its strike (LSJ 20090528-2-2), and Strike with Conviction may still make that gun's strike at +1: a strike committed to a weapon is that weapon's strike however it is made, through a card or an ability using it ("same way they could use Target Vitals on the gun strike"). The committed strike's options are the weapon's own and every play or ability whose `WeaponStrikePlus` names it. Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-22): yes, the engine changed. (Tests strike_with_conviction_strikes_with_the_gun_its_maneuver_committed, strike_with_convictions_added_damage_is_the_weapons.)

**87.43** Rejuvenate's "during your unlock phase, if this imbued has fewer life than his or her starting amount, he or she gains 1 life" works on an incapacitated imbued (0 life): it gains the life and stays incapacitated — life gained is not leaving the region, which only the appendix's rule does (3 conviction, +1 life). Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-22): "it gains life, it does not get them out of incapacitated", the engine changed (no `HostReady` on the rider). (Test rejuvenate_gives_life_to_an_incapacitated_imbued_left_there.)

## Batch 88 — Open contradictions that change play (rules-digest §23: K5–K7, K15, K16, K18, K23, K26, K27, K29): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-22; owner, 2026-10-22: 88.1, 88.8, 88.9 confirmed, 88.4 answered: 5.8 kept)

Each contradiction read against the KRCG rulings, the advanced rules and the rulebook, graded per the add-cards skill: a blind second reader read the sources, its reads diffed against the code. Red: 88.1, 88.4, 88.8, 88.9 (each revising an owner item), answered by the owner (2026-10-22).

**88.1** Yawp Court's combat leaving the acting vampire not ready: the action ends (LSJ 20060902) having reached resolution, its cost paid first (LSJ 20080201): successful with no effect, as a fizzle (56.1); NRA holds; nothing more is played (RTR 20180719); a queued combat is lost (LSJ 20090826); Daring the Dawn's damage is still taken (ANK 20220331, as 23.3's ended actions). Revises 54.1's "failed, with no after-resolution window". Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test yawp_court_ends_the_action_after_resolution_daring_the_dawn_still_burns.)

**88.2** A cost that runs a Methuselah's pool out mid-action (the bleed's target paying to cancel Spying Mission [OBF]): the oust's decision point comes first (45.4: Life Boon), before the bleed resolves (LSJ 20050607); saved, they are bled as normal; not saved, the oust, then the bleed fizzles (24.1). 24.1's oust follows the Boon declined (45.4): no conflict. Green. (Tests life_boon_saves_a_cancel_cost_before_the_bleed_resolves, spying_mission_canceled_lets_the_bleed_resolve_after_the_oust.)

**88.3** An ally paying its last life: for its own action's cost, the action resolves in full, then it burns (LSJ 20080512: Rotting Behemoth's rush successful, no combat; Herald of Topheth), the oust it causes first (advanced rules §6.5.2); for an action modifier, it burns and the action fails (ANK 20180928-1); for a strike, combat ends before strike resolution; for a block toll, its attempt fails (41.4). Narrows the default reading's "its attempt/effect then fails". Green. (Tests rotting_behemoth_paying_its_last_life_for_its_rush_burns_without_combat, an_ally_paying_its_last_life_as_a_vampire_resolves_the_action_then_burns, spectral_servitor_paying_its_last_life_for_a_modifier_burns_and_the_action_fails, sergio_bueno_makes_corrupted_minions_burn_blood_or_life_to_block_him.)

**88.4** Between a diablerie and its blood hunt only the cards keyed to the diablerie are played: Slake the Thirst (68.2) and Taking the Skin: Minion (RTR 19991206: "must be played before the Blood Hunt", so not after it); after-resolution cards (Freak Drive) wait for the after-resolution window after the blood hunt (5.8), over advanced rules §2.3.4 and §3.7.8.1. Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-22): 5.8 kept. (Tests taking_the_skin_on_a_diablerie_comes_before_the_blood_hunt, slake_the_thirst_after_a_diablerie.)

**88.5** Anarch Revolt's ready Anarch is read as the unlock phase ends (LSJ 20080106; advanced rules §5.2.2): its burn is not ordered among the unlock-phase effects; it resolves as the active Methuselah passes, before the impulse passes (6.1, LSJ 20100723). Revises 6.1's "the condition is read when the effect resolves". Green (the card's ruling). (Tests anarch_revolt_burns_after_the_only_anarch_left_during_the_unlock_phase, anarch_revolt_burns_as_the_active_methuselah_passes_before_the_impulse_passes.)

**88.6** Elen Kamjian with no blood: "unless she must hunt" — the rulebook hunt (17.1); unlocked after it, with blood, while you control a locked minion, her bleed is still owed (advanced rules §3.9.2: not yet discharged). Green. (Test elen_kamjian_hunts_instead_when_she_has_no_blood.)

**88.7** "Only when needed" is a threshold, not a cap (rulebook "Stealth and intercept"; advanced rules §3.2.1; Angelica, LSJ 20010111): once stealth (or intercept) is needed, a play may give more than needed; a play once it is no longer needed is illegal — 3.2's "surplus plays". Green. (Tests stealth_only_when_needed, angelica_discards_masters_for_intercept.)

**88.8** The Meddling of Semsith leaving play mid-phase — its player ousted (LSJ 20021008: "removed from play and its effect cancelled") or the card burned: the replacements it held are drawn at once, unless another copy still holds them. Revises 70.4's last clause. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test meddling_gone_mid_phase_draws_the_held_replacements_at_once.)

**88.9** A locquipment changing controller goes on any minion of the new controller able to hold it, a torpid one too; burned only with none (RTR 19960112: Living Manse, Disputed Territory, Dominate Kine; advanced rules §6.3.2). Revises 31.4 and 39.4's "ready" (LSJ 19990928, not in the KRCG rulings). Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-22). (Test a_locquipment_goes_to_a_torpid_minion_and_burns_with_none_to_hold_it.)

**88.10** Obedience before the combat Psyche! [CEL] queues: an unlocked older vampire about to fight the acting younger vampire again plays it between the combats (LSJ 19991025); the action ends with no second combat, failed, NRA holding (LSJ 20070709) — blocked if a block began the combats. Not against Hedonism's combat, whose vampire is not the acting one (56.2), nor Fast Reaction's, whose vampire locks first. Green (the card's ruling). (Test obedience_avoids_the_combat_psyche_queues.)

**88.11** Promise of 1528 likewise: the Giovanni "about to enter combat with a Camarilla vampire" in Psyche!'s combat — the locked Giovanni blocker facing the Camarilla actor, or the acting Giovanni facing a Camarilla opponent — ends the action before it, blocked if a block began the combats (as Promise played on the block), failed otherwise. Amber: 88.10's ruling on the same template; both reads agree. (Test promise_of_1528_ends_the_action_before_psyches_combat.)

## Batch 89 — Round 20 (the cards players asked for, the two-player list's last two): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-23; owner, 2026-10-23: 89.1 corrected, 89.2 confirmed)

Twenty-seven cards (the server's /admin/unsupported of 2026-10-23: a Kiasyd deck, a Lasombra one, Catacombs, Humanitas, Nocturn; Fee Stake: Corte and Los Angeles, VEKN's two-player list), graded per the add-cards skill: three blind second readers read the cards, their KRCG rulings and the advanced rules, their reads diffed against the code. Red: 89.1, 89.2.

**89.1** Harvest Rites's "once each turn, when this vampire successfully hunts": a hunt at capacity is successful — it gets its blood, which drains off — and spends the once (owner: "the fact the blood bleeds off does not stop the hunt from getting the blood and being successful", 63.12); the bonus is the hunt's, from its target (RTR 20030519, RTR 20180511). The engine first read LSJ 20050720-2 (Immaculate Vitae: no blood gained) as covering it. Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-23): corrected. (Test harvest_rites_adds_a_blood_to_one_hunt_each_turn.)

**89.2** Quincy, The Trapper: the first successful action at an ally recorded on Quincy, the second, in a later turn of any of his controller's (ANK 20170202), burns it; kept while both stay in play — through Quincy's torpor, a change of control, a reseating — the second action needing the ally's controller to be his predator or prey then. Red (no precedent), confirmed by the owner (2026-10-23). (Test quincy_burns_an_ally_with_two_actions_in_two_turns.)

**89.3** Chanjelin Ward: on a vampire its player controls, one a vampire (`TargetWithout`); "other Methuselahs' actions targeting this vampire cost an additional blood" on any one of the action's targets (LSJ 20080809-2, RTR 20081119), other than the Ward's controller's, read live; paid with the action's cost at resolution, unpayable it fizzles (3.6); blood only, so no ally's action at the vampire is offered (73.5, D3). Amber (the reads agree). (Tests chanjelin_ward_makes_actions_at_its_vampire_cost_a_blood, chanjelin_ward_bars_an_allys_action_at_its_vampire, chanjelin_ward_goes_on_your_vampire_one_each.)

**89.4** Chanjelin Ward's burn: the vampire's current controller decides (ANK 20210309-3), in torpor too (D25), not during a block attempt nor after a block (ANK 20260316, LSJ 20041022); the failed action reached resolution (NRA), was not blocked (ANK 20211015, LSJ 20070219). Green. (Tests chanjelin_ward_burns_to_fail_an_action_at_its_vampire, chanjelin_ward_is_not_burned_during_a_block_attempt.)

**89.5** Great Symposium: a master requiring Kiasyd (a ready Kiasyd controlled); one Kiasyd searched, the search may fail (ANK 20210304, LSJ 20100306); then all 3 blood from the bank, one at a time, among the Kiasyd in the uncontrolled region (`Effect::BankBloodAmongUncontrolled`), none with no Kiasyd there; once a game, anyone's copy (`NotPlayedThisGame`). Green. (Test great_symposium_searches_a_kiasyd_and_distributes_three_blood.)

**89.6** Stone Travel [MYT]: its stealth only when needed (3.2), its cancel with it; blocked, the acting vampire may cancel the combat (`Effect::MayCancelCombatIfBlocked`, `Choice::CancelCombat`) as it begins: the action stays blocked, no bleed. Not Mirror Walk's end before block resolution. Amber (the reads agree). (Test stone_travel_superior_may_cancel_the_combat_after_a_block.)

**89.7** Aura Absorption [aus], Stone Travel [obt]: the blood burned is an effect, not reduced, and the card not played without it (ANK 20210226); [aus] an intercept play, as Aura's own play, not a granted ability (the window matrix's rider pattern narrowed). Green. (Tests burn_blood_modes_need_the_blood, stone_travel_and_aura_absorption_trade_stealth_and_intercept.)

**89.8** Fae Contortion [myt]: "a grapple card" is a card printing "Grapple." (the generated label Michaela, Bollix and Charismatic Aura read); in the pool only Immortal Grapple is one. A reader would take only the cards named "… Grapple": the same cards in the pool. Amber (no pool card differs). (Tests fae_contortion_cancels_a_grapple_as_it_is_played, fae_contortion_maneuvers_and_cancels_only_grapples.)

**89.9** Gremlins [myt]: Ⓓ at the equipment's controller (LSJ 20090324), undirected at one's own; "melee weapons" are weapons with a melee strike (`CardFilter::MeleeWeapon`), one with both a melee and a ranged strike included; a locquipment in play is no equipment. Green. (Test gremlins_burns_a_non_melee_equipment_or_bleeds.)

**89.10** Goblinism: [pot] Ⓓ at the location's controller (LSJ 20090324); [myt] the location (a locquipment too) shown and put in hand, the library shuffled, the hand discarded down after; [MYT] a haven (the generated "Haven." label: Secure Haven, Zaire River Ferry) may go on the acting vampire instead, not played in the normal fashion (119.1), its cost paid as normal (Therbold Realty's discount included), only if it holds no haven yet — chosen before the discard down (the card goes "instead" of the hand). One haven a minion, Secure Haven's and the Ferry's "only one haven" alike (`ONE_HAVEN`: Secure Haven not put on the Ferry's bearer, the Ferry not equipped by a Secure Haven's; revises 70.20). Amber (the reads agree). (Tests goblinism_burns_a_location, goblinism_finds_a_location_and_may_put_a_haven_on, one_haven_a_minion_whichever_it_is.)

**89.11** Guarded Rubrics: requires a ready Black Hand vampire; the bearer's bleed at +2 for 1 blood, the action fizzling if the Rubrics left it (RTR 19960221); "Gehenna cards" the cards printing "Gehenna." (the generated label), costing every Methuselah 1 more pool while the bearer is ready (`LabelPoolSurcharge::while_host_ready`). Amber. (Test guarded_rubrics_bleeds_and_taxes_gehenna_cards.)

**89.12** Humanitas: on a ready vampire its player controls; rescuing, +1 stealth and 1 blood less whoever pays (PIB 20110918, LSJ 20020304-2); the vampire commits no diablerie (advanced rules §3.7.8). Catacombs: the same for its bearer, the acting rescuer (a Catacombs on the rescued vampire does nothing); a locquipment (LSJ 20080114). Green. (Tests humanitas_helps_rescues_and_bars_diablerie, catacombs_helps_its_vampire_rescue.)

**89.13** Harvest Rites: a Sabbat vampire's +1 stealth action, put on it, one a vampire. Green. (Test harvest_rites_adds_a_blood_to_one_hunt_each_turn.)

**89.14** Nocturn [obt]: Spectral Servitor's shape — acts the turn it is recruited, in its Methuselah's minion phase only (ANK 20200930); its recruiter unlocked on its first successful recruit this turn; plays non-ally cards requiring basic [obt] as an independent vampire of capacity 1 (`AsVampire::non_ally`; LSJ 20020503), so not another Nocturn; burned in its controller's unlock phase, after Khobar Towers if its player orders so (ANK 20170625). Green. (Tests nocturn_acts_at_once_plays_obtenebration_and_burns_at_unlock, khobar_towers_takes_nocturn_before_it_burns.)

**89.15** Nocturn [OBT]: each successful [OBT] recruit lets its recruiter recruit Nocturn once more this turn (`Minion::repeats`), each costing +1 blood for each earlier one this turn, blocked ones counted (NRA's: reached resolution); a repeat at [obt] allowed, granting none. Amber (the reads agree). (Test nocturn_superior_lets_its_recruiter_recruit_again_at_a_higher_cost.)

**89.16** Ravager: on any Kiasyd (33.3); only changeling allies (the generated label: Mylan Horseed, who cannot block vampires) may block its undirected hunts; its directed actions blockable as normal. Green. (Test ravager_bars_non_changelings_from_blocking_its_kiasyds_hunt.)

**89.17** Song of Pan: the acting Kiasyd unlocked, the card in play; every Methuselah's Kiasyd and changeling allies +1 bleed (D11; `Modifier::SubtypeStat`); in its controller's discard phase it burns and each of their Kiasyd (torpid too) and changeling allies that did not bleed successfully this turn takes 1 unpreventable damage (`Effect::DamageUnbledThisTurn`). Amber (the reads agree). (Test song_of_pan_raises_kiasyd_bleeds_and_hurts_those_that_did_not_bleed.)

**89.18** Watchtower: Four Ride Forth: requires a ready Seraph (`ControlsReadySeraph`); in its controller's discard phase, locked to unlock any Methuselah's ready Black Hand vampire (D11), not one already unlocked (48.7: an unlock alone, no card cycled); burned as another watchtower — any Methuselah's — enters play (`Hook::LabelEntersPlay`). Amber (48.7). (Test watchtower_unlocks_a_black_hand_vampire_and_burns_for_another.)

**89.19** Fee Stake: Corte, Fee Stake: Los Angeles: as Fee Stake: Perth (an Anarch of capacity 5 or more, Baron of the city; contested face down, LSJ 20070808-2). Green. (Test fee_stakes_corte_and_los_angeles_make_a_baron.)

**89.20** Isanwayen: locked in his controller's master phase while ready for +1 master phase action; rescuing him costs 1 more blood, any rescue's (a card's too), read as announced and as it resolves (48.8). Amber (a reader asked about card rescues; the reads agree). (Tests isanwayen_locks_for_a_master_phase_action, rescuing_isanwayen_costs_one_more_blood.)

**89.21** The Arcadian (Cybele's bleed at +1 with +1 stealth), Omme Enberbenight (Ludmijla's discount on [myt] modes, 38.4), Pherydima (Dónal's unlock in the discard phase, for a pool, in torpor too): green. (Tests the_arcadian_bleeds_at_plus_one_for_a_blood, omme_pays_less_for_mytherceria_cards, pherydima_burns_a_pool_to_unlock_in_the_discard_phase.)

**89.22** Roderick Phillips March: while ready, +1 stealth on the equip actions of every minion his controller controls, himself included; an equip action as 78.28 and 80.44 read it (`PrintedCtx::equip_action`). A reader asked whether a move from another minion counts: it does not (78.28). Green. (Test roderick_gives_stealth_on_equip_actions.)

**89.23** Andrew Emory, Dame Hollerton, Randel, The Coward print no text; Banjoko's Black Hand and Seraph are generated traits (LSJ 20070322, ANK 20180807). Green. (Test the_textless_round_20_vampires_have_no_definition_and_their_traits.)

**89.24** Mylan Horseed is a changeling (his type line), so two texts 77.32 and the definitions called inert now act: Winterlich's "while he is ready, changelings cost you 1 fewer pool" (his and his Methuselah's other minions' recruits; his "as if he met the requirements": 115.3) and Black Forest Base's "changeling allies can burn this location as a +1 stealth Ⓓ action". Revises 77.32. Green (a gap found on the way). (Tests winterlich_makes_changelings_cost_one_pool_less, black_forest_base_is_burned_by_a_changeling_ally.)

**89.25** Foresee's "you may then choose to cancel combat" is offered as `Choice::CancelCombat` (Stone Travel's), no longer as `ContinueAction` (Form of Mist's continue): the choice is the cancel, continuing only when the imbued blocked (87.31). No reading changes. Green. (Tests foresee_looks_and_cancels_the_combat_the_bleed_going_on, foresees_continued_action_asks_no_decliner_again.)

**89.26** "Successfully hunts" (Adisa, Eddie Gaines, Enhanced Coagulant, Restricted Vitae's "until a vampire successfully hunts", Harvest Rites, Songs of the Distant Vitae's "successfully hunted", The Anarch Free Press; any hunt, a card's too, 2026-11-11): a hunt at capacity is successful, its blood got and drained off (owner, 89.1; 63.12); a vampire that cannot gain blood by hunting does not (59.4). Abactor's "successful resolution" is the same successful hunt (63.2 as corrected, 2026-11-11, LSJ 20090411). Before, the engine fired these only on blood kept. Green (the owner's 89.1, generalized; found on the way). (Tests a_hunt_at_capacity_is_a_successful_hunt, harvest_rites_adds_a_blood_to_one_hunt_each_turn.)

## Batch 90 — Open contradictions, the rest (rules-digest §23: K14, K25, K30, K31, K35, K37, K41–K44): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-23; owner, 2026-10-24: 90.4 confirmed, 90.6 and 90.7 answered)

Each contradiction read against the KRCG rulings, the advanced rules and the rulebook, graded per the add-cards skill, and played out against the engine. Red: 90.4, 90.6, 90.7, answered by the owner (2026-10-24).

**90.1** Dawn Operation's "all damage inflicted on vampires during the resulting combat is aggravated" takes every source, strikes, environmental and retainer damage alike (PIB 20150603-1; advanced rules §4.4.3); Psychic Assault stays normal by its own text (62.26): the two agree, no contradiction (K25). Green. (Tests dawn_operation_aggravates_the_combat_damage, dawn_operation_does_not_aggravate_catatonic_fears_damage.)

**90.2** The rulebook's allies box for an ally playing a card as a vampire: its life is its blood for costs and effects, life over its printed life does not drain, aggravated damage costs it life as normal and it does not go to torpor, an effect sending it to torpor burns it (LSJ 20060409); its capacity the granted one (73.6), 1 when none is printed (K42). Vivienne Géroux's Daring the Dawn [for] costs her 2 life. Green. (Test vivienne_loses_life_to_her_own_daring_the_dawn.)

**90.3** Vote reductions are netted (ANK 20251125, LSJ 20050221-2): a vampire can have net negative votes, kept against votes gained later, and only its total, tallied, stops at 0 — 51.2's and 52.2's "a vampire's votes stop at 0" is that total (K31). A Fee Stake's "non-Anarch titles are worth -1 vote" is netted the same (as Free States Rant's, worded alike): a priscus's title, worth no votes, takes one gained later. Before, the Fee Stake's minus stopped at the title's worth. Amber (the ruling's net, extended to a title's worth by Free States Rant's wording). (Tests absolute_tyranny_keeps_a_net_negative_against_later_votes, a_fee_stakes_title_penalty_nets_against_votes_gained.)

**90.4** No steal of a card in play targets one's own (K35; rulebook glossary "Steal (a card)": "you cannot steal a card from yourself"); blood steals are untouched (71.13). Every steal of a card in play — equipment, retainer, ally, vampire, location, title (Darius Shirazi, Saankaláxt, Xipe Totec, Far Mastery, Valerius) — targets another Methuselah's, masked; Chameleon's "take control" still may take one's own vampire, its ruling over the glossary (LSJ 20080803-2: "to make it Anarch"; 70.3). Revises 17.4's own-minion steals. 27.2 is no steal (Puppet Master takes control until the end of the turn): kept. Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-10-24). (Tests steals_never_target_ones_own_cards, saankalaxt_steals_an_equipment_but_not_a_second_vehicle.)

**90.5** Merging (rulebook "Advanced"): the counters and cards on the vampire already in play stay; those on the card coming from the uncontrolled region are burned (its transferred blood) (K43, completing 69.5). Green. (Test appolonius_merges_in_the_influence_phase.)

**90.6** The burn option's "a Methuselah who does not control a minion who meets the requirements of this card or is not a legal target" (rulebook, advanced) reads the requirements only: "not a legal target" is a minion in torpor where the requirement is a ready one (owner: "not a legal target if it's on torpor and requirement is ready, then you can burn"; 33.8), never the card's own targets — Chanjelin Ward with one on each of its player's vampires stays in hand; one discard each unlock phase; a unique card's copy in play no bar (LSJ 20100203-2) (K37). Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-24): the legal-target opening proposed, no. (Tests chanjelin_ward_is_kept_with_a_ward_on_each_vampire, a_burn_option_card_is_discarded_without_a_minion_to_play_it.)

**90.7** Stealing the Edge after its holder burns it mid-action (K44): the steal still gets it (RTR 19980623: Tereza Rostas "cannot be thwarted by burning the Edge"), over TOM 19951214-2 and the owner's earlier lean (2026-07-22). No pool card steals the Edge (Tereza Rostas is not in it): recorded for when one enters. Red, answered by the owner (2026-10-24).

**90.8** The prisci block's 3 votes are no title's votes: a reduction of titles' or vampires' votes leaves them (ANK 20180307-1: "the votes provided by the Priscii sub-referendum are not affected"), the block's 3 going to the side with more ballots (52.9); Free States Rant's "priscus title is worth 1 less ballot" lessens the ballots, which decide the block, not its 3 votes (K30: the ledger rows' `n/a` stale since the prisci entered the pool). Green. (Tests a_fee_stakes_burn_leaves_the_prisci_block_its_3_votes, free_states_rant_lessens_prisci_ballots_not_the_block.)

**90.9** Game end follows the tournament rules (6.9, 45.5); a withdrawal scores half a victory point (tournament rules), not the rulebook's 1 (K41); the withdrawal itself is Batch 95's. Green.

**90.10** Anathema is a political action, a minion card: on a vampire it is controlled by the vampire's controller and stays when its player is ousted (7.5's minion-card rule; PIB 20121028; advanced rules §1.12.2, whose example it is); a master card on another's minion stays its player's (7.5, 31.5; advanced rules §6.6.3, §6.4.3). No exception, no contradiction (K14). Amber (7.5). (Test anathema_pays_once_and_belongs_to_its_hosts_controller.)

## Batch 91 — Two-player games under VEKN's variant ("Two-player variant for Vampire: The Eternal Struggle", vekn.net, §2.3): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-23)

**91.1** A game dealt with 2 seats plays the variant (owner, 2026-10-23: "the engine should use 2 players rules when instantiating a 2 players game"; `Format::TwoPlayer`, set as it is dealt); a table of 4 or 5 brought down to 2 by ousts keeps the rulebook's; a test fixture (`Engine::bare`) keeps the rulebook's at any seat count. Green. (Test two_player_transfers_are_3_then_4.)

**91.2** A contested crypt card "can still be used as normal" and is "no longer out of play": it acts, blocks, votes, plays cards and uses its abilities; its own text, title, bonuses and the cards on it apply, nothing suspended; only its upkeep is due (`Minion::contested_in_play`). Green. (Test a_contested_crypt_card_stays_in_play_its_upkeep_pool.)

**91.3** Its upkeep is 1 pool in each of its controller's unlock phases, ordered freely among the other unlock dues (7.8), payable down to 0 pool and ousting as a card contest's; yielded, it is burned with the cards and counters on it, as a rulebook yield; once every rival has yielded, the contest ends in its controller's next unlock phase. Green. (Test a_contested_crypt_card_stays_in_play_its_upkeep_pool.)

**91.4** Only unique crypt cards contest in play: the variant's rule names them. A unique library card (an ally, an equipment, a location, a title card) contests by the rulebook in a two-player game, face down and out of play, 1 pool. Amber (the variant is silent on library cards; read narrowly, as its text says "unique crypt card").

**91.5** An imbued, a crypt card, contests in play as a vampire does. Amber (the variant does not mention the Imbued; read from "crypt card").

**91.6** "If two contested vampires (two copies of the same vampire) have a title, their title is not contested": both copies claim it, neither pays blood for it; a title shared with a different vampire is contested by the rulebook (blood), both copies among its claimants. Amber (the second half is the rulebook's, the variant silent). (Test two_contested_copies_keep_their_title.)

**91.7** Transfers: 3 in the first player's first influence phase, 4 in every other influence phase, the second player's first included ("everyone else gets the standard 4"). Green. (Test two_player_transfers_are_3_then_4.)

**91.8** A 2-seat game's decks: a library of 40 to 60 cards, every card on VEKN's "List of allowed cards in 2-player VTES" (`data/two-player.json`, from `scripts/two_player.py`); the crypt as the rulebook's, unchecked by the host (as at any seat count: 4 crypt to deal). The list names V5 cards: a vampire the pool holds in several printings is allowed in its V5 one only (Lucinde, Alastor; Nikolaus Vermeulen; the newest group, not a legacy addition), not its older printings. VEKN's own Tzimisce decklist spells Neserian "Nesserian" (an alias in `scripts/two_player.py`): it is among the suggested lists. Amber (the list is silent on older printings of a listed name; read narrowly, as a list of V5 cards). (Tests two_seats_take_the_variant_s_decks_only, the_two_player_lists_are_legal.)

## Batch 92 — A block attempt under way failed by a bar on blockers (the owner's playtest, sc-bot game d253f9c8dcf5bcb5, decision 459): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-24; owner, 2026-10-24: 92.1)

**92.1** A block attempt under way fails when the action becomes one its minion cannot block: every bar on blockers (vampires, allies, titled or small minions cannot block; the action unblockable; a card's "cannot block" on the minion) is read live against the attempt as each effect resolves in the action, as when attempts are offered, the attempt failing as a barred one (the minion cannot attempt again this action). Daring the Dawn [FOR] played into a vampire's attempt fails it, the action going on unblocked, its damage taken after resolution; an ally's attempt stands. Day Operation [FOR] ("usable if a minion is attempting to block") reads alike. Owner (2026-10-24, playtest: "The block happened it should not have"); generalized to every bar (conditions read live). (Test daring_the_dawn_fails_a_vampires_attempt_under_way_not_an_allys.)

**92.2** Faceless Night [OBF]'s "minions who attempt to block this action and fail become locked before action resolution" takes an attempt failed by such a bar (the minion attempted and failed; LSJ 20081202: one attempting before it is played cannot back out). Amber (LSJ 20081202, ANK 20171017). (Test faceless_night_locks_a_vampire_whose_attempt_daring_the_dawn_fails.)

**92.3** The Kiss of Ra ("only usable when a vampire … attempts to block") never follows Daring the Dawn: played before, it bars the attempt; played into one, it fails it (92.1). Daring the Dawn's ledger row on the action canceled before resolution (ANK 20220401) stays `n/a` for The Kiss of Ra, reworded. Green.

## Batch 93 — The ledger's questions with no reading yet (rules-digest §24.2): the Fury's block against later plays, The Erciyes Fragments, Nergal at resolution, I am Legion's cancels, a banished Great Beast: readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-24)

**93.1** Unleash Hell's Fury and Elder Impersonation [OBF]: the Fury's attempt fails, no damage, the card stays in play, its token out of play again; the Fury is the same minion for every attempt, so it never attempts this action again (LSJ 20100210). Green. (Test elder_impersonation_fails_the_furys_attempt_for_good.)

**93.2** Unleash Hell's Fury and Faceless Night [OBF]: the Fury's failed attempt locks nothing — the card is never locked (60.21) —, and a locked Fury card still attempts (LSJ 20100210). Green. (Test faceless_night_does_not_lock_the_fury.)

**93.3** The Fury attempting is a 9-capacity infernal vampire with 0 blood: its intercept takes other cards as any blocker's, at the short attempt's window (KRCG News Radio, ANK 20211112-2; 82.1); it pays no blood cost (Elder Intervention, LSJ 19970707); burned, it is no minion (Tension in the Ranks, ANK 20230317). Green. (Tests krcg_news_radio_raises_the_furys_intercept, the_fury_cannot_pay_for_elder_intervention, the_fury_burning_is_no_minion_for_tension_in_the_ranks.)

**93.4** Unleash Hell's Fury and Mirror Walk [THA]: Mirror Walk's end of the action before block resolution and the Fury's damage and burn trigger at the same moment: the action ends unbled, the acting minion still takes the damage and the Fury burns (LSJ 20100301, ANK 20210627, ANK 20211207, ANK 20220116). Green. (Test mirror_walk_ends_the_action_the_fury_still_burns.)

**93.5** Unleash Hell's Fury's successful block: no effect before its damage and burn (ANK 20210627, ANK 20211207, ANK 20220116); after it, what 60.21 says (Change of Target); Crypt's Sons' "continue the action as if unblocked" is not offered to an Anarch the damage sent to torpor (ANK 20211207: "If they are in torpor, they cannot continue the action"). Green; its ground that the action ends at once superseded with 60.21 (owner, 2026-11-11). (Tests crypts_sons_cannot_answer_the_furys_block, change_of_target_follows_the_furys_block.)

**93.6** Unleash Hell's Fury's burn option reads ready infernal vampires (33.8, 78.31): with its only infernal vampire in torpor it may be discarded (LSJ 20091203); with a ready one it may not, a Fury already in play notwithstanding (LSJ 20100203-2). Green. (Test the_furys_burn_option_reads_ready_infernal_vampires.)

**93.7** A banished Great Beast stays a vampire in the uncontrolled region (RTR 19990712), its blood as counters and its card with it (25.1): influenced out, it returns as it left. A Great Beast that would go to the crypt burns instead (LSJ 20041201-2): Kaymakli Nightmares sends the banished one's card to its owner's ash heap, its counters to the pool, none drawn for it. The ledger's "not modeled" was stale: the banish and Kaymakli paths carried created vampires already. Green. (Tests a_banished_great_beast_is_influenced_back, kaymakli_nightmares_burns_a_banished_great_beast.)

**93.8** I am Legion's cancel: in the as-played window, the reacting Methuselah has not replaced the card and nothing but the cancel (or a wake) is used — The Erciyes Fragments are not offered (LSJ 20061207, RTR 20040501; 60.19) —; the canceled card is replaced after; a canceled "do not replace until" card (Read the Winds) is replaced at once (LSJ 20080630); a card played from the Fragments can be canceled, and burned it is removed from the game to its owner's removed cards (LSJ 20060106; 60.19). Its limited-effect row is `n/a`: no reaction requiring Auspex in the pool is limited. Green. (Tests i_am_legions_cancel_comes_before_the_replacement_and_the_fragments, i_am_legion_cancels_read_the_winds_replacement_clause, i_am_legion_cancels_a_card_played_from_the_fragments.)

**93.9** Nergal's reduction (60.7) is taken off a bleed or political action card's cost at the action's resolution, as off any action card's (a gap found on the way: those two paths, and a political cost paid early, ignored it, so a card payable only with it fizzled): Propaganda at 1 blood, redirected by Deflection, is paid with it (LSJ 20091021-2); a card left unpayable even with it does not spend it, nor does a card canceled unpaid (Direct Intervention). Green. (Tests nergal_pays_a_deflected_propaganda_with_his_reduction, nergal_pays_reckless_agitation_with_his_reduction, nergals_reduction_is_unspent_when_his_action_is_left_unpayable, nergals_reduction_is_unspent_by_a_card_canceled_unpaid.)

**93.10** The card on The Erciyes Fragments is no card in hand: never burned for a vote nor discarded for another effect (LSJ 20050118); a named card the Fragments took leaves Sudario Refraction resolving for the others, 3 still discarded (LSJ 20090722); the Fragments lock at any impulse of an action (ANK 20200616; 60.19). Green. (Tests the_fragments_card_is_no_political_card_to_burn_for_a_vote, sudario_refraction_resolves_without_the_card_the_fragments_took, the_erciyes_fragments_lock_during_an_action, delaying_tactics_returns_the_fragments_card_to_its_owner.)

## Batch 94 — The ledger's untested rows to tests (cards board): readings the tests found wrong (Claude, 2026-10-24)

**94.1** Rötschreck goes on any Methuselah's vampire facing an opposing minion's aggravated strike, played by any Methuselah but the active one (out-of-turn): "put this card on a vampire" (RTR 19980623: the vampire that would receive the damage); LSJ 20080821 has it on the acting vampire of a Yawp Court combat, so by the Yawp Court's player. The engine had held it to one's own vampire. Green. (Tests yawp_court_against_rotschreck_damages_then_ends_the_action, rotschreck_answers_a_weapon_strike_blood_fury_would_nullify.)

**94.2** Slake the Thirst answers a gain made at the end of a round (Taste of Vitae) in that end-of-round window, once for each other Blood Brother (LSJ 20100206-2; 68.2: the window of the next real decision). Green. (Test slake_the_thirst_twice_on_a_taste_of_vitae.)

**94.3** A banished vampire's leave-ready effects come before its cards go out of play with it: Righteous Aura burns, it does not come back on it (ANK 20180511-1; 25.1 for the cards that stay). Green. (Test righteous_aura_burns_as_its_vampire_is_banished.)

**94.4** A minion that cannot have equipment still takes a locquipment, a location once in play: The Beast, Leatherface of Detroit equips Palatial Estate through Vast Wealth; playing it from hand stays barred (an action card for him) (LSJ 20050315, LSJ 20050413-1). Green. (Test the_beast_equips_a_location_through_vast_wealth.)

**94.5** A minion an action card's effect burns as the directed action resolves is burned by that action (ANK 20220507): Predator's Transformation [ABO] after Smash and Grab [ani] burns the prey's ally, the Trophies alike (a gap found on the way: the card action's burns went unrecorded). Green. (Test smash_and_grab_burning_an_ally_answers_predators_transformation.)

The ledger's `n/a` ("no pool effect brings a vampire out during an action") was wrong: the Soul Gem does (egress finding). Green. (Test a_jimmy_dunn_burned_for_a_second_is_not_burned_by_the_action.)

## Batch 95 — Tournament withdrawal (rulebook "Withdrawing from the Game"; 90.9: half a victory point): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-24)

**95.1** A minion leaving play with blood on it (burned, removed from the game, out of play) does not lose blood for a withdrawal: its counters go to the bank with it; only blood lost or spent while it stays breaks the withdrawal (rulebook: "None of your minions lose (or spend) any blood"). A diablerist takes the victim's blood first: a loss (95.2). Amber. (`Minion::clear_blood`.)

**95.2** An ally's or an imbued's life is not blood (advanced rules 5.5.1): losing it does not break a withdrawal; a vampire's blood does, whatever took it (damage, a cost, a steal, a diablerist's taking it, blood moved off, a disease, blood above capacity). Amber. (Tests an_allys_life_lost_keeps_a_withdrawal, a_withdrawal_fails_on_blood_spent.)

**95.3** "If you have exhausted your library and begin your turn with less than a full hand": read as the turn begins, the library empty and the cards counted against the hand size (the hand and the cards not replaced yet, as for drawing up) under it; the announcement any time in that unlock phase, once. Amber. (Tests a_withdrawal_kept_a_round_scores_half_a_victory_point, no_withdrawal_with_a_full_hand, no_withdrawal_with_a_library_left.)

**95.4** The conditions are checked "when you would start your unlock phase": as the Methuselah's turn begins, before anything unlocks; met, the Methuselah leaves the game then and that turn is not played, the next Methuselah's begins. Any pool lost or spent (a gain after does not repair it: ANK 20180813), any blood a vampire of theirs loses or spends, or a minion of theirs entering combat before then breaks it; broken, it may be announced again in that unlock phase. Green. (Tests a_withdrawal_fails_on_a_pool_loss, a_withdrawal_fails_on_blood_spent, a_withdrawal_fails_on_combat, a_withdrawal_breaks_as_its_minion_enters_combat.)

**95.5** A withdrawal is no oust: the predator gains neither victory point nor pool, the prey-ousted effects (Khabar: Glory, War of Ages) and "until a Methuselah is ousted" (Absimiliard's Army) do not follow; the Methuselah's cards leave the game as an ousted one's. Its half victory point is gained as any victory point is: a Life Boon remembering the Methuselah takes it (6.17 of docs/engine.md). The last Methuselah standing scores as after an oust. Amber. (Tests a_withdrawal_kept_a_round_scores_half_a_victory_point, a_life_boon_takes_a_withdrawals_half_point.)

## Batch 96 — The ledger's blocked gaps (cards board): each its mechanism, built (Claude, 2026-10-25)

**96.1** Dual Form: several of a set leaving the ready region at once (two empty vampires under Pulled Fangs sent to torpor together, or a pair damaged by Edged Illusion [CHI]: corrected round 30, Edged Illusion is in the pool, 121.35), their controller keeps one of them, the others burned (LSJ 20050805; `Interrupt::DualFormKept`, purpose `dual_form_kept`, protocol 2.16.0); the members not leaving burn at once as before (LSJ 20050117). The choice comes as the torpor lands: those not kept burn from torpor, not as they leave. Amber (the timing follows the interrupts' own, as Fear of Mekhet's move). (Tests dual_form_keeps_the_one_chosen_of_a_set_going_down_together, edged_illusion_superior_sends_a_dual_form_pair_down_together.)

**96.2** A hand card's owner (85.27's gap): a card taken in hand from a stolen Storage Annex is recorded by its copy as its owner's (`GameState::foreign`); leaving the hand to another's ash heap or library, it goes to its owner's instead, into play it is its owner's card (LSJ 20001031, PIB 20121220). Owner (2026-10-25): a table of foreign cards keyed by copy, over an owner on every pile. (Tests a_card_taken_from_a_stolen_storage_annex_goes_back_to_its_owner, a_card_played_from_a_stolen_storage_annex_is_still_its_owners.)

**96.3** Nergal's reduction declined as its card was announced (60.7): the card left unpayable without it as its action resolves (a blood lost meanwhile), he is offered it there, or lets the card fizzle with the reduction kept (LSJ 20091021-2: "if he lost a blood during the course of an action that he could have paid for when announced, he can choose"); an action card, a bleed or political action card (`Interrupt::ReductionAtResolution`, purpose `reduction_at_resolution`); asked once. Owner (2026-10-25): yes, 60.7 standing. (Test nergal_may_take_his_reduction_at_resolution.)

**96.4** A wake in an action card's as-played window, its action not declared yet (2.4: a wake there resolves before the card's effect): a locked minion's wake carrying no effect on the action to come (On the Qui Vive, Eyes of Argus, Wake with Evening's Freshness — not Forced Awakening, whose burn reads the action) is offered, its woken status held until the action is declared, dropped if it is not; the woken minion then cancels the card (Rewind Time, Touch of Clarity); an unlock-and-attempt reaction is not offered there (LSJ 20021011). A woken minion meets "usable by an unlocked vampire" for a reaction card (Touch of Clarity) — the wake's "they ignore the requirement to be unlocked for playing reaction cards", which had not been read during an action either (a gap found on the way) — not for another card or a card in play's ability (Yawp Court's "ready unlocked Sabbat vampire"). Green. (Tests a_woken_minion_plays_a_reaction_needing_it_unlocked, yawp_court_needs_an_unlocked_sabbat_vampire_not_a_woken_one.) (Test a_wake_in_an_action_cards_as_played_window_lets_it_be_canceled.)

**96.5** Rows the engine already met, now tested: an active Angelo's Unwholesome Bond [SAN] on one uncontrolled circle of one, another Angelo's included (ANK 20211113; 68.7's mode, Angelo as well as New Blood); The Erciyes Fragments' card in every player's view (ANK 20200523: stored from an ash heap, face up, `GameState::stored_seen_by`). Lucian, the Perfect is not in the pool: his .44 Magnum row is `n/a`. Green. (Tests unwholesome_bond_lets_angelo_feed_one_circle_of_one, the_erciyes_fragments_card_is_seen_by_every_player.)

## Batch 97 — Sabbat paths followed in the influence phase (Black Chantry, "New Sabbat rules, terms and errata", 2025-06-12): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-24)

**97.1** "During your influence phase, a Sabbat vampire not following a Path can burn 1 blood to follow a Sabbat Path": any Sabbat vampire the active Methuselah controls in play, ready or in torpor (the text says a vampire, not a ready one), following no path and with a blood to burn. Amber. (Tests a_sabbat_vampire_following_no_path_follows_one_for_a_blood, a_torpid_or_created_sabbat_vampire_follows_a_path.)

**97.2** Its player chooses any of the four paths; the blood burned is lost (a withdrawal breaks, 95.2); any number of vampires follow one in a phase, each once (one following a path is offered none: "only one Path"); no transfer spent. Green. (Test a_sabbat_vampire_following_no_path_follows_one_for_a_blood.)

**97.3** The path a vampire follows, printed or followed since, is told as its trait; its benefits are a Sabbat vampire's only (49.7, "must be Sabbat to receive the benefits"). Green. (Test a_vampires_path_is_a_trait.)

**97.4** A library card as a Sabbat vampire (a created vampire) follows a path as a crypt card does. Amber. (Test a_torpid_or_created_sabbat_vampire_follows_a_path.)

## Batch 98 — Combat polled past the combatants (rulebook "Combat": the impulse goes on to every Methuselah; advanced rules: effects for a minion "not involved in the current combat"): readings graded (Claude, 2026-10-24)

**98.1** Each ordered combat window polls every Methuselah in the game: the acting minion's, the opposing one's, then the others clockwise from the acting one; the others' prevention (Martyr's Resilience) keeps 29.1's order, every Methuselah clockwise from the acting one. An ousted one is polled no more. Amber (the order of the third Methuselahs: the rulebook says the impulse passes clockwise). (Tests a_third_methuselahs_abjure_ends_another_pairs_combat, a_third_methuselah_plays_rotschreck, the_defending_methuselah_is_polled_before_the_others, an_ousted_methuselah_is_polled_no_more.)

**98.2** A Methuselah outside the combat is offered what a card allows one outside it: its cards and abilities whose text reaches a combat it is not in (Rötschreck, 94.1; Abjure, 87.28; Maman Boumba; Martyr's Resilience, 29.1), its own out-of-turn and "any time" effects; never a vampire's printed ability nor a card-in-play ability whose host is out of the combat, unless the ability says so (15.2, its reading for the combatants' Methuselahs extended to every Methuselah). Amber. (Tests a_third_methuselah_plays_rotschreck, a_third_methuselahs_abjure_ends_another_pairs_combat, a_card_on_a_minion_out_of_the_combat_is_not_offered_in_it.)

## Batch 99 — "A vampire can have only one X" (the owner's playtest, Wall of Filth offered twice; the cards swept) (Claude, 2026-10-25)

**99.1** A card put on its vampire as it is played, "a vampire can have only one" of it, is not played by one that has one: Wall of Filth (owner's playtest, 2026-10-25: "card text forbids that play"), as Divine Sign, Heart of the City and the others already were. Green. (Test wall_of_filth_is_not_offered_to_a_vampire_with_one.)

**99.2** A card put on its vampire only after the play ("you can put this card on this vampire" after the action or the combat), one per vampire: still played, its other effects standing; a second is not put on — to the ash heap: Pillars Fall (enforced already by its one-per-vampire placement, `place_now`; now pinned by a test), as Dogged Pursuit [ANI] and Fever Pitch [FOR]/[PRE] (`may_put_held`). Green. (Test pillars_fall_does_not_stay_on_a_vampire_with_one.)

**99.3** The sweep of the pool's "can have only one X" cards: the rest were enforced already — by the play's requirement (Divine Sign, Heart of the City, Kevlar Vest, Taking the Skin: Minion…), by placement (Dogged Pursuit, Fever Pitch, Pillars Fall), by the target (Alastor's referendum, Disarm and Pulled Fangs on the opposing vampire), by label (vehicles, archetypes, havens), per Methuselah (Augury of Doom, Form of Corruption, Raising the Portcullis); Vampiric Disease on a diseased vampire stays a legal no-op (25.5). Green.

## Batch 100 — Reachable edges: contests with oneself, an X past a surcharge (the cards board; owner, 2026-10-25: 100.1, 100.2) (Claude, 2026-10-25)

**100.1** A unique title given to a vampire whose controller claims it already through another vampire, read as a contest with oneself (the incoming title lost when forced, the choice masked): superseded by 3.1 (owner, 2026-11-11: two of one Methuselah's vampires claiming the same unique title contest it; the rulebook's self-contest bar is for cards only). Kept: one's own titled vampire stays Chameleon's target (LSJ 20080803-2: made Anarch, no contest). (Test chameleon_makes_ones_own_vampire_anarch.)

**100.2** No vampire printed with a unique title his Methuselah claims already influenced out: superseded by 3.1 (owner, 2026-11-11: two of one Methuselah's vampires claiming the same unique title contest it; the rulebook's self-contest bar is for cards only).

**100.3** "Burn X blood" beside a fixed cost (Lightning Reflexes [CEL], Martyr's Resilience, Hidden Strength, Awe, Veil the Legions…): X up to the blood left after the card's cost as paid — its surcharges in (Terror Frenzy [ANI], Unleashing the Bestial Soul, Libertas), the reductions announced with it out (D20, 77.8; the cost terms come first). It stopped at the printed cost: under Terror Frenzy one X too many, a strike unpaid. Whether a card is affordable before or with the reductions announced reads the same cost: under Terror Frenzy, Nergal's 1-blood card with 1 blood left takes his reduction (no unreduced pass); the playing minion's printed surcharge in (Tom's for Sanguinus: no Hive Mind offered with 1 blood; his Octopod paid 2, found by the review). Green. (Tests lightning_reflexes_x_stops_at_the_blood_left_after_terror_frenzys_surcharge, a_surcharge_counts_before_nergal_announces_his_reduction, toms_sanguinus_surcharge_bars_and_charges.)

**100.4** One's own claimant out of play for now barring a choice or an influence of its title: superseded by 3.1 (owner, 2026-11-11: two of one Methuselah's vampires claiming the same unique title contest it; the rulebook's self-contest bar is for cards only); it contests the title as it returns (`enter_title_contest`).

## Batch 101 — Reachable edges: mid-play ousts and costs (the cards board; owner, 2026-10-26: 101.1, 101.3) (Claude, 2026-10-26)

**101.1** A card canceled with its cost still paid (Meditative Grove, Groundfighting, Diana Iadanza, I am Legion, Predator's Transformation, Taking the Skin: Minion) takes the reductions announced with it as that cost is paid — Focus the Blood burned, Regina Blake's blood, Baldesar's 1 off, Jack's lock — but not the lock-type locations, never locked by a card canceled as played (The Shard, Sunset Strip; 36.1, ANK 20230620). It paid in full before, the announced Focus kept (`settle_canceled`; `take_announced` shared with `resolve_play`). Regina Blake's blood, the case DESIGN named, is unreachable so far: she pays locations, no card cancels one with its cost paid. Owner (2026-10-26). (Test a_canceled_card_still_paid_takes_its_announced_reduction.)

**101.2** Disarm's torpor ousting the active Methuselah inside the play (Fame's last 3 pool, the blocker's Disarm): ousted at once — the predator's VP, the turn passes — and the Disarm on the ousted vampire is removed from the game with it, the ousted Methuselah's to control (ANK 20180408, as Charnas the Imp; not 67.1's burn of another's card on a minion removed otherwise). Green (verified: the play's bookkeeping after the oust leaves nothing behind). (Test a_disarm_whose_torpor_ousts_the_active_methuselah.)

**101.3** Supersedes 18.2: a pool paid as a cost out of turn may be a last pool — a cost, payable down to 0 (D3) — its payer ousted at once (`pay_pool_for_ability`; Life Boon played right before it, 45.1), offered nothing more, all their cards gone. True Love's Face's and Golconda: Inner Peace's cancels: the card canceled, the bleed with no Methuselah left to bleed; with one Methuselah left, the game is over. Owner (2026-10-26). KRCG News Radio's 1 pool for another's intercept likewise (it was masked by analogy to 18.2): its player ousted with the card before the intercept is given, as a last pool paid to vote casts nothing (33.2). (Tests true_loves_face_cancel_may_cost_a_last_pool, a_last_pool_cancel_ends_a_two_player_game, golconda_cancel_may_cost_a_last_pool, a_last_pool_golconda_cancel_ends_a_two_player_game, krcg_news_radio_may_cost_a_last_pool.)

## Batch 102 — Round 21: Step 1 and Nights of Reckoning, 27 cards (the cards board; owner, 2026-10-26: the round; 2026-10-28: 102.9, 102.11, 102.15) (Claude, 2026-10-26)

**102.1** "[n CONVICTION]" on a played card is a cost: n convictions on the playing imbued, its player choosing which (87.10). A non-action card's is paid with its blood and pool, a canceled card's too (101.1); an action card's conviction is named as it is announced and burned as the action resolves — no longer on the imbued then, the action fizzles (RTR 20111202, as 48.8's declared payment; 3.6). A mode's own bracket (Cleave's "spend 1 conviction") is that mode's cost (`Effect::ConvictionCost`). "[X CONVICTION]" on an equipment (Living Wood Staff): X announced from 0 up to the convictions held (LSJ 20030502), paid as the equip resolves with its blood, and kept on the card (`fixed_x`) through a change of bearer; found or named rather than played (Moise Kasavubu's search, Angel of Berlin: "pay the cost as normal"), its X is paid as it is equipped, a conviction at a time, a pass stopping (`Interrupt::XConvictions`: the egress review's catch). Amber (87.10, 48.8). (Tests augur_spends_a_conviction_to_look_and_unlocks, smite_strikes_aggravated_at_range_and_burns_electronics, imprison_tolls_the_opposing_monsters_strikes, determine_redirects_a_predators_bleed_or_cancels_an_action_card, cleave_as_a_weapon_strikes_past_an_immunity_to_normal_damage, living_wood_staff_spends_x_convictions_for_x_more_damage, living_wood_staff_x_may_be_0_and_be_taken_by_a_non_avenger, living_wood_staff_found_by_moise_asks_its_x, living_wood_staff_equipped_by_angel_of_berlin_asks_its_x.)

**102.2** An imbued's creed reads as its clan for card requirements, a master's too (Vigil: The Thin Line needs a ready Defender, Unity a ready Visionary: the rulebook's master requirement, as clan masters), and its virtues as Disciplines (rulebook "Appendix: Imbued Rules"). A single-mode card printed without its icon (the Nights of Reckoning virtue cards: Smite) requires the card's virtue: `gen_library.py` gives such a mode the card's Disciplines. Equipment taken from another minion needs no creed either (ANK 20180719-3). Green. (Tests a_virtue_card_requires_its_virtue, vigil_gives_a_defender_intercept_against_a_monster, unity_moves_two_imbued_cards_to_the_library_top_then_may_cut.)

**102.3** "Requires a ready imbued" on a master or an event (The Church of Vindicated Faith, Angel of Berlin, Blood Cult Awareness Network) is met by a ready imbued its player controls; on an ally (Moise Kasavubu) by its recruiter, a ready imbued (rulebook "Requirements": a minion card's by the minion playing it). Green (the second reader's correction for Moise). (Tests moise_kasavubu_is_recruited_by_an_imbued, the_church_feeds_an_uncontrolled_imbued_after_an_imbued_succeeds.)

**102.4** Bartholomew's "if unlocked at the beginning of your turn" is read as the turn begins, before the unlock phase unlocks him; in torpor too. Kassiym Malikhair's blood goes to any Methuselah's ready Pander (D11). Marconius's discount is on cards played with Mytherceria [myt] (D29): Faerie Wards' [myt] and [MYT] cost him 1. Julia Prima bleeds another Methuselah (her own is no bleed), the ability setting the bleed's target (a granted seat-targeted bleed had none: fixed); her +1 once each action, before resolution (ANK 20231124). Amber (both reads agree; Julia green). (Tests bartholomew_gains_a_pool_if_unlocked_as_the_turn_begins, kassiym_malikhair_moves_a_blood_to_any_ready_pander, marconius_pays_one_less_for_mytherceria_and_strikes_harder, julia_prima_bleeds_any_methuselah_and_burns_blood_for_bleed.)

**102.5** Faerie Wards: "an action directed at you (or a card you control)" is an action directed at its player; "younger" than the reacting vampire. [MYT]'s "does not unlock as normal" leaves an infernal vampire its paid unlock (LSJ 20050114); not while a block is attempted or made (ANK 20260316, LSJ 20041022); the failed action reached resolution and was not blocked. Green. (Tests faerie_wards_intercepts_or_fails_a_younger_vampires_action, faerie_wards_superior_leaves_an_infernal_vampire_its_paid_unlock, faerie_wards_intercept_and_none_after_a_block.)

**102.6** Antithesis: "cannot hunt" bars every hunt, a card's too (as Week of Nightmares); made to hunt, the vampire locks, then goes to torpor; burned in its vampire's controller's discard phase. Amber. (Test antithesis_sends_a_vampire_that_must_hunt_to_torpor_locked.)

**102.7** The Church of Vindicated Faith: any Methuselah's imbued succeeding (D11); the blood to an imbued in its controller's uncontrolled region, after resolution. Green. (Test the_church_feeds_an_uncontrolled_imbued_after_an_imbued_succeeds.)

**102.8** Break the Code: "monster" is the rulebook's — a vampire or an ally not mortal (`is_monster`); every imbued gets −1 intercept attempting to block a monster; the granted action is a rush, at another Methuselah's imbued only (17.3); Karen Suadela, a prince, takes no action an event provides (LSJ 20100121). Green. (Tests break_the_code_weakens_imbued_blocks_and_lets_monsters_attack_them, karen_suadela_takes_no_break_the_code_action.)

**102.9** Punish: still its player's on another's vampire; that vampire cannot gain blood, every gain going to the bank (hunts, moves, steals: a hunt still a hunt); burned in its player's next unlock phase; blood moved or stolen to it goes to the bank (116.135). [COMBAT] is a strength hand strike putting the card on the opposing vampire as it lands (as Sculpt the Flesh [PRO]); masked against an ally (no opposing vampire to put it on). Owner (2026-10-28). (Test punish_bars_a_vampire_from_gaining_blood_until_its_players_unlock.)

**102.10** Bond: the incapacitated imbued leaves the region with one life in all, not one more (LSJ 20091118); another Methuselah's imbued: directed at them (20.1). Green. (Test bond_moves_an_incapacitated_imbued_back_with_a_life.)

**102.11** Augur: the imbued unlocks only if the action succeeds (its effect); looking at the predator's and the prey's hands targets them: the action is directed at both (§3.4, as Vaticination's every hand), cards like Deflection usable against it. Owner (2026-10-28: the second reader's reading; it was read undirected). (Test augur_spends_a_conviction_to_look_and_unlocks.)

**102.12** Muse of Flame: the monster is chosen at random as the action succeeds, among all its prey's monsters, torpid ones too (D28; the second reader's correction: "ready" was read in). Amber. (Test muse_of_flame_sends_the_imbued_into_combat_with_a_prey_monster.)

**102.13** Smite: strength+1 aggravated ranged damage; the electronic equipment (`Label::Electronic`) on both combatants burns as the strike resolves, dodged or not, none if it never resolves. Amber. (Test smite_strikes_aggravated_at_range_and_burns_electronics.)

**102.14** Imprison: for the rest of the combat, no maneuver and no press to continue (a press to end stays), and each strike but "combat ends" costs the opposing monster 1 more blood or life — a cost increase: reductions apply (LSJ 20070829-1), strikes granted later pay it (LSJ 20090529), a strike already chosen may be declined (LSJ 20071017). **102.15** Inflict: the amount of damage the imbued takes from hand strikes, melee weapon strikes and retainers, before prevention, is inflicted on the striker too as normal damage, whatever the strike's aggravation, preventable by the striker. **102.16** Determine: [first mode] the bleed redirected to the predator's predator after blocks are declined (in two players, oneself: a legal no-op, D14); [second mode] the action card canceled as played, no cost paid, the monster not locked, and barred from that card for the rest of the turn; a cancel's generic rulings hold (the as-played window, a canceled card still played). **102.18** Vigil: The Thin Line: +1 intercept to any Methuselah's Defender attempting to block an acting monster (D11; the second reader's correction: its controller's was read in), while intercept is needed. Amber. (Test vigil_gives_a_defender_intercept_against_a_monster.)

**102.19** The Crusader Sword: equipped by any minion (LSJ 20030607), its strike usable by an imbued only (no True Faith in the pool); while an imbued bears it, the vampires opposing it cannot maneuver to close range, read live. Green. (Tests the_crusader_sword_bars_opposing_vampires_from_closing, the_crusader_sword_strikes_aggravated_for_an_imbued_only.)

**102.20** Cleave: untimed (2.2). The melee weapon chosen, or the card itself put on as a strength weapon (an equipment, LSJ 20060608), inflicts +1 damage each strike this action, part of its strike's damage (TOM 19960225, LSJ 20060412), inflicted past an immunity to normal damage; burned as the action ends, Living Wood Staff spared, a weapon out of play then staying. **102.21** Expiate: the 5 convictions a requirement, not a cost. Two rounds in a row at close range (any two), the imbued ready and still holding 5, an opposing monster: as the second round's press step begins, 5 convictions burned, the imbued and the monster burned; none if the combat ended first (LSJ 20010312). Otherwise — a mortal opponent included (the second reader's correction) — 1 conviction a copy burned as the combat ends. Amber. (Test expiate_burns_both_after_two_close_rounds_or_a_conviction.)

**102.22** Lock: controlled by its minion's Methuselah (D5): other Methuselahs' actions targeting the minion cost a monster 1 more blood or life, paid as they resolve (unpayable, they fizzle), any one target enough (LSJ 20080809-2); the minion, a monster, burns a blood or life as it attempts an action or a block, what it has if short, as Archon's (LSJ 19970707); +1 stealth undirected; its minion may burn it as an action.
