**107.14** Prison of the Mind: each burn action directed at the card's controller, by any vampires, three in all. Follow the Blood's maneuver or press is named as played (amber). Tests: `prison_of_the_mind_goes_on_the_vampire`, `follow_the_blood_enters_combat_with_the_hunter`.

**107.15** The Framing goes on the anarch when its action succeeds, so it answers a later block; no combat if the blocker and the chosen minion cannot fight (same Methuselah, not ready). Tests: `the_framing_sends_the_blocker_at_the_chosen_minion`.

**107.16** Haven Hunting is asked of each side's controller in turn, not with a combat queued (RTR 20020501). Tests: `haven_hunting_starts_another_combat`, `haven_hunting_loses_the_after_combat_and_waits_for_a_queue`.

**107.17** Power of All and Watchtower: The Wolves Feed cancel with the cost paid (ANK 20260216); Power of All's second anarch is chosen as it cancels. Tests: `power_of_all_cancels_with_two_anarchs`, `power_of_all_outside_actions_and_not_in_combat`, `watchtower_strikes_and_cancels`.

**107.18** Praetorian Backer and Black Hand Emissary need a Seraph actor. The Emissary's move is directed at the card's controller. Backer burns as soon as no ready Seraph remains. Tests: `seraph_cards_follow_their_seraph`, `black_hand_emissary_gives_votes_and_moves`.

**107.19** Keystone Kine [cel] gains on the action's success, a bleed of 0 too; [obf] reads the prey, even after a redirect. Tests: `keystone_kine_bleeds_gains_and_burns_an_ally`, `keystone_kine_picks_the_ally_at_resolution`.

**107.20** Detect Authority [ani]: a minion or location; it fails, not blocked (LSJ 20070219). Final Loosening [aus] (owner, 2026-11-02): any blood gain of the acting vampire: its next gain this action, of any kind, is 1, the reacting Anarch gaining 1 too (`final_loosening_splits_any_gain`). Amber: played at reaction timing, before the gain, a card with no gain coming is wasted. Tests: `detect_authority_makes_the_acting_vampire_pay`, `detect_authority_not_at_a_retainer_nor_with_a_block_under_way`, `final_loosening_stops_an_acting_ally`, `final_loosening_splits_a_set_gain_too`, `final_loosening_splits_a_turnips_blood`.

**107.21** Their Master's Voice by a locked or unlocked vampire; no gargoyle creature ally in the pool. Tests: `their_masters_voice_unlocks_a_slave_gargoyle`.

**107.22** The World's a Canvas [dom]: the crypt card is picked at resolution (amber, against choices at declaration); half rounded down. Tests: `the_worlds_a_canvas_burns_uncontrolled_blood`.

**107.23** Enrage [PRE]'s 2 blood is an action toll (as Daria's, 42.1): masked if unpayable with the action's own cost (ANK 20180327), paid at resolution, so a blocked or canceled action pays nothing (LSJ 20090208); copies add; hunts and combat entries free. Played on the actor, it waits for its next action. Tests: `enrage_tolls_actions_but_hunts`, `enrage_superior_needs_the_blood_and_spares_a_canceled_action`, `enrage_leaves_the_red_list_hunt_free`.

**107.24** Drink the Blood of Ahriman: the unlock choice is the controller's (ANK 20220503); only a ranged aggravated strike burns it, even prevented, not dodged (amber). Tests: `drink_the_blood_of_ahriman_is_fed_at_unlock`.

**107.25** Joseph Pander's granted combat lives on the card: burned, it is lost (amber). Grey Thorne: cel or pot, not both (ANK 20180731-1); healed by any Methuselah's ready anarch, that anarch's controller choosing, each asked from the active Methuselah clockwise (owner, 2026-11-02; `grey_thorne_is_healed_by_any_methuselahs_anarch`). Tests: `joseph_pander_guards_and_sends_his_panders`, `joseph_panders_two_actions_do_not_restrict_each_other`, `grey_thorne_plays_one_discipline_at_a_time`, `grey_thorne_asks_from_the_active_methuselah`.

**107.26** Epiphany takes the advanced version from crypt, uncontrolled region, then ash heap (amber: no choice), bringing a slot's blood. Tests: `epiphany_merges_with_the_advanced_version`.

**107.27** No Confidence: two lose the title's benefit, a contested one yielded (LSJ 20080602); each burns as the acting vampire it remembers leaves the ready region. Tests: `no_confidence_strips_a_title_at_two`, `no_confidence_through_play_and_title_yielded`.

**107.29** Blood Turnip (owner, 2026-11-02): an anarch moves it onto any minion, that anarch's controller choosing, not the anarch itself (`blood_turnip_goes_on_any_minion`); a last life moved burns the ally. Tests: `blood_turnip_moves_once_between_non_anarchs`, `blood_turnip_converts_and_an_anarch_redirects`.

**107.30** Liquefy the Mortal Coil [VIC] (owner, 2026-11-02): next round's initial strike can be the diablerie, a real choice beside any other (`liquefy_superior_strike_is_a_choice`), a stand-in on the vampire offering it until the combat ends; ~~it ends the combat, no would-end play nor offer, what the combat's end leaves standing, the after-combat window skipped for the diablerie's~~ superseded (owner, 2026-11-11: a strike, the diablerie and its blood hunt inside the combat, 113.10), a blocked action still blocked; [vic] lands or does nothing. Tests: `liquefy_burns_a_younger_vampire`, `liquefy_diablerie_and_blood_hunt_inside_the_combat`, `elixir_of_distillation_lowers_capacity_until_a_lock`, `elixir_of_distillation_stays_if_the_combat_ends_first`, `a_cut_short_combat_burns_its_stand_in`.

**107.31** Achilles' Heel's circle is chosen as it enters. Cadet keeps to its own slot: two copies of one vampire uncontrolled are two slots, each may have one (`CardInPlay::on_slot`, an index). Blade Clot reads the victim's capacity as diablerized, one move per copy; a rescue by an older vampire burns them all (PIB 20150122). Tests: `achilles_heel_opens_a_vampire_to_a_circle`, `blade_clot_sends_the_struck_to_torpor`, `blade_clot_adds_up_and_is_rescued_or_burned`, `cadet_keeps_to_its_own_copy`, `cadet_follows_its_slot_merged`, `cadet_burns_with_its_slot_reshuffled`.

**107.32** Trophies (reversing 67.5's scoping): put in play by a master phase action, awarded for a Red List minion burned in combat, by a Ⓓ action or diablerie, from hand, library, ash heap or play, the player picking the zone, only a library searched reshuffled, a hand taken from drawn back up at once (before the blood hunt). Trophy: Chosen (owner, 2026-11-02): a vampire Black Hand as it took the card is a Seraph only while the card is on it (`trophy_chosen_makes_a_seraph_only_while_on_it`). Eccentric Billionaire locks as the card is announced, the same effect as at the payment (amber). Seraph's Second needs a ready Seraph, so not from torpor. Tests: `trophy_chosen_rewards_a_red_list_kill`, `trophy_chosen_burned_instead_does_nothing`, `a_trophy_comes_from_the_zone_picked`, `a_trophy_from_hand_draws_back_up_at_once`, `eccentric_billionaire_locks_as_announced`, `seraphs_second_after_a_block_and_for_a_new_black_hand`.

Tests: `touch_of_pain_damages_the_acting_minion_once_an_action`, `census_taker_gives_a_black_hand_blocker_intercept`, `reverend_adams_advanced_looks_by_the_gehenna_cards`.

**107.34** Blessings of the Loa (owner, 2026-11-02): "for each vampire in torpor they control" reads the prey (`blessings_of_the_loa_counts_your_preys_torpor`).

## Batch 108 — The events' deep sweep: a reading graded (the engine board) (Claude, 2026-10-30)

**108.1** A contested vampire, out of play, keeps its blood above its capacity (by analogy with advanced rules 5.1.1, an uncontrolled vampire's cards out of play, "blood held for the raised capacity does not drain off in the meantime", and 78.54's out-of-play vampire): a card that raised its capacity is out of play with it (`drain_all_excess` reads `present`), and the excess drains as it returns if nothing raises it then. A two-player contested vampire, in play, drains as any other. Amber, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-02). (Tests `a_contested_vampire_keeps_blood_above_its_capacity`, `a_two_player_contested_vampire_drains_its_excess`.)

## Batch 109 — Round 25: Ebony Kingdom and Blood Shadowed Court completed, 137 cards (the cards board; owner, 2026-11-03: "Ebony Kingdom + BSC"; red: 109.2, 109.3, 109.6, 109.40, 109.44) (Claude, 2026-11-04; the reds answered by the owner, 2026-11-06: 109.2 and 109.6 corrected, 109.3, 109.40, 109.44 confirmed)

**109.1** Orun: +2 capacity per Orun for non-Orun cards other Methuselahs play, read live, by cards already in play too (LSJ 20051110: Aranthebes, Veneficti) and over Wormwood's ceiling (LSJ 20051205); one vote for every three Orun altogether (LSJ 20051204); nothing applies off a Laibon (ANK 20221208). Tests: `orun_raises_capacity_for_others_and_votes_by_three`, `orun_capacity_reaches_cards_already_in_play`, `orun_and_aye_over_capacity_the_controller_picks_which_burns`.

**109.2** Orun and Aye over capacity (owner, 2026-11-06): every one triggers; the Laibon's controller picks which burns, it resolves first, and the rest stay once the count is back within capacity (copies alike: one choice a kind). Pallid and Mundane burn apart from that choice. Tests: `orun_and_aye_over_capacity_the_controller_picks_which_burns`, `pallid_burns_apart_from_the_orun_and_aye_choice`.

**109.3** Orun's capacity counts for another Methuselah's vampire ability too (owner, 2026-11-06; vampires are played cards, LSJ 20090205, https://usenet.krcg.org/t/kh8fU-OqPrk/): the reader is the Methuselah who played the card or the vampire, compared with whoever controls the Laibon now (LSJ 20090929, 20090930, https://usenet.krcg.org/t/-Bp4t8XyKPo/); cards in play keep knowing who played them, Orun played before or after, +2 per Orun however many cards (LSJ 20090626, 20090701, https://usenet.krcg.org/t/XPWNnrTUUdg/); a stolen vampire's ability reads as its player's, not its thief's, as it is offered and as its action resolves (LSJ 20090930), its "you control" still its controller's (Abderrahim); Wormwood's cap applies first, Orun adds after (LSJ 20051205, https://usenet.krcg.org/t/K-RE-nTJ_Cg/). Printed passive bonuses read the plain capacity. Tests: `orun_counts_for_a_vampires_ability_requirement`, `a_stolen_vampires_ability_reads_orun_as_its_player`, `a_stolen_abderrahim_reads_orun_as_his_player`.

**109.4** Orun burns one, however many, after a successful bleed for more than 2 (LSJ 20090603), the amount as resolved; after a successful action directed at a non-mortal minion, after its combat (ANK 20240809). Tests: `orun_burns_one_after_a_bleed_for_more_than_two`, `orun_burns_after_a_rushs_combat`.

**109.5** Aye: a frenzy card targeting, choosing or affecting its Laibon is played on him (LSJ 20051113), canceled still played (ANK 20190104); three Aye lock, this one among them, for "as if unlocked" this action; nothing off a Laibon (ANK 20221208). Tests: `aye_cancels_a_frenzy_card_played_on_its_laibon`, `aye_lets_a_locked_laibon_block_for_three_locked_aye`, `aye_does_nothing_off_a_laibon`.

**109.6** Aye's "if this Laibon burns a minion, equipment or location" (owner, 2026-11-06): any burn his effects cause: in combat, his blocks included, a minion his strikes' damage burns (one per minion, its equipment going with it no second) and an equipment his strike burns, not a card burning by its own text (Waxen Poetica) or a cost paid with the last life; out of combat, the minions his directed action burns (a diablerie included) and an equipment or location his burn-card action burns, one per action; a burn outside his action and outside combat is not counted. Tests: `aye_burns_one_when_its_laibon_burns_a_minion`, `aye_burns_one_when_its_blocking_laibon_burns_an_ally`, `aye_burns_one_when_its_laibons_strike_burns_equipment`, `aye_burns_one_when_its_laibons_first_strike_burns_equipment`, `aye_ignores_an_equipment_burned_by_its_own_effect`.

**109.7** Bamba: one Discipline card in all from the magaji's search (LSJ 20090528-1); its clan the acting Laibon's as it enters (amber); it cannot act this turn, so it is never forced to hunt then (an engine fix: a minion that cannot act is not starving). Tests: `bamba_becomes_a_laibon_and_a_magaji_gives_it_more`.

## Batch 110 — The as-played window draws nothing (the engine board; owner, 2026-11-06) (Claude, 2026-11-06)

**110.1** Nothing but wakes and cancels in an as-played window, no draw either (owner, 2026-11-06: "only wakes and cancels can be played there, not any other card or effect (even drawing to replace or using Dreams to augment hand size)"): a card played and resolved in another card's as-played window (a wake, a cancel) is replaced as the stack's last as-played window closes, before its bottom card resolves, not as it resolves — so a cancel the replacement would draw is not offered in that window. An extra draw it brings (Ira Rivers's for an Auspex card) waits too, offered after the replacements. Its "do not replace until" clause, if any, still wins. (Tests `a_wake_in_an_as_played_window_is_replaced_as_it_closes`, `an_extra_draw_on_a_wake_waits_out_the_as_played_window`, `an_unlock_reaction_waits_out_the_as_played_window`.)

## Batch 112 — Round 27: Secrets of New York and Noites Brasileiras, 63 cards (the cards board; owner, 2026-11-08; red: 112.14, 112.19, 112.21, 112.47) (Claude, 2026-11-09; the owner: 112.14, 112.19, 112.21, 112.47 confirmed)

**112.10** Durok's +1 intercept: an action directed at a minion or other card his controller controls, never a bleed of the Methuselah. Tests: `durok_intercepts_actions_at_his_cards`.

**112.11** Eduardo, o Oráculo Mascarado: once each referendum (D7), ready, a random discard (replaced) for +2 votes. Tests: `eduardo_discards_at_random_for_votes`.

**112.13** Gianni D'Angelo: 1 blood for +1 intercept to another Camarilla vampire of his controller attempting to block, while intercept is needed, for the rest of the action; once each turn. Tests: `gianni_lends_intercept_to_another_camarilla_vampire`.

Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-11-09). Tests: `hellene_is_stunned_after_her_referendum_fails`.

**112.16** Julia Sowinski: a successful bleed (1 or more) gives her controller +1 transfer in that turn's influence phase, after the minion phase; in another Methuselah's turn, nothing (amber). Tests: `julia_gets_a_transfer_after_a_successful_bleed`.

**112.17** Kaiser: ready, once each action, a discarded card printing a Discipline requirement (D29) reduces a bleed against his controller by 1. Tests: `kaiser_discards_a_discipline_card_against_a_bleed`.

**112.18** Kali: ready, once each turn, a random discard (replaced, paid with the cost) takes 1 pool off a location or equipment her Methuselah plays, offered only for a pool cost (D4); one reducer a play, Baldesar Rossellini's (amber). Tests: `kali_discards_at_random_to_cheapen_equipment`.

Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-11-09: targeted, so a card barring targeting protects it). Tests: `katherine_locks_a_younger_vampire_as_she_bleeds`.

Tests: `maria_strikes_again_and_tharmas_maneuvers`, `titled_vampires_enter_combat_with_torque`.

**112.27** Thomas Arturo's lock: a titled vampire of another Methuselah, in torpor too, locked or not (57.10). Tests: `thomas_arturo_locks_a_titled_vampire`.

**112.28** Tubarão: once each referendum, in torpor too, the crypt's top card revealed and moved to the bottom; +1 vote if a vampire of more capacity than his in play (D30). Tests: `tubarao_reveals_his_crypt_top_for_a_vote`.

**112.29** Vritra: a location of a Methuselah controlling more locations than his, read as announced and as it resolves (D1); a location on a minion goes to a minion of his controller's choice (Dominate Kine's; amber). Tests: `vritra_steals_a_location_from_a_richer_methuselah`.

**112.30** Luana do Lago's hand size, Rey's and Tereza Rossi's strength: printed, read live. Tests: `luana_rey_and_tereza_print_their_stats`.

**112.31** Porto de Santos: once each game for its player (D27); from the player clockwise, each Methuselah's top 3 crypt cards (fewer in a smaller crypt), one to the uncontrolled region, the others to the bottom; "up to" read as all three (amber). Tests: `porto_de_santos_moves_a_crypt_card_for_everyone`.

**112.32** Aaron Fletcher: as he enters play, up to three equipment cards of the hand (replaced), then of the ash heap, face down on him; a minion of his controller equips one, named as the action is declared (D1), with Delivery Truck's +1 stealth equip, requirements and cost as normal, copies alike one choice (amber). Tests: `aaron_fletcher_stores_equipment_and_lends_it`.

**112.33** Academia de Campeões: in the discard phase, up to two combat cards printing Potence (D29) from the ash heap to the library's bottom, one at a time. Tests: `academia_moves_potence_combat_cards_to_the_library_bottom`.

**112.34** The Art Hole: played only with a ready primogen; after a successful action of its controller's primogen (a fizzle too), lock to unlock it; titled Camarilla vampires steal it. Tests: `the_art_hole_unlocks_a_successful_primogen`.

**112.35** Backroom Deal: [1] the acting minion a primogen; [2] another ready primogen of the acting Methuselah's, the acting vampire Camarilla; one Backroom Deal each action, by anyone (Veil the Legions'). Tests: `backroom_deal_lends_a_primogens_stealth`.

**112.36** Bar Último Trago: locked as its controller's minion's block is made; neither combatant uses equipment in that combat; each takes 1 environmental damage in its first round's strike resolution ("R" the ranged notation). Tests: `bar_ultimo_trago_hurts_both_combatants`.

**112.37** Cãoera: its steal takes 1 blood or life as a vampire's steal (blood becoming its life); +1 bleed per life above 2, read live; 1 maneuver each combat. Tests: `caoera_bleeds_for_its_life_above_two`.

**112.38** Chave de Braço: "Grapple." a label, no timing (Grasp of the Python prints "Only usable at close range" beside it); [POT] as the round ends, at close range, more damage inflicted (Disarm's comparison) puts the card itself on the opposing minion (D21), -2 strength; a vampire burns 2 blood, an ally 1 life, to burn it (amber). Tests: `chave_de_braco_goes_on_the_outdamaged_minion`.

## Batch 113 — Round 28: Heirs to the Blood, 178 cards (116 library cards, 62 vampires) (the cards board; owner, 2026-11-09: the set-group lines; red: 113.1, 113.10, 113.25, 113.47, 113.57, 113.60, 113.64, 113.68, 113.69, 113.77, 113.81, 113.84, 113.86, 113.87, 113.95, 113.99, 113.112, 113.120, 113.135, 113.141, 113.142, 113.151, 113.155) (Claude, 2026-11-10; the owner, 2026-11-10: 113.57, 113.86 and 113.120 corrected, 113.1, 113.47, 113.64, 113.69, 113.81, 113.84, 113.95, 113.112, 113.141 and 113.151 confirmed, 113.99 open with the owner's lean; the owner, 2026-11-11: 113.10, 113.25, 113.87 and 113.135 corrected, 113.60, 113.68, 113.77, 113.142 and 113.155 confirmed)

**113.1** [mal] (Maleficia) and [str] (Striga) are not Disciplines: nothing that counts, lists, requires or taxes Disciplines sees them (Dabbler's count, Blood Weakens' hold, The Slow Withering's surcharge, Concoction of Vitality, "requires a Discipline", Agent of Power's and Spontaneous Power's choice); the cards requiring them (Barrenness, Evil Eye, Fractura, Greater Curse, Hexe, Masca, Minor Curse, Scobax, Strix) are played through Maleficia's and Striga's level on the vampire, the mode played as for a Discipline (LSJ 20011204-3). Red, confirmed by the owner (2026-11-10). Tests: `maleficia_and_striga_lend_their_power_to_an_infernal`, `barrenness_sterilizes_and_shrinks_a_vampire`, `evil_eye_superior_cancels_an_action_card_and_locks_the_actor`, `hexe_superior_is_unpreventable_aggravated`, `masca_gives_an_optional_strike_each_round_of_the_action`.

**113.2** Crypt slaves (Rusticus, Toy, Cedric, Chaundice, Fustuk, Grotesque; Chalcedony and Sheela Na Gig, which lacked both, fixed): no directed action while their controller controls no ready vampire of the master clan (`slave_clan`: printed or created, none once freed, 109.49); as a vampire of that clan is blocked, the slave may lock to fight the blocker in its place, the master unlocked (62.14; advanced rules §5.7.2: the slave is never the acting minion). Tests: `crypt_slaves_serve_their_master_clan`, `every_crypt_slave_fights_for_its_master`.

**113.3** Text-only vampires (Abebe, Angel Chavarria, Aredhel, Ariel, Black Wallace, Brigitte Mandisa, Frank Weisshadel, Greer Worder, Lord Vauxhall, Macoute, Malachai, Mariel St. John, Rashiel, Tarrence Moore) and the printed traits of the others: sect, Black Hand a trait beside it (LSJ 20070322), Flight among the Disciplines (Malachai, Rocia; 84.37), titles, sterile, infernal, scarce (LSJ 20011015), all generated crypt data. Tests: `the_round_28_text_only_vampires_carry_their_printed_traits`, `black_hand_is_a_trait_beside_the_sect`, `le_dinh_tho_is_scarce`.

**113.4** Al-Muntathir: "4 or more True Brujah" counts every True Brujah his controller controls, torpid ones and himself included (D28), and burns him (a burn) as each step settles (70.19; amber); his, Lydia's and Mikael Birkholm's "cannot use cards requiring Celerity" bars the [cel] mode played only, another mode of the card playable (LSJ 20050322-2). Tests: `al_muntathir_plays_no_celerity_mode`, `lydia_buys_stealth_once_an_action_and_shuns_celerity`.

**113.5** Alcoan's look: in combat with an ally or a vampire of lower capacity in play (D30), once each combat, before range in its first round; Nkechi's alike, its 1 blood a cost (D3), shown to her controller. Tests: `alcoan_looks_at_a_younger_opponents_hand_on_the_first_round`, `nkechi_pays_a_blood_to_see_the_opposing_hand`.

**113.6** Annazir: one printed action of two kinds, once a turn whichever kind (ANK 20220615); directed at the card's controller, not at the minion bearing it; his own Methuselah's location undirected, never a steal of his own Methuselah's equipment (LSJ 20090324, 90.4); his title's votes kept through a sect change (LSJ 20010714). Tests: `annazir_burns_a_location_or_steals_once_a_turn`, `annazir_steals_at_the_bearers_methuselah_not_the_bearer`, `personal_vote_titles_survive_a_sect_change`.

**113.7** Azrael: any hunt of his, a card hunt too (72.1); "another vampire you control" his controller's, torpid too, never an ally (D28); the 1 blood held back and the move asked as the hunt's blood lands, after the hunt's riders, a pass keeping it (amber); at capacity he still would gain it, so 1 may move; a hunter gaining nothing (a disease counter) has nothing to move; the hunt stays successful (89.26, 59.4; amber); he cannot press to end combat. Tests: `azrael_hunts_one_blood_into_another_vampire`, `azrael_cannot_press_to_end_combat`.

**113.8** Baroque's rush: a vampire younger by capacity in play (D30), of neither clan named Giovanni nor Nosferatu exactly (not Hecata, not Nosferatu antitribu: 84.25's exact clan name; amber); Jozz's: twice his capacity in play, read live, the target's capacity as his player reads it (amber); Langa's: another Methuselah's ready vampire, never an ally (17.3). Tests: `baroque_rushes_a_younger_vampire_but_no_giovanni_or_nosferatu`, `jozz_rushes_a_vampire_up_to_twice_his_capacity_or_an_ally`, `langa_rushes_another_methuselahs_vampire_not_an_ally`.

**113.9** Byzar: his burn is replaced (to torpor with a counter), so nothing keyed to his burn fires (Sacrificial Lamb) while burn conditions are met (Judgment: Camarilla Segregation) (LSJ 20091217, LSJ 20091217-2, LSJ 20100423); a diablerie of him is unsuccessful: blood and equipment stay, the diablerist gets nothing, no blood hunt (LSJ 20100228), and no after-diablerie window opens (advanced rules §3.7.8.3; amber); aggravated damage burning him puts one counter per damage resolution (ANK 20210424); yielded in a contest, he burns (LSJ 20100423); at the third counter he goes to torpor, then is removed from the game, not burned (50.6; amber). Tests: `byzar_burned_goes_to_torpor_with_a_counter`, `byzar_spoils_a_sacrificial_lamb`, `byzar_survives_a_diablerie_unsuccessfully`, `byzar_burned_by_aggravated_damage_takes_one_counter`, `byzar_yielded_in_a_contest_burns`, `byzar_leaves_the_game_at_his_third_burn_counter`.

**113.10** Byzar and a second Amaranth (ANK 20210424: "if Amaranth burns him and he comes back in torpor, another Amaranth can be played"): Amaranth's diablerie resolves as the damage lands, inside the combat (revising 111.25), and so do the cards keyed to it and its blood hunt, the combat suspended (owner, 2026-11-11: "during combat yes"; advanced rules §3.7.8.2: an Amaranth blood hunt "happens during combat"; fixed, it waited for the action's last combat), Liquefy [VIC]'s alike (107.30): the window before the hunt takes combat cards (Slake the Thirst [san], ANK 20201228), the combatants vote, the hunt's burn is in combat (Hector Trelane, LSJ 20091210); then the combat resumes, the victim gone ending it as any combatant leaving; each diablerie its own hunt, a second in a chained combat too; on Byzar unsuccessful (LSJ 20100228), his replacement's torpor a new going-to-torpor moment, where another Amaranth may be played, a burn counter each. Red, answered by the owner (2026-11-11: "yes, Amaranth resolves inside combat so a second one can be played"). Marie-Pierre may still enter combat with the diablerist as the combat ends, after the hunt, her Samedi burned in it (her text keys on a combat involving him, as after any burn in combat; ANK 20180928-2: after the combat; amber); Hector Trelane diablerized is burned in combat, the diablerist's controller gaining his 4 pool (LSJ 20091210: "in combat"; amber). The owner's rulings' consequences (amber): after a Liquefy diablerie, the would-end cards and the end-of-combat offers are gated by their own requirements, as after Amaranth's (`liquefy_diablerie_and_blood_hunt_inside_the_combat`); the combat cards offered before the hunt are those whose own definitions key to a diablerie (Slake the Thirst [san]; `slake_the_thirst_follows_amaranth_and_games_of_instinct`); a combat ended by an oust settled before the suspension (a Life Boon declined) runs the hunt first, then ends with no end-of-round window (`amaranth_ousting_the_blockers_methuselah_hunts_before_the_combat_ends`); an oust while the combat is suspended (Ferraille's last pool in the hunt's polling, at once or a Life Boon declined) is read as it resumes, the hunt going on (`an_oust_in_a_suspended_hunt_is_read_as_the_combat_resumes`); Sandra White's pool burn on an older victim is an oust read at once, the ousted Methuselah's action and its hunt gone with it (`sandra_whites_amaranth_ousts_the_acting_methuselah_at_once`). Tests: `byzar_back_in_torpor_after_amaranth_faces_a_second_one`, `amaranth_diablerizes_instead_of_torpor`, `hector_trelane_burned_by_a_blood_hunt_after_amaranth_pays_the_opponent`, `amaranth_blood_hunts_during_each_combat_marie_pierres_too`, `hector_trelane_diablerized_by_amaranth_pays_the_diablerist`, `slake_the_thirst_follows_amaranth_and_games_of_instinct`, `liquefy_diablerie_and_blood_hunt_inside_the_combat`.

**113.11** Cedric's unlock: after he blocks a directed action, in that action's after-combat window (Gold Pan Dan's), once, in torpor too (D25; amber); a combat he takes over as a slave is no block of his. Tests: `cedric_burns_a_blood_to_unlock_after_blocking_a_directed_action`.

**113.12** Chaundice: both uses on one strike, 4 prevented (LSJ 20100708); never aggravated damage, even treated as normal (LSJ 20040812-2, PIB 20130327). Tests: `chaundice_prevents_twice_on_one_strike`, `chaundice_prevents_no_aggravated_damage`.

**113.13** Darlene Killian: allies and retainers she plays cost 1 more blood, on top of a pool cost, paid with the cost; a card played by another card is surcharged (Piper's ally: the engine did not charge it, fixed with 119.2), one put in play pays no cost (58.9, D2; amber). Dovey Ebfwe: +1 stealth on an employ action, from hand or a card's (§3.7). Tests: `darlene_pays_a_blood_more_for_an_ally`, `darlene_pays_a_blood_more_for_pipers_ally`, `dovey_employs_with_a_stealth`.

**113.17** Grotesque: with no selectable location as he enters, removed from the game at once (LSJ 20091216); the location chosen as he enters play, before any contest (LSJ 20080526); "not chosen for another Grotesque" reads every Grotesque in play, any Methuselah's; "you no longer control" his current controller's: the location gone, contested or another's removes him, checked as each step settles, in torpor too (D25; amber); removed, not burned (50.6); one put in play without entering (no location chosen) is never removed by the clause (amber). ## Batch 114 — Changing blocker during an attempt, and the pass that declines (the engine board's "No switching blockers" line, asked of the owner through Z-main; owner, 2026-11-11) (Claude, 2026-11-11)

**114.1** The rulebook's "no other minion can attempt to block until this block attempt is resolved" (the course of an action, B.3; LSJ 20081202 on Faceless Night, "cannot back out") was taken to supersede 48.3's change of blocker, which left open what a pass on an attempt short of the stealth then does (82.1). The owner (2026-11-11): "You can start another attempt while another is going : it stops/fails the other. You cannot have two attempts running at the same time. But either you start another one, or you pass and decline any further block by doing so". So 48.3 stands (the change fails the attempt under way: one attempt at a time), 82.1 stands (the pass on a short attempt is the decline, no further attempt), and the supersession is withdrawn: nothing was to build but 114.2.

**114.2** What the owner's answer (114.1) leaves open. (a) A card whose own minion "unlocks and attempts to block" (Second Tradition: Domain, One With the Land, Guardian Vigil's kind: `Effect::AttemptBlock`), played for another minion during an attempt short of the stealth, makes the change of blocker as Organized Resistance's does (48.3 named "a card whose unlocked minion attempts to block"): it was offered then but only unlocked its minion, no attempt made — a defect, fixed (green). (b) The change while the attempt under way meets the stealth: "while another is going" sets no condition, so it is legal; the engine offers it only while the attempt is short, as since 48.3 — an offer pruning in 48.6's manner, not a rule (amber; confirmed by the owner, 2026-11-11: "ok for the switch"): offered always, it adds a decision to every successful block whose Methuselah has another minion able to block, to abandon a block that holds. Tests: `second_tradition_changes_the_blocker_to_its_prince`, `a_blocker_meeting_the_stealth_is_not_changed`.

## Batch 115 — Inert clauses that gained a pool tenant (the cards board; Z-main's upkeep docs review at e85742f, 2026-11-11: "not in the pool" clauses gone stale as later rounds added their tenants; red: 115.7) (Claude, 2026-11-11; the owner, 2026-11-11: 115.7 confirmed)

**115.1** Abdelsobek's "vampire or mummy you control": a mummy is an ally whose type line says so (Saatet-ta), his controller's; another ally (Mylan Horseed) not. Revises 78.2's "none in the pool". Green (the card's text). (Test abdelsobek_unlocks_a_mummy_ally.)

**115.2** Veneficti's steal takes another Methuselah's demon ally (Herald of Topheth, Infernal Servitor, Nocturn, Impundulu: one cannot steal from oneself, D12); his burn takes any demon retainer (Charnas the Imp), another Methuselah's directed at them, his own Methuselah's undirected (Ⓓ only a reminder, 20.1). Revises 62.30's "no demon retainer is in the pool". Green. (Tests veneficti_steals_a_demon_ally, veneficti_burns_a_demon_retainer.)

**115.3** Winterlich's "can recruit a changeling ally as if he met the requirements of playing that card": the pool's changelings require only a clan (Iara: Malkavian; Draeven Softfoot: Kiasyd, his own), so he recruits Iara from hand as if Malkavian (`VampireDef::recruits_as_if`, read at the play's clan check); never for a burn option (LSJ 20050225; no changeling in the pool has one) nor a card not played normally (LSJ 20050119; no pool card plays a changeling but its own recruit). Revises 89.24's "still inert". Green (the rulings). (Test winterlich_recruits_a_changeling_as_if_he_met_its_requirement.)

**115.4** Tupdog's "Visceratika [vis] cards cost Tupdog 1 less blood": a card offering [vis] or another Discipline by the mode played (PIB 20150924-2): Brick by Brick as [vis] or [VIS] 1 less, as [tha] not. Revises 62.14's "no Visceratika card". Green. (Test tupdog_plays_visceratika_for_one_blood_less.)

**115.5** Louis Fortier, Gengis, Marguerite Foccart, Karsh, Menele, Tegyrius, Vizier, Nergal and Reverend Adams merge, their base cards in the pool: both cards' text and the [MERGED] clause (Louis: +1 level of Dominate and Presence; Gengis: of Auspex and Celerity, his base card's Brujah takeover kept; Marguerite: +1 strength; Karsh: the Imperator title, generated (ANK 20190407), and +1 intercept; Menele: a strike stealing 2 blood or life, Goratrix's, not ranged; Tegyrius: his base card's votes and strength beside the advanced card's polling votes, the Justicar title generated; Nergal: his base card's discount and +2 bleed beside the advanced card's reaction tax and stealth; Reverend Adams: his base card's political -1 stealth beside the advanced card's look, the blockers' clause both print once), the advanced card's sect (ANK 20180524: Brunhilde's and Xaviar's merges tested with them). Sundown's and Boss Callihan's merged definitions were already in (104.20 listed them still). Revises 104.20, 80.5, 80.7. Green. (Tests louis_fortier_merged_gains_superior_dominate_and_presence, gengis_merged_gains_levels_and_keeps_the_brujah_takeover, marguerite_foccart_merged_strikes_harder, karsh_merged_is_the_imperator, menele_merged_strikes_to_steal_two_blood, tegyrius_merged_keeps_his_base_cards_strength, every_merge_keeps_its_base_cards_text, a_merge_takes_the_advanced_cards_sect.)

**115.6** The Fourth Cycle's "burn all Gehenna events and conviction cards": every conviction card in play (on an imbued), any Methuselah's, to its owner's ash heap, with the Gehenna events, before the torpor; none in a hand. Green (the card's text). (Test the_fourth_cycle_burns_the_conviction_cards.)

**115.7** Blood Trade's "Burn all boons. No more boons can be put in play": a boon is a card whose type line says "Boon." (Life Boon, Major Boon, Consanguineous Boon: the generated `Label::Boon`); as Blood Trade enters play every Methuselah's boons in play are burned (green, the text); while it is in play a boon whose play puts it in play (Life Boon, Major Boon) is not played at all, as a unique that cannot enter play is not (7.1), Consanguineous Boon still played (LSJ 20040531-2); so a Life Boon never defers an oust. Red, answered by the owner (2026-11-11: "(a) no": not played); the second reader had read them played, the card to the ash heap, the pool given. (Tests blood_trade_burns_the_boons_in_play, blood_trade_bars_the_boons_entering_play.)

## Batch 116 — Round 29: Keepers of Tradition and its 2018 reprint, 166 cards (103 library cards, 63 vampires) (the cards board; owner, 2026-11-09: the set-group lines; red: 116.2, 116.6, 116.29, 116.34, 116.38, 116.41, 116.42, 116.44, 116.47, 116.50, 116.55, 116.57, 116.66, 116.88, 116.98, 116.129, 116.130) (Claude, 2026-11-12; the owner, 2026-11-12: 116.2, 116.6, 116.29, 116.34, 116.38, 116.41, 116.42, 116.47, 116.50, 116.55, 116.57, 116.88 and 116.98 confirmed, 116.44 confirmed after a first answer withdrawn, then reopened as a reading with the Rules Director (vtes-advanced-rules 1d1c191), 116.66, 116.129 and 116.130 corrected)

Graded per the add-cards skill: 498 readings, two blind reads diffed against the build, every citation checked in its source; 364 green, 115 amber, 19 red (17 asked; Talbot's built against D20 and fixed, 116.126; Bernard's merged into Veles' Hunt's, 116.88). Built wrong and fixed in the round: Will of the Council (116.92), Bulscu's Edge (116.102), the Ghoul Retainer's stake tally (116.74), a chosen steal contesting with oneself (116.6), Agate Talisman's "only one" (116.3), Talbot (116.126), the wake after combat and resolution (116.106), Vidal's alternatives (116.130, the owner's).

**116.1** Abomination: the werewolf burned is the action's target, named at declaration, one recruited this turn too; gone or locked by resolution, the action fizzles (RTR 20180303, D1); "move up to 4 blood" chosen as it resolves, a choice inside the effect (D1); the new vampire may act the turn it enters, nothing printed bars it (Nosferatu Bestial's precedent, §3.7; amber); its additional strike mandatory, after the normal pair, its rush at another Methuselah's ready minion, directed, no stealth (PIB 20121028, 17.3). A created vampire (Abomination, The Becoming) is a library card in the ash heap, not a vampire; banished, it stays a vampire and is influenced again; sent to the crypt, it is burned (LSJ 20020115, RTR 19990712, LSJ 20041201-2). Green unless noted. Tests: `abomination_burns_a_werewolf_and_becomes_a_vampire`, `a_burned_abomination_is_a_library_card_in_the_ash_heap`, `a_banished_created_vampire_can_be_influenced_again`, `a_created_vampire_going_to_the_crypt_is_burned`.

**116.2** Abomination's "It becomes a unique Independent Abomination": a Methuselah's second Abomination action is not offered while theirs is in play (7.1 extended to a created vampire); two Methuselahs' Abominations contest as unique vampires (D5: properties are the card's). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `abomination_is_a_unique_vampire_contested_across_methuselahs`.

**116.3** Agate Talisman: equipped by any minion; locked only by a vampire of capacity 4 or more, read as it is used (LSJ 20030607, D13); "any referendum" a blood hunt's too, before any vote or ballot of it (the no-votes-cast check now reads every referendum, not a political one only); one lock a referendum a copy (D7); a torpid bearer may lock it, its vote not cast (D14, D25; amber); "A vampire can have only one" bars a second copy on a vampire only, an ally or an imbued may hold two (D4; fixed: it masked every minion). Tests: `agate_talisman_on_a_young_vampire_gives_no_vote`, `agate_talisman_votes_in_a_blood_hunt_before_any_cast`, `agate_talisman_is_locked_by_a_torpid_bearer`, `agate_talisman_only_one_is_a_vampires`.

**116.4** Al's Army Apparatus: the weapon found is not shown, none printed (PIB 20121028); "during your minion phase" any time in it, inside actions too, once by its lock, never in the master phase (26.5, D7); with no weapon in the library still offered, the library shuffled (§19.4). Green. Tests: `als_army_apparatus_searches_a_weapon_unseen_in_the_minion_phase`, `als_army_apparatus_searches_in_vain_with_no_weapon`.

**116.5** Anima Gathering: the chosen minion is a target named at declaration, any other minion of its player (D1, D28); its +2 intercept and +1 bleed stand while the card is in play, the bleed not limited (D15's exception); "this minion" that may stay locked and burns it on unlocking is the bearer (LSJ 20060808-2; amber), the choice offered in its controller's unlock phase (amber); any unlock burns it, an effect's too; a Banishment return is no unlock (LSJ 20060213). Tests: `anima_gathering_lends_intercept_while_its_vampire_stays_locked`, `anima_gathering_burns_when_its_vampire_unlocks_by_an_effect`, `anima_gathering_survives_a_banishment`.

**116.6** Arcane Appraiser: the equipment on any Methuselah's torpid vampire is the target, another's directed at them (D12, 20.1); the receiving ready minion, the Appraiser itself included, chosen as the action is announced, both re-checked at resolution (D1, 41.7, D28); a move, not an equip: no requirement, no cost (§3.7); a locquipment on a torpid vampire is no equipment in play, not a target; a Ghoul is a monster, not a mortal (ANK 20200203-2, LSJ 20060515). "If a second copy of a unique equipment you control would enter play, instead it is burned" reaches only a copy coming under the Appraiser's controller (the copy it moves to its player's minion burns, to its owner's ash heap); another Methuselah equipping their own copy contests it as usual. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Found with it (fixed): a chosen steal or move (Bulscu, Reformation [chi]; `Effect::takes_card`) is not offered at a unique card whose copy its player controls, and a copy arriving mid-action leaves it taking nothing; only a forced move (Rowan Ring's and Wooden Stake's transfer, the Appraiser's own clause) burns the incoming copy (rulebook "Contested Cards": you cannot voluntarily contest cards with yourself; advanced rules §1.13.1; 58.19's card analogue). Tests: `arcane_appraiser_moves_equipment_off_a_torpid_vampire`, `arcane_appraiser_burns_a_second_unique_copy_it_moves`, `arcane_appraiser_takes_no_locquipment_and_may_take_for_itself`, `arcane_appraiser_is_no_mortal`, `a_unique_copy_one_controls_is_not_stolen_by_choice`.

**116.7** Armor of Vitality: fewer than 3 damage pending, all prevented, the rest lost; none pending, not playable (RTR 20041202, ANK 20200318, LSJ 20001114). [FOR]'s "any of the damage was from the opposing minion's melee weapon": the opposing strike resolving is a melee weapon's, Bundi's included (LSJ 20080702-1, LSJ 20090114), with strike damage pending; "destroyed" is burned, as the Armor resolves, before its prevention (§3.20; amber). Tests: `armor_of_vitality_prevents_what_there_is`, `armor_of_vitality_needs_damage_to_prevent`, `armor_of_vitality_superior_destroys_the_striking_melee_weapon`, `armor_of_vitality_superior_destroys_a_striking_bundi`.

**116.8** Arms Dealer: a library card labelled weapon, found as the action succeeds, not shown, the library shuffled, then a discard down to hand size ("discard afterward", 78.26); undirected (17.2). Green. Test: `arms_dealer_fetches_a_weapon_and_discards_down`.

**116.9** Backstep: only at the maneuver, from close range; "successfully inflicts" is damage landing after prevention, any opposing strike of the round, additional ones too, never environmental or retainer damage (29.3, §3.20); the press the opposing minion's, optional, this round only (TOM 19960521); two copies, two presses (amber). Tests: `backstep_goes_long_and_gives_a_press_if_hit`, `backsteps_press_is_for_its_round_only`, `two_backsteps_give_two_presses`.

**116.10** The plain weapons and ally: Bang Nakh — Tiger's Claws a strength+2 melee strike; Sengir Dagger strength aggravated at close range; Mark V 4R at long range only, once each round a copy, a second copy usable as the additional strike (ANK 20230316); "Weapon, gun." on the type line is a gun (Desert Eagle, Sawed-Off Shotgun, Beretta 9mm, Mark V: the generator read only "Gun"); Loyal Street Gang a Brujah ally whose type line is all its text. Green. Tests: `bang_nakh_strikes_for_strength_plus_two`, `sengir_dagger_cuts_aggravated_by_strength`, `a_second_mark_v_fires_again_in_the_round`, `a_weapon_comma_gun_is_a_gun`, `a_comma_gun_is_a_gun_for_kevlar_vest`, `loyal_street_gang_is_a_plain_brujah_ally`.

**116.11** Beretta 9mm: "another Beretta" read live; the 2R strike and the maneuver need two; the maneuver is the 2R strike's, committing the round's initial strike, one copy's maneuver a round, each copy's once each combat (10.1, 113.150, LSJ 20090528-2-2; amber); no maneuver while its strike is barred (LSJ 20021028). Tests: `beretta_pair_strikes_2r_and_maneuvers_once_a_round`, `beretta_maneuver_is_not_used_while_its_strike_is_barred`.

**116.12** The Becoming: the Discipline cards chosen from hand as it resolves, 0 to 3, put (no cost, no requirement), replaced (D1, D2, advanced rules §6.8; amber); "capacity-increasing discipline cards" the master Discipline cards giving a level, the burn read after the picks (D13; amber); a 0-capacity result floors at 1, and it is a 1-capacity vampire in the uncontrolled region (LSJ 20090625); the sire's clan and sect fixed as it resolves, idle this turn, not unique (D5). Tests: `the_becoming_makes_a_vampire_of_its_discipline_cards`, `the_becoming_burns_without_discipline_cards`, `a_banished_becoming_is_a_one_capacity_vampire`.

**116.13** Blade of Enoch: equipped by any minion; its strike and frenzy shield only for a vampire of capacity 6 or more, read live (LSJ 20030607); aggravated against a vampire of clan Brujah or Brujah antitribu, never an ally (no clan); "Frenzy cards cannot be used on this vampire" leaves its bearer's own Dam the Heart's River on the opponent playable (ANK 20220409). Green. Tests: `blade_of_enoch_on_a_young_vampire_neither_strikes_nor_shields`, `blade_of_enoch_cuts_brujah_aggravated_and_shields_from_frenzy`.

**116.14** Blood Hunt: "another Methuselah" is one other than the hunted vampire's controller, so Blood Hunt's player may rush too; the rush at +1 stealth, directed at that controller (Gengis G3's precedent, D5; amber); the target's titles read effective (§3.15; amber). Karsh (ADV) plays it from hand or from his player's ash heap as a prince, for that card only (no prince votes), from the ash heap not replaced (LSJ 20101013, 59.14). Revises 80.5 and 111.19 ("no card named Blood Hunt is in the pool"). Muaziz's ruling (TOM 19960604) n/a, Muaziz not in the pool. Tests: `blood_hunt_lets_others_rush_the_hunted_vampire`, `karsh_plays_blood_hunt_from_his_ash_heap_as_a_prince`.

**116.15** Blood of Sandman: [tha] burns any ally, one's own undirected, burned by a Ⓓ action (D12, 94.5); [THA] a ready locked younger vampire, unlocked by resolution a fizzle, its 2 blood unpaid (ANK 20210124, LSJ 20070411, 117.1); "reaction cards" are the cards of the Reaction type its locked bearer plays when not acting, canceled as played, their cost paid (D18, ANK 20260216); it burns as its bearer goes to torpor. Green. Tests: `blood_of_sandman_burns_an_ally`, `blood_of_sandman_fizzles_on_a_target_unlocked_by_resolution`, `blood_of_sandman_cancels_the_locked_vampires_reactions`, `blood_of_sandman_burns_when_its_vampire_goes_to_torpor`.

**116.16** Bomb: a 5R strike; its bearer takes 5 environmental damage whenever its strike resolves, dodged too, none if the combat ends first; burned after its strike (LSJ 19970801, LSJ 19981006, 84.2); the location action at any location, another's directed at its controller, the Bomb burned only on success (LSJ 20090324); blocked, no forced Bomb strike, the Bomb kept (LSJ 19971215); a fizzle (the Bomb off the actor, the location gone) has no effect and keeps the Bomb (RTR 19960221, 117.1). Green. Tests: `bomb_strikes_5r_burns_and_hurts_its_bearer`, `bomb_is_kept_when_combat_ends_before_its_strike`, `bomb_burns_a_location_directed_at_its_controller`, `bomb_action_blocked_keeps_the_bomb`, `bomb_action_fizzles_without_the_bomb`, `bomb_action_fizzles_on_a_gone_location_and_keeps_the_bomb`.

**116.17** Bounty: X 0 or more, X+1 counters, the card on any Methuselah's ready vampire, its player's own too (LSJ 20030502, D11); torpor in combat: the opposing minion's controller takes 0 to 2 counters, none for a torpor outside combat; burned in combat (by its opponent, whatever the cause: advanced rules §5.4.1, LSJ 20090922) or by a diablerie (its acting vampire): all the counters, the card burning with the vampire (amber); emptied by a payout, burned. Tests: `bounty_at_x_zero_holds_one_counter`, `bounty_pays_the_combatant_who_sends_it_to_torpor_and_the_diablerist`, `bounty_goes_on_its_players_own_vampire`, `bounty_pays_all_for_a_burn_in_combat_and_burns_when_emptied`.

**116.18** Brick Laying: "younger" than the acting vampire by capacity; "that minion" the haven's host or the locked one, its damage out of combat (D30, §12.7); "a haven" a card labelled Haven (amber); [POT] an ally burned by the damage is burned by a Ⓓ action (ANK 20220507). Tests: `brick_laying_burns_a_haven_on_a_younger_vampire_and_wounds_it`, `brick_laying_locks_an_ally_and_its_damage_burns_it`.

**116.19** Brujah, Tremere and Ventrue Justicar: only a ready Camarilla vampire of the clan is chosen, none and it is not played (LSJ 20041026, LSJ 20100129); "each Tremere gets 1 extra vote" (no "ready"): a torpid one casts nothing (D25); an antitribu is another clan; another holder of the title, its Methuselah's own too, contests it (3.1); on a titled prince the card's title replaces the printed one (19.2). Every title card: the chosen vampire re-checked as the referendum passes, no longer choosable and the card has no effect (D1; fixed: the term was read for presence only). Green. Tests: `keepers_justicars_title_a_ready_camarilla_vampire_of_their_clan`, `a_justicar_title_replaces_a_printed_princes_title`, `persona_non_grata_has_no_effect_on_a_vampire_titled_since_its_terms`.

**116.20** Brute Force: each level splits in two (a hand strike, or a melee weapon's own strike with its riders, at +n), held and replaced as the combat ends (57.4, 29.7); the added damage of the strike's kind (RTR 19970630, TOM 19960225); its weapon mode with Bundi under Immortal Grapple (LSJ 20090114, LSJ 20080702-1). Green. Tests: `brute_force_strikes_by_hand_or_weapon`, `brute_force_adds_to_the_strikes_kind`, `bundi_strikes_as_a_hand_and_a_melee_weapon_strike`.

**116.21** Bundi: its strike is a hand strike (Immortal Grapple allows it, hand-strike improvers apply) and a melee weapon strike (Wendell's +2, Armor of Vitality's check) (TOM 19960225, LSJ 20071020, LSJ 20090114); it prevents 1 of each opposing melee weapon strike's damage, aggravated first, once each strike exchange, never a gun's, no strike with Bundi forced (LSJ 19971215); two copies prevent 1 each (amber); a Ghoul Retainer's weapon use is no strike, nothing prevented (LSJ 20080210; amber). Tests: `bundi_strikes_as_a_hand_and_a_melee_weapon_strike`, `bundi_prevents_one_from_each_melee_weapon_strike`, `two_bundis_prevent_one_each`, `bundi_prevents_nothing_of_a_ghoul_retainers_weapon_use`.

**116.22** Burst of Sunlight: the striker's own damage is environmental, at any range (LSJ 19970801, ANK 20221212), taken though the strike is dodged, none when the combat ends before the strike resolves (advanced rules §4.6.3, §4.7.1; Breath of the Dragon's build; amber); an ally playing it as a vampire is n/a (none in the pool, LSJ 20060409). Tests: `burst_of_sunlight_burns_the_striker_at_any_range`, `burst_of_sunlight_self_damage_survives_a_dodge`, `burst_of_sunlight_hurts_nobody_once_combat_ends_first`.

**116.23** Can't Take it with You: 1 pool for each equipment, location and retainer card (LSJ 20071005-2), a card on a minion counting for the minion's controller (D5); a locquipment in play one location card (§3.17; amber); allies, contested cards and effect-only stand-ins never; the gain first, then the burn, the ousts as one batch. Green, the locquipment amber. Test: `cant_take_it_with_you_burns_a_pool_per_equipment_location_and_retainer`.

**116.24** Charming Lobby, the named political action: named as Charming Lobby is played (its terms), a political action card from hand with its mode (requirements and cost checked as for its own play, an unaffordable one not offered: ANK 20220704 "The requirements and cost … apply"; amber), kept in hand through the action, not shown; gone from hand by resolution, the action fizzles, nothing paid (amber); canceled as played, it stays in hand, unpaid (LSJ 20100130: "must be announced and in hand when Charming Lobby is played … If the action is canceled or failed, the political action card stays in hand"). At successful resolution the named card is played: out of hand, replaced, its card ballot offered, counted for "only one … each turn / game", never through an as-played window (no Direct Intervention) (ANK 20220704, LSJ 20090113-3); its sect and title requirements re-read then, and not the action's (CrimethInc. not offered after a named Firebrand: LSJ 20100725, LSJ 20100303, ANK 20180817); its cost paid then, its own (printed pool and blood with the actor's printed reductions, no action-cost source: never Ravnos Carnival; an X chosen then, 0 to the acting vampire's blood) (LSJ 20100819, LSJ 20091021); unpayable then, a fizzle (117.1), the card staying in hand. "Allowed by an effect in play": a political action a card in play or a vampire's text lets the acting vampire take, named alike, its own printed pool paid, its printed stealth not added, no card ballot (ANK 20201029 excludes only the "as if from hand" plays; amber); a card played as if from hand (Echo of Harmonies, n/a) not offered; nothing to name, Charming Lobby is not offered. Charming Lobby is a political action, not a political action card: no ballot of its own (ANK 20220704, LSJ 20070927, LSJ 20050407). Tests: `charming_lobby_calls_a_hand_political_action_and_the_next_passes`, `charming_lobby_named_card_stays_in_hand_when_canceled`, `charming_lobby_named_card_cannot_be_canceled`, `charming_lobby_named_requirements_are_not_the_actions`, `charming_lobby_pays_its_cards_x_at_resolution_never_by_ravnos_carnival`, `charming_lobby_calls_a_referendum_a_card_in_play_allows`, `charming_lobby_needs_a_referendum_to_name`.

**116.25** Charming Lobby, after it: "the next referendum called by any vampire thereafter passes automatically": any Methuselah's vampire, no "this turn" (kept until spent), no polling, no margin (PIB 20110719, LSJ 19980107); a blood hunt is called by no vampire, neither passing by it nor spending it (TOM 19950921); an ally calling "as a vampire" does not spend it (Cryptic Rider's "vampire"; amber); a Charming Lobby referendum passed by an earlier pass sets it again (amber). [PRE]: the acting vampire's 2 votes in that first referendum only. Delaying Tactics returns the named card to hand, Charming Lobby to the ash heap, and bars Charming Lobby by name this turn (ANK 20201029-2, LSJ 20030426); the action's card stays Charming Lobby once the named referendum is called: Epikasta recycles it, Hay Ride replays it (LSJ 20090430, ANK 20180628); an ally playing it as a vampire (Herald of Topheth [pre]) names with its grant's traits (RTR 20020501; amber). Revises 62.21's "none in the pool": a card Charming Lobby plays is not the action's (CrimethInc.). Green unless noted. Tests: `charming_lobby_superior_gives_two_votes_in_its_referendum`, `a_blood_hunt_does_not_spend_charming_lobbys_pass`, `delaying_tactics_returns_charming_lobbys_named_card`, `epikasta_retrieves_charming_lobby_whatever_it_called`, `hay_ride_replays_charming_lobby_not_its_named_card`, `herald_of_topheth_calls_a_referendum_with_charming_lobby`.

**116.26** Childling Muse: +1 bleed to a Malkavian employer only, read on the current bearer (9.1); on a Malkavian ally nothing, an ally having no clan (amber; moot: an ally cannot employ it). Test: `childling_muse_gives_its_malkavian_a_bleed`.

**116.27** Contingency Planning: usable on its player's own turn (printed), still an out-of-turn master spending the next master phase action (3.3, 84.15; adv §6.6.2; amber); a minion card whose played mode redirects the bleed (Deflection, Redirection), not Two Wrongs (a master); the pool burned capped at 1 for the whole action, whoever is bled, the bleed amount kept (LSJ 20030211, 80.23; amber); the canceled card still played, its cost unpaid (ANK 20190104, LSJ 19980212), one played from The Erciyes Fragments too (LSJ 20060106). Tests: `contingency_planning_cancels_a_deflection_and_caps_the_bleed`, `contingency_planning_cancels_a_bounce_played_from_the_erciyes_fragments`, `contingency_planning_on_its_own_turn_spends_the_next_master_phase_action`.

**116.28** Coterie Tactics: the partner another ready unlocked vampire of its controller, of the reacting vampire's sect (two Independents match), able to attempt itself (prey or predator, bars, tolls) (ANK 20180623, LSJ 20010714-2, 42.8; amber); either already attempting, the attempt kept and the other joins, its toll paid then; another of its Methuselah's minions attempting, a change of blocker (48.3, 114.1; amber); intercept summed live, either vampire may play intercept cards for it, a reduction on "the blocking minion" (Suppressing Fire) lowering the sum (amber); "both lock and then you choose": both lock as the block succeeds, each keeping its own no-lock effects, then the reacting Methuselah picks the blocker; the one not picked has blocked (Cats' Guidance, Minor Irritation, Mental Maze read it) and is not in combat (LSJ 20090309). Tests: `coterie_tactics_blocks_with_the_sum_and_its_player_picks_the_blocker`, `coterie_tactics_is_not_offered_where_it_cannot_attempt`, `coterie_tactics_joins_an_attempt_under_way`, `minor_irritation_unlocks_the_coterie_vampire_not_picked`.

**116.29** Coterie Tactics' "These two vampires attempt to block": one joint attempt; a partner dropping out before the block resolves (locked, burned, barred) fails it as a whole, both recorded failed, and the other cannot attempt again this action (one attempt at a time, 114.1; a lapsed requirement ends it, 92.1). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Tests: `coterie_tactics_joint_attempt_fails_with_its_partner`, `coterie_tactics_fails_whole_once_one_partner_locks`.

**116.30** Dark Mirror of the Mind: capacity read live (D13). Forgery: successful for 1 or more, the blood gain capped by capacity (§3.2). The Haunting: on any minion, one's own undirected; "their unlock phase" its controller's; blood, or life for an ally (D12, 73.5); one on a minion, masked as played (99.1). Green. Tests: `dark_mirror_of_the_mind_gains_two_pool_for_an_elder`, `forgery_bleeds_and_feeds_its_vampire`, `the_haunting_bleeds_its_minion_each_unlock_phase_and_can_be_burned`.

**116.31** Day Operation: "Vampires cannot block": allies may; a vampire's attempt under way fails, barred, at [FOR] (92.1); Eagle's Sight, Falcon's Eye and Anneke do not lift it (RTR 19950413, RTR 20020501, LSJ 20041203); the torpor whenever the action reaches resolution, blocked by an ally after its combat too, not when canceled before (RTR 19980623, ANK 20220331, ANK 20220401, 23.3; Daring the Dawn's precedent; amber); the torpor and damage after resolution (Force of Will) ordered by the acting Methuselah (LSJ 20040120-2; the new `torpor_after_resolution` choice); [for] as announced only, before other modifiers, the action card replaced first; [FOR] as announced or while a minion attempts (ANK 20190425, ANK 20181208); an acting ally goes to no torpor (D16; n/a in the pool). Tests: `day_operation_bars_vampires_and_sends_the_actor_to_torpor`, `day_operation_superior_fails_a_vampires_attempt_under_way`, `day_operation_is_not_circumvented_by_eagles_sight_or_anneke`, `day_operation_and_force_of_will_resolve_in_either_order`, `day_operation_sends_the_actor_to_torpor_after_an_allys_block`, `as_announced_modifiers_precede_other_modifiers`.

**116.32** Dragon's Breath Rounds: after strikes are declared, before resolution, on the user's own gun, one ammo a gun each combat (RTR 19990105, LSJ 20020425, 111.17); the base damage normal, +2 aggravated beside it, none on a strike dealing none (LSJ 20030419-2, RTR 19960221), damage from gun strikes (Kevlar Vest prevents it, the aggravated first; 10.3; amber); the combat ending before the strike resolves keeps the gun (LSJ 19981006, LSJ 20001127-2); dodged, the strike resolved, the gun burns (84.2; amber). Revises 64.10's "No ammo card is in the pool": Zip Gun's "Ammo cards cannot be used with this gun" is now enforced (fixed: inert, with Scattershot already in the pool). Tests: `dragons_breath_rounds_is_loaded_only_on_the_users_gun_after_strikes`, `dragons_breath_rounds_adds_aggravated_damage_and_burns_the_gun`, `dragons_breath_rounds_spares_the_gun_when_combat_ends_first`, `dragons_breath_rounds_burns_the_gun_though_dodged`, `kevlar_vest_prevents_dragons_breath_aggravated_damage`, `zip_gun_takes_no_ammo`.

**116.33** Elder Kindred Network: "the Methuselah calling the referendum" its caller; "each vote difference" against minus for at the tally, a tie failing by 0 (1 pool); not during a referendum passing automatically; a canceled referendum (no tally) burns nothing (PIB 20150105, LSJ 19980107, LSJ 20010209); two copies by two vampires burn each (Rant!'s per-play damage; amber); the burn may oust. Tests: `elder_kindred_network_burns_the_caller_per_vote_difference`, `elder_kindred_network_is_not_offered_on_an_automatic_pass`, `elder_kindred_network_copies_each_burn`.

**116.34** Elder Kindred Network on a referendum forced to fail with more votes for it (Confusion of the Eye [OBF], 1 for, 0 against): no vote difference against, the caller burns 1. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `elder_kindred_network_forced_fail_burns_one`.

**116.35** Elysium: The Arboretum: its controller's Camarilla vampire in combat with another Camarilla vampire (never an ally) ends it before range each round, end-of-round cards still played (RTR 20001020, LSJ 20001024); the burn referendum any Camarilla vampire's +1 stealth political action, undirected even by another's (rulebook "political actions are always undirected"; 113.70) — the readings' "directed at the card's controller" was wrong; Zoning Board's and Tabriz Assembly's burn referendums alike (77.26 corrected). Green. Tests: `elysium_the_arboretum_ends_a_camarilla_combat_before_range`, `elysium_the_arboretum_is_burned_by_an_undirected_camarilla_referendum`.

**116.36** Eyes of the Beast: the +1 intercept for this action, the card itself put on the vampire, -1 stealth while there (47.1; Under My Skin's shape; amber), until its controller's discard phase, played in another's turn too (D11); [PRO]'s maneuver an if-blocks rider of this action only, given again to a second block in it (ANK 20191108); two copies stack, -2 stealth (D4; amber). Tests: `eyes_of_the_beast_stays_on_its_vampire_until_its_discard_phase`, `eyes_of_the_beast_superior_maneuver_in_its_action_only`, `eyes_of_the_beast_copies_stack_on_their_vampire`.

**116.37** First Tradition: The Masquerade: the skip decided just before the unlock phase, before any unlock, Bartholomew's and the held replacements (ANK 20210110); not skipped, the 2 pool an unlock-phase due its payer orders (Vessel first; 6.1); burned only once one condition is met fully (three skips, or 6 pool paid for this copy: LSJ 20031011), only the controller's skips and pool for this copy counting; copies cumulative (4 pool), one skip counting on each (RTR 19941222); every Methuselah pays and may skip, its controller too (D11); short of 2 pool, they burn what they have and are ousted, an effect (D3; Anarch Revolt's; amber); played in a minion phase, first due at each Methuselah's next unlock phase (D10). Tests: `first_tradition_burns_two_pool_each_unlock_and_itself_after_six`, `first_tradition_skip_comes_before_the_unlock_and_vessel_orders_first`, `two_first_traditions_burn_four_and_a_skip_counts_on_both`, `first_tradition_ousts_a_methuselah_short_of_two_pool`.

**116.38** First Tradition's "A Methuselah can choose to skip his or her entire turn": none of the turn happens: no unlock, no unlock-phase dues, no Edge pool, no master, minion, influence or discard phase; "until your next unlock phase" effects keep waiting; the turn count does not advance; the prey's turn starts; a withdrawal kept until this unlock phase is checked before the skip offer. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Tests: `first_tradition_skipped_turns_burn_it_after_three`, `first_tradition_skipped_turn_ends_no_unlock_effect`.

**116.39** Force of Personality: [PRE] as announced, before regular modifiers, the action card replaced first (ANK 20190425, ANK 20181208); a vampire pays 1 blood to attempt to block, a non-zombie ally cannot, a zombie ally or an imbued attempts free (Stiff Contempt's reading, §3.6); [pre] a plain "strike: combat ends". Green. Tests: `force_of_personality_taxes_vampires_and_bars_non_zombie_allies`, `force_of_personality_ends_combat`, `an_as_announced_modifier_drawn_as_the_replacement_is_playable`.

**116.40** Ghoul Retainer: 1 damage at close range only, environmental, the retainer its source, through a dodge, in the normal pair only (ANK 20210929, ANK 20180612, RTR 19941109); instead, the use of a weapon of the employer not struck with nor committed this round nor picked by another retainer, chosen in the employer's strike step, the employer then not striking with it that round; the employer is the bearer (Zip Gun's and Bomb's damage on him) and the whole strike text resolves — Rowan Ring's torpor and transfer, Flash Grenade's end, Weighted Walking Stick's counters, an empty Jar of Skin-Eaters, the Wooden Stake's tally (fixed: its damage was left off the stake's count) — but no maneuver, press or additional strike (Meat Hook), no ammo played by it, the employer's loaded ammo applying (LSJ 20080210: "The Ghoul uses weapon as if making the strike, including all side-effects"; "not a strike"; ANK 20171003); a monster, not a mortal. Green. Tests: `ghoul_retainer_bites_at_close_range_only`, `ghoul_retainer_fires_the_employers_zip_gun`, `ghoul_retainer_uses_rowan_ring_and_flash_grenade`, `ghoul_retainer_spends_weighted_walking_stick_counters`, `ghoul_retainers_meat_hook_gives_no_extra_strike`, `ghoul_retainer_uses_an_empty_jar_of_skin_eaters`, `ghoul_retainer_plays_no_ammo_but_uses_loaded_scattershot`, `ghoul_retainers_wooden_stake_damage_counts_for_the_stake`.

**116.41** Ghoul Retainer's weapon use goes through the opponent's dodge: its damage the retainer's environmental damage, the use "not a strike" (LSJ 20080210; group "Environmental damage": "Damage is inflicted even against a dodge"); a Rowan Ring's torpor alike. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `ghoul_retainers_weapon_use_goes_through_a_dodge`.

**116.42** Ghoul Retainer with a "strength+N" weapon (Bang Nakh's strength+2): the ghoul's printed 1 strength, 3 damage, not the employer's 3+2. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `ghoul_retainer_swings_a_melee_weapon_with_its_own_strength`.

**116.43** Haymaker: "last round" the previous round of this combat, a new combat starting clean, a second Haymaker in the same round not barred (D4, D32; amber); the committed strike the initial strike only, mandatory once played, none if a later effect bars it; not playable once a strike is committed this round (12.3, 4.3, 55.8; the commitment bar amber); the opposing initial strike gets first strike, a dodge facing it still protecting, both Haymakers both first (58.2); +1 of the hand strike's kind (TOM 19960225). Tests: `haymaker_is_not_played_two_rounds_running`, `haymaker_commits_a_hand_strike_and_gives_first_strike`, `haymaker_adds_to_an_aggravated_hand_strike`.

**116.44** Haymaker's "If either minion inflicts more damage than the other this round": the damage each minion's strikes inflicted on the other this round, a dodged strike none, prevented or not, and damage the struck minion is immune to too (Mictlantecuhtli under a weapon, Tye Cooper under a hand strike); never environmental or retainer damage. "Successfully inflicted" is 116.138. No ruling covers Haymaker, so its text applies exactly: a reading, open with the Rules Director (vtes-advanced-rules 1d1c191 §4.5.5: "without such a ruling the printed 'inflicts' counts prevented damage, e.g. {Haymaker} *(reading)*"; the owner, 2026-11-12: "it is likely to be of the same category as Twisted the Knife or Blood Clot if I'm being honest. But we would need a Rules Director ruling (which we could not find) to make it sure."). Amber, open. A's 2, all prevented, beat X's 1: A gets the press; a strike prevented by a round-long prevention (Rolling with the Punches [FOR]) counts too, as one on an immune minion. Tests: `haymaker_press_counts_prevented_damage`, `haymaker_counts_strikes_prevented_for_the_round`, `haymaker_counts_damage_on_an_immune_minion`.

**116.45** High Ground: the range it sets holds, determine range skipped, other pre-range cards still playable, an earlier setter first (RTR 19970630, ANK 20180720, PIB 20120214, 84.1); one a minion each round, either use, its maneuver only from close range; flight read live, the range set no maneuver ("cannot maneuver" does not stop it) (D13; amber). Tests: `high_ground_sets_long_range_for_a_flier`, `high_ground_yields_to_an_earlier_range_setter`, `high_ground_maneuvers_only_to_long`.

**116.46** High Museum of Art, Atlanta: +4 as it enters play, -4 when burned, stolen, moved to a library or contested, +4 to whoever gains control; a second copy entering: its player +4, then both contested, each holder -4 (advanced rules §1.13.2: entry effects before the contest; amber); moved to its owner's library by Zoning Board, X 0 and -4; its controller ousted, nothing (amber). Tests: `high_museum_pays_four_and_costs_four_when_stolen_or_contested`, `high_museum_costs_four_when_burned`, `zoning_board_moves_a_high_museum_for_no_pool_and_it_costs_four`.

**116.47** High Museum's contest won back (the rival copy yielded): control gained again, +4 again (the card reads becoming contested as losing control). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `high_museum_pays_four_again_when_its_contest_is_won`.

**116.48** Honor the Elders: ready is not unlocked, a locked one gains (50.5); capacity read as it passes (D30); every uncontrolled region, by printed capacity, a banished vampire's own (D11; amber); one at capacity gains nothing beyond it. **116.49** Hostile Takeover: any vampire of capacity 6 or less, in torpor or one's own too; the change for good, still stoppable (Diamond Thunderbolt), the bids paid (D6, LSJ 20040518); the highest bid the controller's twice their pool, the others' their pool, the controller winning burns half rounded up (LSJ 19990825). Green. Tests: `hostile_takeover_lets_the_controller_bid_twice_their_pool`, `hostile_takeover_reaches_torpor_and_own_vampires_and_its_bids_stay_paid`.

**116.50** Hostile Takeover's auction: open and ascending, clockwise from its player, each bid strictly above the last (from 1); one who passed may bid again when the turn to bid comes back; it ends once all the others pass in a row after the last bid; no bid, no change (LSJ 19990104: "open format, similar to a referendum"). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `hostile_takeover_auctions_control_and_pays_the_controller`.

**116.51** Instability: playable while the prey or no one holds the Edge, read again as it resolves, the prey may take it; one each turn, any Methuselah's, canceled ones counting (D13, D27). King's Rising: pool read as it resolves, 5 or fewer +3, else +1; no Transfer nor Withdraw while it is in play, the crypt draw and merges allowed (only counter moves are transfers; amber); the Edge read in its controller's unlock phase, burned then (6.1, D10). Tests: `instability_offers_the_free_edge_to_the_prey_and_gains_two`, `kings_rising_gains_by_pool_bars_transfer_moves_and_burns_with_the_edge`.

**116.52** Keep it Simple: X fixed as the card resolves, the action's current stealth (cards, printed, reductions), none below 1 (a legal no-op at 0 or less) (Night Terrors, RTR 20030519; D14, D24); the reduction stays with the bleed after a redirect (Deep Ecology's; amber); an ally cannot pay its blood cost. Tests: `keep_it_simple_reduces_a_bleed_by_the_stealth_as_played`, `keep_it_simple_follows_a_redirected_bleed`.

**116.53** Leech: an imbued may play it (an imbued is a mortal ally): it unlocks, burns its convictions, takes the Potence grant (113.129; the second reader agrees; amber, graded down from red); a vampire with no blood a legal target (RTR 20010711); the stolen blood a life, no cap (D4; amber); basic Potence until the ally's controller's next unlock phase, one grant a card, never superior with another grant, the ally having none (ANK 20180111, LSJ 19980303, LSJ 20011217, LSJ 20020218).
