**116.54** Malkavian Prank: each other Methuselah holds 1 to 4 secretly, the player guesses each, all revealed at once; a right guess against less pool takes all they have, ousting them (StealPoolFromTarget's reading; amber). Test: `malkavian_prank_right_guess_takes_all_and_ousts`.

**116.55** Malkavian Prank's "If you guess incorrectly, that Methuselah gains that much pool": the number held (Ben held 3, Anna guessed 4: Ben gains 3). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `malkavian_prank_steals_on_a_right_guess_and_pays_from_the_bank_on_a_wrong_one`.

**116.56** Martinelli's Ring: "cards requiring" Dementation or Serpentis are those with that printed Discipline or clan requirement, in any mode played (17.4, 113.82); a granted action of a card requiring Malkavian (Muddled Vampire Hunter's rush) cannot target the bearer (LSJ 20080604; amber); such cards on the bearer burn as the Ring arrives and as they arrive later (amber). Test: `martinellis_ring_shields_its_bearer_from_dementation_cards`, `martinellis_ring_burns_a_malkavian_retainer_on_its_bearer`.

**116.57** Martinelli's Ring's bearer "cannot use [ser] or [dem] cards on themselves" (LSJ 20080212, "eg. Mummify"): only such a card targeting him or put on him is barred; his own [dem] action modifiers on his bleed and his [dem] cards aimed at others stay playable (caps read narrowly, D4). Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-12). Test: `martinellis_ring_bars_mummify_but_not_a_dementation_bleed`.

**116.58** Minor Boon: usable as a ready vampire of another Methuselah is about to go to torpor, by any cause: its torpor waits, the vampire provisionally in torpor, the others asked clockwise from the active Methuselah, its controller skipped; in combat asked as the combat ends ("Is played after the combat ends, keeping the vampire out of torpor", RTR 19950509-2), the combat still ending; out of combat asked at once (Redline's deferred burn; amber); several at once queued, each in turn (amber), those declined going to torpor together once the last is answered, lost together (118.2); saved, the vampire never went to torpor, no torpor rider (amber); declined, the torpor lands with its riders, read with its combat (Bounty paying the vanquisher); not on its player's own turn (rulebook "You cannot play an out-of-turn master card on your own turn"); "This vampire cannot bleed you": its player, every bleed of it and every bleed redirected to them (PIB 20130711); burned as its vampire's rescue succeeds on a vampire its player controls (amber); two on a vampire legal, each its player's. Tests: `minor_boon_keeps_a_vampire_out_of_torpor_after_combat`, `minor_boon_declined_lets_the_torpor_and_its_riders_land`, `minor_boon_declined_twice_lands_the_torpors_together`, `minor_boon_is_not_played_on_its_players_own_turn`, `minor_boon_bars_bleeding_and_redirecting_to_its_player`, `minor_boon_burns_when_its_vampire_rescues_the_players_vampire`.

**116.59** Minor Irritation: played after the block succeeds, before combat, the block's lock undone: a blocker unlocked before left unlocked, one woken left locked, no unlock (The Sleeping Mind does not bar it) (Mental Maze, Change of Target: "left as it was", LSJ 19980224; amber); "younger" than this vampire by capacity, an ally always (D30); Coterie Tactics' vampire not picked undoes its lock alike (LSJ 20090309; amber). Tests: `minor_irritation_keeps_its_blocker_unlocked`, `minor_irritation_unlocks_the_coterie_vampire_not_picked`.

**116.60** Mouthpiece: [DOM] by the acting Methuselah's other ready vampire, locked or not (LSJ 19990425, LSJ 19980210), reaching an acting ally or a vampire lacking [dom] (group "Meets missing disciplines", LSJ 19980303, D16; amber), basic only even with two copies, one requirement of a combo card, two grants never two missing ones (ANK 20180111, LSJ 20011217, LSJ 20020218), until the action ends, its combat included; [dom] +1 bleed, limited. Green unless noted. Tests: `mouthpiece_superior_lets_the_acting_minion_play_basic_dominate`, `mouthpiece_superior_is_only_for_the_acting_methuselah_and_stacks_by_minion`, `mouthpiece_meets_one_requirement_of_a_multi_discipline_card`, `mouthpiece_and_an_allys_own_grant_never_meet_a_combo_card_together`, `mouthpiece_inferior_adds_a_limited_bleed`.

**116.61** Muddled Vampire Hunter: first strike on each of his strikes, his rush at another Methuselah's ready vampire (rulebook "First Strike", 17.3). Wendell Delburton: +2 with melee weapons, of the strike's kind, none on a strike dealing none (RTR 19970630, TOM 19960225, RTR 19960221); a 2R strike, one maneuver each combat, his rush at another Methuselah's minion (17.3); in the unlock phase +1 life at 2 or less, then 2 unpreventable damage on a ready vampire his controller picks, Carolina Vález immune (6.1, ANK 20210228). Green. Tests: `muddled_vampire_hunter_rushes_and_strikes_first`, `wendell_hits_harder_with_melee_weapons`, `wendell_adds_nothing_to_rowan_ring`, `wendell_rushes_and_maneuvers`, `carolina_takes_no_unlock_damage_from_wendell`.

**116.62** National Guard Support: any Methuselah's copy in play bars another (D11); every minion's directed actions -1 stealth, allies too, never undirected ones (3.4); a counter at its controller's unlock phase from the next (D10); the burn referendum any vampire's +1 stealth political action, undirected, no title needed ("Requires a titled vampire" is the play's) (6.2); the counters' votes the card's own, cast by no Methuselah (Bribes ignores them), counted as polling opens (1.1; amber). Tests: `national_guard_support_is_not_played_while_another_is_in_play`, `national_guard_support_slows_directed_actions_and_counts_turns`, `national_guard_support_counter_votes_burn_it`.

**116.63** New Management: the location's printed cost, either currency (D4; amber); requirements not checked; one's own location allowed (no change); the card replaced after the action, not when blocked (TOM 19960226-1, LSJ 20080803-2, LSJ 20001120, LSJ 20101021). Tests: `new_management_takes_a_cheap_location`, `new_management_is_not_replaced_when_blocked`.

**116.64** Nocturn Theater: in its controller's unlock phase, the card and an unlocked ready vampire of theirs locked to lock any minion of the prey, a locked one a legal no-op (D14); its burn a titled vampire's action. Powerbase: Chicago: one of its two unlock abilities each unlock phase (6.6; amber); its blood taken by another Methuselah's vampire's action, directed at the card's controller, legal with no blood on it (D12, D14). Tests: `nocturn_theater_locks_a_prey_minion_for_its_lock_and_a_vampires`, `powerbase_chicago_banks_blood_and_another_vampire_takes_it`.

**116.65** The Oath: played by the vampire stolen from or whose blood a Taste of Vitae took — in combat as the gain lands (Slake the Thirst's windows), out of combat as a reaction after the stealing action resolves (68.2, §3.8; amber), a Taste of Vitae counting only this vampire's lost blood (amber); its Ⓓ action needs a counter from the giver already ("another"), adds the second, the card in the ash heap (D33, 74.2; amber); counters tracked per giver, 2 or more barring (25.1; amber); "one Oath each round" combat rounds only, once an action as a reaction (D4, D26; amber). Tests: `the_oath_answers_a_strike_steal`, `the_oath_answers_a_taste_of_vitae`, `the_oaths_action_adds_the_second_counter`, `the_oath_is_played_once_a_round`.

**116.66** The Oath's "A vampire with 2 oath counters from this vampire cannot block or enter combat with this vampire": no combat between the two in any way: the sworn vampire never blocks the giver; the giver may block the sworn vampire, the action then ending blocked with no combat; neither rushes the other; no third party's effect puts them in combat. LSJ 20090210 (https://usenet.krcg.org/t/vMreifpePC0/#m1): "entering combat is prohibited"; asked whether, A having put 2 oath counters on B, A blocking B ends the action with no combat: "Correct."; whether Taunt the Caged Beast or Blissful Agony can put them in combat: "No. See also Deflection and Minor Boon."; "Combat takes two. A enters combat with B === B enters combat with A." Built first as Reginald Moore's bar (58.6: the giver could still rush the sworn vampire), which reads "take actions to enter combat with them" and stands for him. Red, corrected (owner, 2026-11-12: "No combat possible in any way, see https://usenet.krcg.org/t/vMreifpePC0/#m1"). Rebuilt so. Tests: `a_sworn_vampire_cannot_block_its_giver`, `the_oaths_giver_blocks_its_sworn_vampire_with_no_combat`, `taunt_the_caged_beast_cannot_pit_a_sworn_pair`.

**116.67** Persona Non Grata: the caller's title its effective one (§3.15); any Methuselah's untitled or Anarch vampire, a baron included, torpor too (D11; Fanfare for Elysium's; amber); one bearing a copy not choosable, none and it is not played (LSJ 20100129, ANK 20191228, 99.1); "non-Anarch title" every title but baron (32.9); the bar masks title cards' plays and terms (a Praxis Seizure not offered, a justicar's terms skip it) and makes a title gained without a card a legal no-op as it resolves: Horatio Ballard's referendum offered, Tobias Smith's gain nothing, Valerius's steal leaves the prince titled (LSJ 20100129 "prohibited by card text", §4.6, 17.4; amber; fixed: Horatio was masked, Valerius stripped the prince); -1 stealth on its directed actions only; the card stays when its bearer later becomes titled or leaves the Anarchs (D13, 19.2; amber). Tests: `persona_non_grata_needs_a_choosable_vampire`, `persona_non_grata_slows_its_vampire_and_bars_non_anarch_titles`, `persona_non_grata_lets_its_anarch_become_a_baron_and_stays`, `persona_non_grata_makes_horatio_ballards_title_a_no_op`, `persona_non_grata_makes_valerius_steal_a_no_op`.

**116.68** Phased Motion Detector: automatic as an action modifier is played (before any cancel) by any vampire of the acting side, its mode played requiring [chi], [obf] or [obt] (LSJ 20100728, D18, D29; amber); once each action a copy, intercept for the rest of the action, needed or not, its bearer blocking or not (ANK 20230116, LSJ 20100329); an ally playing as a vampire does not count (D16; amber). Tests: `phased_motion_detector_gives_intercept_on_an_obfuscate_modifier`, `phased_motion_detector_ignores_an_ally_playing_as_a_vampire`.

**116.69** Pier 13, Port of Baltimore: once each of its controller's influence phases, its host equips a non-unique, non-location equipment from hand, requirements and cost as normal, not an action (6.6), a torpid host too (D25; amber); Synner-G and Vulture apply to it (PIB 20150105-2); locquipment rulings as the group (RTR 19960112, LSJ 20090324, LSJ 20080114); its equip mode read at the ability's index (fixed). Green unless noted. **116.106** Gracetius: "can wake" at the wake cards' moments: another Methuselah's action before resolution, the as-played window of a card played during it or of another's action card, and after combat and after resolution (LSJ 20091123: "Can be played or used after action resolution (e.g. to use a reaction card like Fast Reaction after combat)"), never in combat, never with no action (ANK 20210817, 96.4); an option, woken for the rest of the action, once (TOM 19951129); the Edge read as he wakes, its later loss keeping him woken (D13, D33; amber). The wake group's ledger n/a ("no V5 reaction usable after action resolution needs the vampire unlocked") was false: Touch of Pain, Burnt Offerings [AUS], Friend of Mine [obt] and The Oath are played after resolution, Fast Reaction after combat; the wake now opens there for the other Methuselahs (fixed: Eyes of Argus, Fast Reaction, Forced Awakening, On the Qui Vive, Wake with Evening's Freshness, Shadow Sentinel, Gracetius). Tests: `gracetius_wakes_with_the_edge_in_the_as_played_window_not_in_combat`, `gracetius_never_wakes_in_combat`, `gracetius_stays_woken_once_the_edge_is_lost`, `a_wake_is_played_after_combat_or_resolution`.

**116.129** Vidal Jarbeaux's "vampire trait (e.g., Black Hand)" (LSJ 20081120-1: "titles, "Red List", "Flight", non-requirements (eg. non-Red List) are vampire traits. Mortal, unlocked, ready, older, Striga, Maleficia, etc. are not"): Black Hand, Seraph (a Black Hand title), Red List and flight he meets, and a Sabbat path, slave and circle requirement too, once each game like the rest; "advanced" never, nor infernal ("non-infernal"), capacity, ready, unlocked. A "non-" requirement (non-titled, non-Red List, non-sterile: the ruling's own example) met too, once each (`MetKey::Untitled`, `NotRedList`, `NotSterile`). Built first with path, slave and circle never met. Red, corrected in part (owner, 2026-11-12: "Sabbat Paths, slave, circle, are vampiric traits. Ok for the rest"). Tests: `vidal_meets_a_sect_and_a_trait_requirement_once_each` (Seraph: Tattoo Signal; slave: Raw Recruit; a path: Augury of Doom), `vidal_meets_a_non_requirement_once` (The Red Question).

**116.130** Vidal Jarbeaux on a requirement listing alternatives ("prince or justicar", "titled", a clan or sect list), inside an either/or requirement too (Mozambique Allure's "a Laibon or non-titled vampire": an alternative he meets naturally spends nothing; else the key of each alternative one requirement short): which alternative he meets, and so spends, is offered to his player and chosen as the card is played (LSJ 20050128, PIB 20150306: "the controller chooses how it is met … He must choose a title when playing a card required a "titled" vampire (eg. Rubicon), but can only choose each title once"); a nondescript "X votes" (a vote-granting trait naming no title) may be spent to meet "titled", only once whatever X is. A requirement on the target of another card (Abombwe: "Put this card on a Laibon or on a vampire with Protean") is not a requirement for Vidal to play a card, so not his to meet (amber: his text, "to play any card"). Built first spending the first unspent alternative in printed order, no choice ("titled" spending primogen first). Red, corrected (owner, 2026-11-12: "The choice must be offered and made. Nondescript "X votes" can be used to meet the "titled" choice, but only once (disregarding the value of X)"). The choice is `Choice::MeetAs` (a new observation id, told to the training line). Tests: `vidal_chooses_which_alternative_he_meets`, `vidal_meets_a_title_once_each_without_its_votes`, `vidal_meets_an_either_or_requirement_once`, `vidal_meets_the_one_alternative_left_unasked`.

**116.131** William Thorbecke: once in his controller's discard phase, from torpor too, 2 blood to unlock a minion his controller controls (himself, a torpid one), a locked one only (D7, D25, D28; 48.7 as 89.18 extends it to another minion; amber). Test: `william_thorbecke_unlocks_a_minion_in_the_discard_phase`.

If another pre-range damaging effect is played then, all damages are prevented and mended together"). Green. Test: `alejandro_aguirre_hurts_both_combatants`.

**116.134** Petra Resonance (71.9) is directed at every other Methuselah: any of them may block it, the one across the table too (advanced rules group "Action directed at all methuselah": "Is a Directed Action against all (other) Methuselah"). Green. Test: `petra_resonance_is_blocked_from_across_the_table`.

**116.135** Punish (102.9, 103.4): blood moved or stolen to a vampire that cannot gain blood goes to the blood bank, the source losing it (ANK 20221019-2: "Any effect that gives a vampire more blood counts as gaining blood (e.g. moving blood with Heidelberg Castle, Germany, stealing blood with Theft of Vitae, etc.) and the blood is moved to the blood bank instead"). Green. Tests: `heidelberg_castle_moves_blood_to_a_punished_vampire_into_the_bank`, `theft_of_vitae_by_a_punished_vampire_sends_the_blood_to_the_bank`.

**116.136** A political action card's printed pool cost is paid as the action resolves (Veles' Hunt's 3, Brinksmanship's 1), with its blood (D20); the engine paid only the blood (fixed). Green. Tests: `veles_hunt_burns_an_ally_or_younger_untitled_vampire_of_the_prey`, `brinksmanship_pays_its_pool_and_enters_play`.

**116.137** Hiram "Hide" DeVries ("While Hiram is ready, actions to burn a location you control cost 1 additional blood"; KoT reprint only, added late in the round): a cost of the action as Daria's and Marcellus's (42.1, 62.7): payable with the action's own blood as announced, else not offered; paid first as it resolves; nothing if blocked. "You" is his controller (32.7); "ready" is not in torpor, so a locked Hiram still taxes (rulebook). Any minion's action, his own included, whose effect burns a location his controller controls: an action card's target (Arson, Rampage, Rewilding, Conceal, Goblinism, Smash and Grab, Loss), a vampire's granted burn (Themistocles, Diego Giovanni, Angelo, Annazir), a location's own "burn this location" action (Maabara); a locquipment is a location (17.4). An ally actor pays it in life, as every action surcharge (73.5). Amber (no ruling on Hiram). Test: `hiram_devries_taxes_burning_his_controllers_locations`.

**116.138** "Successfully inflicted" is damage neither dodged, nor prevented, nor ignored through an immunity, on every card printing it (Pulled Fangs, Disarm, Target Vitals, Backstep 116.9, Street Cred, Chave de Braço, Shoulder Drop, Smallpox Griet, Elie Sansaricq); plain "inflicts" counts prevented and immune damage alike, a printed "(even if prevented)" reminder text (Weighted Walking Stick, Improvised Flamethrower: "even if it is prevented or ignored", LSJ 20040802), except on an older card an LSJ ruling reads as "successfully inflicts", which keeps that ruling until a reprint updates its text: Blade Clot moves a counter only on damage successfully inflicted (LSJ 20080820; vtes-advanced-rules 1d1c191 §4.5.5: "Prevented damage, like damage a minion is immune to, is inflicted but not successfully inflicted … a ruling reading a card's 'inflicts' so stands until a reprint updates the text, e.g. {Twisting the Knife}, {Blade Clot}"; owner-rulings D, 2026-11-12). Immunity was built as a reduction to 0 for Carolina Vález and Helena, and a weapon strike on Mictlantecuhtli was never counted: Haymaker, the Walking Stick (a Ghoul Retainer's use of it too) and the Flamethrower missed it (fixed; a reduction, Saulot's, Nephandus's, stays not inflicted). Green. Tests: `backstep_gives_no_press_for_damage_ignored_through_immunity`, `blade_clot_moves_only_on_damage_successfully_inflicted`, `mictlantecuhtlis_immunity_still_counts_as_inflicted`, `carolinas_immunity_still_counts_as_inflicted`, `haymaker_counts_damage_on_an_immune_minion`, `wooden_stake_counts_damage_its_target_is_immune_to`.

## Batch 117 — What a fizzle pays (the engine board's "A fizzle pays no cost unless the cost is why it fizzled" line; owner, 2026-11-11; what it left open asked of the owner through Z-main and ruled, 2026-11-12: "1. b 2. a 3. a 4. a 5. a 6. (.9 a and 43.1 a") (Claude, 2026-11-12)

**117.1** The ruling (ANK 20210124, https://usenet.krcg.org/t/vekn-79001/#m2): a fizzle is a successful action whose effects cannot be applied because "1/ the target (or ALL the targets) has become illegal during the course of the action", "2/ things happened during the course of the action that prevent the action from happening" (a Bomb gone from its bearer) or "3/ the cost of the action cannot be paid anymore"; "If the action fizzles because of 1/ or 2/, the action is not paid. If the action fizzles because of 3/, the acting minion must pay as much as possible, and the action fizzles." Its one example of 3/: a vampire left with 1 blood as Dominate Kine (2 blood) resolves burns the 1. The rulings database's "cost, if any, is paid" on its fizzle groups (Action targeting locked minion, Action only usable by vampire in torpor, Action targeting vampire in torpor, Action targeting unlocked minion; Harass, Sleep of Reason) paraphrases it wrongly (owner, 2026-11-11). Corrected by it: 3.6, 17.4, 26.1, 28.7, 42.6, 48.8. The thread says nothing of pool; the owner (2026-11-12): a pool cost its Methuselah can no longer pay is paid as far as it goes too, their pool burned to 0, and they are ousted by the fizzle (read first as no pool burned). The oust is a cost's (38.5, 41.4): the predator's victory point and pool, the turn passing if it was theirs, the action over with its Methuselah (49.6); a Life Boon may still be played. (Tests a_leave_torpor_action_short_of_its_cost_pays_what_blood_is_left, a_recruit_the_methuselah_cannot_pay_at_resolution_fizzles_paying_their_last_pool.)

**117.2** A cost of several parts, one of them short: every part still payable is paid, the blood and the pool as far as they go (owner, 2026-11-12), an ally its life, the last burning it; a conviction named and still there is burned, of an X of convictions those still there. A reduction used once (Nergal's, LSJ 20091021-2) and the counters that would have paid stay unspent. (Test living_wood_staff_short_of_its_convictions_burns_those_left.)

**117.3** Cause 1/ or 2/ is read before anything is paid: an action that lost its target and could not pay either is not paid (owner, 2026-11-12). Read as 2/, the effect prevented, nothing paid (amber): the card or vampire granting the action gone from the acting minion (Talbot's Chainsaw, Codex of the Edenic Groundskeepers, Vast Wealth), a vampire out of the torpor its card is played from, an equipment no longer holdable (a second vehicle), a second copy of a power, a vampire who may no longer become Anarch, the Edge the action burns no longer its Methuselah's (Inside Dirt, Shatter the Gate), the copy to discard gone from the hand (Shattering Crescendo [mel]), the ash heap card or pair named gone (Cold Amber's Hold; Giovanni del Georgio's pair, which 59.1 calls the action's cost: read here as what the hunt needs, so the copy left stays), a Red List hunt whose marked minion is no longer ready. (Tests a_leave_torpor_action_fizzles_once_warsaw_station_raised_its_vampire, a_rescue_fizzles_unpaid_once_warsaw_station_raised_its_vampire, a_rescue_fizzles_unpaid_once_its_vampire_left_play, fleetness_superior_enters_combat_with_a_locked_minion_or_fizzles, mind_numb_and_make_the_misere_fizzle_on_a_target_no_longer_eligible.)

**117.4** The surcharges on an action are its cost (42.1): Daria's and Marcellus's blood, François "Warden" Loehr's and Safe Passage's, Blood Cult Awareness Network's, Lock's, Transcendent Laibon's, Chanjelin Ward's, Ambrosio Luis Monçada's and Alia's pool. None is paid by an action fizzling for 1/ or 2/; on 3/ they are paid first (42.6), each as far as it goes, then the action's own cost likewise (owner, 2026-11-12). (Tests daria_pays_a_blood_more_for_directed_actions, a_short_surcharge_is_paid_as_far_as_it_goes.)

**117.5** Rescue's announced split (48.8), a share short as it resolves: each payer pays its own share as far as it goes (owner, 2026-11-12). (Test rescue_cost_split_is_chosen_as_announced_and_paid_at_resolution.)

**117.6** Political actions (owner, 2026-11-12). A cost paid before the cause arose stays paid: the card's cost paid ahead of Yawp Court's combat (LSJ 20080201; 5.9). Terms with no legal choice when they are chosen (43.1) are cause 1/: the action is not paid, the terms' legality read as it resolves, before its cost. Only the first term can be read then: a later one left without a legal choice by the earlier choices is found once the cost is paid. (Tests parity_shift_with_nobody_richer_at_resolution_fizzles, a_political_action_without_legal_terms_does_not_pay_marcellus.)

## Batch 118 — An oust happens as the pool reaches 0 (the engine board's line; owner, 2026-11-11: 45.1, 45.4 and 101.3's waiting oust superseded; what it left open read here) (Claude, 2026-11-12)

**118.1** Pools running out one after the other in one effect (separate triggers, 118.2) are ousted one after the other, not together: each oust pays its predator the victory point and the 6 pool before the next pool is judged, so a Methuselah whose prey is ousted first may be kept in by that pool (the pool below 0 is kept until then, 45.8). Life Boon is asked for the first to run out alone, then for the next still out. Green (ANK 20220210; LSJ 20090208-2). (Test a_declined_life_boon_ousts_before_the_next_pool_runs_out.)

**118.2** Tension in the Ranks, minions burned or sent to torpor together (LSJ 20090208-2: "the acting Methuselah sets the order in wich the pool is burned"; advanced rules §2.4.2: separate triggers from one event resolve one at a time, the ousts they cause not simultaneous): the order is the acting minion's Methuselah's, outside an action the active Methuselah's, set whole before any pool is burned; Fame's and the vampires' own torpor burns (6.4) are ordered with it. It is asked only when two Methuselahs or more would burn their last pool: no other order shows. Green on who sets it outside an action (the rulebook's sequencing: the acting Methuselah first). (Tests the_acting_methuselah_orders_the_pool_burns_of_minions_lost_together, pool_burns_ordered_against_oneself_oust_one_after_the_other, fame_is_ordered_with_the_burns_of_minions_lost_together, justicar_retribution_burns_are_ordered_by_its_caller, the_becoming_vampires_burned_together_have_their_burns_ordered, minor_boon_declined_twice_lands_the_torpors_together.)

**118.3** One effect burning several Methuselahs' pool at once (a referendum's, Poison Pill's hand-back, 69.10) stays one simultaneous oust (rulebook: a prey ousted as its predator is pays no 6 pool); only separate triggers are ordered (118.2). Green.

**118.4** The engine's limit, for the record: an effect runs to its end without suspending, so with a Life Boon held the rest of the effect that ran the pool out (no decision in it) is applied before the Boon is asked, the Methuselah still seated; nothing is asked or played in between (45.4), and later pools running out wait their turn (118.1). Exact would take replaying the step from a snapshot: not built. Amber.

## Batch 119 — A card put in play by another card is played (the engine board's line; owner, 2026-11-11: the default reading, 40.10, 44.4 and 77.3 corrected; what it left open read here) (Claude, 2026-11-12)

**119.1** A card another card brings into play, "put in play" included, has been played, though not in the normal fashion (owner, 2026-11-11, on advanced rules §1.8.2 and §1.6.3; ANK 20201229, RTR 20001020): it has no as-played window, so it is never canceled as played, and the card that brought it in played it, not a minion: an effect keyed to a minion playing a card does not see it (Concealed Weapon's weapon "is not played by the minion", Louhi cannot cancel it). "Put in play" still bypasses requirements and cost; "equip", "recruit" and "employ" keep them (41.13); "not recruited" stands (40.10, 44.4, 113.123: able to act the turn it enters). The engine: such a card never goes through the play of a card from hand (`announce_play`), so no window opens and none of a minion's play triggers runs (Veil of Darkness's first Discipline card, Andrew Stuart's reveal, Ira Rivers's draw, Marthe Dizier's cards played, the limits a minion's plays count against). A card played "as if from your hand" (Spiritual Guidance, The Erciyes Fragments) is a normal play, window and triggers included (digest §7.1; LSJ 20060106). Green. (Tests summon_history_card_opens_no_as_played_window, piper_employs_by_the_anarchs_disciplines_and_is_no_action, a_retainer_piper_employs_is_not_played_by_the_anarch, asguresh_cannot_cancel_the_weapon_a_disguised_weapon_equips.)

**119.2** What does see it: a limit on the card itself, and what the card costs the minion paying it. "Only one Flaming Candle can be played or equipped in a game" counts a Candle Summon History puts in play (it has been played, 119.1): the hand's is not equipped after (the engine counted the equips only, fixed; amber: the card's ruling speaks only of the other direction). That other direction is ruled: a Candle played or equipped already, an effect that is no equip action may still put another in play (LSJ 20060209), so Summon History finds one whatever was played. A cost keyed to the minion paying follows the card whoever plays it: the reductions (41.13) and the surcharges, Darlene Killian's blood on Piper's ally (113.13) and on The Summoning [PRE]'s recruit (116.76), which the engine did not charge, fixed; a card put in play pays nothing. Green but the amber named. (Tests summon_history_puts_a_flaming_candle_in_play_whatever_was_played, darlene_pays_a_blood_more_for_pipers_ally, the_summoning_charges_darlenes_surcharge.)

**119.3** An effect keyed to a Methuselah playing a card (Troglodytia's "after any Methuselah plays a master card, you may look at that Methuselah's hand", Sascha Vykos's draw after another Methuselah plays a master card) is read as the minion's: the card that brought it in played it, so a Discipline master Childe of the Revolution or Lord Ashton puts on a vampire, an Aye or Orun Umdava or Nkishi places, shows no hand and draws her nothing; no card of the pool brings in a Gehenna card (111.1) or a boon (115.7) this way. A retrieval worded on "a card played" reaches only normal plays (advanced rules §1.11.1; Marthe Dizier). Amber.

**119.4** Played by no card: the Discipline master a diablerist of an older vampire takes is put on by the diablerie rule (81.1), and Absimiliard's Army's ghoul is the library's top card as a nameless, textless stand-in (40.10; LSJ 20040531): neither is a card played, in any fashion. Amber.

## Batch 120 — Flight among the "any Discipline" choices (the cards board's line, found in round 29's fuzz review; owner, 2026-11-13) (Claude, 2026-11-13)

**120.1** Flight is a Trait, not a Discipline (rulebook 2024: "Flight: This Trait allows a minion to play or use cards requiring Flight"): a card choosing "a Discipline" or "any Discipline" (Agent of Power, Spontaneous Power, Sanguine Instruction) never offers it; the choices are the Disciplines some vampire of the crypt prints at basic level (`state::disciplines`), Flight printed only at superior. Red, confirmed (owner, 2026-11-13: "Ok for your recommendation for flight"). (Test agent_of_power_never_offers_flight.)

## Batch 121 — Round 30: Lords of the Night, 172 cards (120 library cards, 52 vampires) (the cards board; owner, 2026-11-09: the set-group lines; red, answered by the owner 2026-11-14: confirmed as built 121.37, 121.42, 121.59, 121.75, 121.81, 121.88, 121.133, 121.150, 121.154, 121.175, 121.180; corrected 121.3, 121.19, 121.20, 121.108, 121.127, 121.129, 121.131, 121.146 (with 55.5), 121.161; 121.121 pending a single parametric decision) (Claude, 2026-11-14)

Graded per the add-cards skill: 672 readings, two blind reads diffed against the build, every citation checked in its source; 480 green, 169 amber, 23 red (20 asked; Will-o'-the-Wisp's Secure Haven vampire fixed per 35.1, 121.174; The Eternals of Sirius's first branch made a legal no-op per D14, 121.39; Monty Coven's priscus settled by 90.8, 121.101), and one more asked from the last fix pass (Meditative Grove canceling an action card, 121.180). Built wrong and fixed in the round: a unique vampire one controls raised from an ash heap (The Eternal Mask, Blessed Resilience, 121.38), Edged Illusion [CHI] with no target left (121.35), Bloodlust's frenzy played on its picks (121.14), Nightmare Curse's copies (121.105), Poison the Well of Life [QUI]'s direction (121.116), Spell of Life's pick of five (121.148), Burden the Mind's toll (121.15, 121.16), Lady Constancia beside Visit from the Capuchin (121.84), Porphyrion's optional search (121.117), a continuation after torpor (Go-getter, Shadow Boxing, 121.57), Meat Hook's tear (121.96), Revelation of Despair's blocker (121.128), Fillip's wake (121.48), Zapaderin (121.176), Powerbase: Zürich's self-contest (121.119). Found on the way and implemented: Alan Sovereign, base and advanced, and Wall Street Night's investment clause (121.1, 121.171). Fixed in the round: "this vampire" met by an ally playing the card as a vampire (121.179, per 73.6).

**121.1** Alan Sovereign (base: "When you play an investment card, add an additional counter to it from the blood bank"; found on the way, implemented this round): his controller's investment card entering play gets the counter while he is in play (amber; text, D11). Investment cards are now the generated `Label::Investment` (the "investment" type line: Secret Horde, Swiss Cut), the `Effect::Investment` marker gone. Alan Sovereign (ADV) ("While Alan is ready, you may pay some or all of the pool cost of equipping from any investment cards you control. [MERGED] During your master phase, if Alan is ready, you may move a counter from any investment card to your pool"): the payment is a cost reduction announced with the equipment, as Ravnos Cache's counters (RTR 19960112), pool only, never blood, on any equip by any minion his player controls, none while he is in torpor ("ready"), no lock (amber; LSJ 20080125 names Alan as "another source"); "Paying from another source (eg. Alan Sovereign (ADV)) does not reduce the cost as seen by Concealed Weapon": with 5 counters, the AK-47 (5 pool) is still not offered (LSJ 20080125); the [MERGED] move takes from any Methuselah's investment card (the first clause says "you control", this one does not), once each master phase (6.6), not a master phase action, Alan ready (amber: the wording's contrast, no ruling); an investment card emptied by another card's effect (Alan's payment or move, Wall Street Night) burns at once by its own text (Secret Horde "Burn this card when it has no counters", Swiss Cut "Burn this card if it has no blood"). Green unless noted. Tests: `alan_sovereign_adds_a_counter_to_his_players_investments`, `alan_sovereign_advanced_pays_an_equip_from_his_investments`, `alan_sovereign_merged_takes_a_counter_from_any_investment`, `wall_street_night_takes_a_counter_from_an_investment_as_an_action`.

**121.2** Ambulance: the continuation needs a combat from a block, the acting minion's own (no slave took it), the minion ready; none after a block canceled with no combat (Shadow Boxing's requirements, ANK 20200114, 17.3; amber); none once the acting minion went to torpor during the action, even brought back ("the action ends and cannot be continued (eg. with Ambulance)", Warsaw Station, ANK 20220218, LSJ 20090325-2; amber; the same for Go-getter and Shadow Boxing, 121.57); continuing keeps the modifiers, reactions and declines, the action card back to limbo (RTR 19941109, ANK 20190116, LSJ 20070808-1, 3.4); "If the action is blocked again, burn this card" mandatory, at the block, before any other effect (the "Triggered by block" group, ANK 20220116); "you may lock" optional, the lock its limit (16.1); its burn action fizzles without the Ambulance on its actor (RTR 19960221, 76.9); one vehicle (digest §3.17). Green unless noted. Tests: `ambulance_continues_a_blocked_action_keeping_the_declines`, `ambulance_cannot_continue_an_action_its_minion_lost_in_torpor`, `ambulance_continuation_keeps_the_stealth_played`, `ambulance_burns_as_the_continued_action_is_blocked_again`, `ambulance_action_fizzles_without_the_ambulance`, `ambulance_is_a_vehicle`.

**121.3** Ambulance: its "attempt to burn an incapacitated imbued as a +1 stealth Ⓓ action" counted as the rulebook's action. Owner (2026-11-14): "No, it is its own action, not the rulebook action": the imbued is burned (its cards with it, as any burn), the actor takes none of its equipment and no other imbued may burn a conviction to damage the actor (the rulebook's burn-imbued riders, 87.12, do not apply); ECTU Operative's shape (`ActionKind::Ability`, `Effect::BurnTargetMinion`), still fizzling without the Ambulance (121.2). Test: `ambulance_burns_an_incapacitated_imbued_as_its_own_action`.

**121.4** Amria: [chi] a maneuver only from close range, to long (116.9, Backstep's reading); [CHI] a dodge with a press usable only to end combat, this round only (TOM 19960521). Green. Tests: `amria_maneuvers_to_long_range_only`, `amria_superior_dodges_with_an_ending_press_this_round`.

**121.5** Apparition: playable any time in combat, before any damage (LSJ 20010315); with fewer points pending all are prevented, each round's allowance fresh, none carried to the next (RTR 20041202, ANK 20200318, LSJ 20001114); two copies add each round (advanced rules §1.15: "Numeric bonuses from multiple copies add"); "this vampire" is met by an ally only playing the card as a vampire through a granted Discipline (73.6, 121.179; graded built wrong against D16, not changed: an ally with no grant cannot play it); its Blood of Acid mention n/a (Blood of Acid not in V5, Soak's ledger row). Green. Tests: `apparition_is_played_before_damage_and_prevents_each_round`, `apparition_does_not_carry_unused_prevention`, `apparition_two_copies_add_each_round`.

**121.6** Army of Apparitions: [chi] only in a political action's polling step, never on a referendum passing automatically (PIB 20150105, LSJ 19980107: "Cannot be used during a referendum that is automatically passing"); usable by a locked vampire, both modes (the card's text); a reaction, so another Methuselah's vampire, a torpid one not (it casts no votes; digest §3.8, D25); [CHI] "younger" than the reacting vampire, an acting ally always qualifying (63.3, Confusion of the Eye's same words; D30; amber); [CHI] "directed at a minion you control": the action targets one of the reacting player's minions, torpid too, a bleed of the Methuselah not (D12, D28; amber); [CHI] the 2 blood an effect, not reduced, needed to play (ANK 20210226: "The card cannot be played if the minion cannot afford to burn the blood"; D20); [CHI] not while a block is attempted nor after one succeeded (ANK 20260316, LSJ 20041022); [CHI]'s failed action reached resolution (no repeat), was not blocked, after-action cards still usable (ANK 20211015, LSJ 20070219, ANK 20221205). Green unless noted. **121.7** Arnulf Jormungandrsson: an attempt to block declared counts, failed or successful, a forced one too (Special Report, Telepathic Tracking: the bar fires at every attempt site; amber, the text "attempts to block"); the bar runs from the attempt to the action's end, modifiers played before it stay (the text; Blessing of Chaos's same wording, LSJ 20080818); it binds the acting minion only, the acting Methuselah's other minions free (D4; amber); it outlives Arnulf burned or sent to torpor during the action (D33, 74.2; amber); a dual card the acting minion plays is a modifier, barred (D18). "Cards requiring X" is the mode played: on a card of either Discipline (non-combo, Dominant Personality [pot] or [pre]) the bar applies only when the acting minion has no unbarred Discipline to play it with; a combo or "more than one Discipline" card stays barred on any barred tag (D29, LSJ 20011204-3: "When played, a multi-Disciplines card counts as requiring the Discipline(s) being used", 113.82; amber; fixed). Green unless noted. Tests: `arnulf_bars_the_acting_minions_dominate_modifiers_after_his_attempt`, `arnulf_bars_by_the_mode_played_and_outlives_himself`, `arnulf_bar_fires_on_a_forced_attempt`.

**121.8** Baal's Bloody Talons: played after strikes are chosen, before resolution, on its player's own melee weapon strike (RTR 19990105, RTR 19960112, LSJ 20020425); "this round" every strike of that weapon this round, an additional one too, the +1 nothing on a strike dealing no damage (RTR 19960221); the weapon burned as the round ends, even if the strike was dodged or the round ended early, not if the weapon is out of play then (LSJ 20100211-2, advanced rules §4.6.3); Dagger's ranged strike is a melee weapon's strike (amber: the ruling's own example names Dagger). **121.9** Bakr: his rush needs another Methuselah's minion (digest §3.4, 17.3); the 1 blood is the action's cost, payable as the action is announced (not offered otherwise), paid at resolution, unpaid if blocked (D20, 3.6, 42.1); +1 stealth from the declaration (Hel-Blá's shape). A granted action's printed blood was checked at announcement only when a toll applied, so a vampire short of it was offered the action (Saankaláxt, Hel-Blá, Bakr, Jayakumar): now always (42.1; fixed). Green. Tests: `bakr_rushes_for_a_blood_at_plus_one_stealth`, `jayakumar_steals_equipment_for_two_blood`.

Green unless noted. **121.11** Blithe Acceptance: the +1 stealth is a standing bonus from resolution on, not this action's, and the card is no "+1 stealth action" (D21); "only 1": not playable by a vampire bearing one (99.1); "enters combat" is every combat, as blocker, acting or rushed, before the first round (rulebook); at [OBF] 1 blood or the card, its controller's choice, the card burned with no blood, asked again in every new combat (Psyche!, a second action) (ANK 20230527, ANK 20220503). **121.12** Blood Awakening: the card's type follows who plays it, [qui] a reaction of the attempting minion, [QUI] a modifier of the acting one (D18, 52.2); [qui]'s intercept only while intercept is needed (D15, 3.2); [QUI]'s "younger vampire" is younger than the acting vampire by capacity, an ally attempting never qualifying (D30). Green. Tests: `blood_awakening_gives_intercept_as_a_reaction`, `blood_awakening_fails_a_younger_vampires_attempt`.

**121.13** Blood Trade (met in the round): its boon mask reads every boon putting itself on a minion (Minor Boon), not only one put in play: such a boon is not played on its vampire (115.7; fixed). Green. Test: `blood_trade_bars_a_boon_put_on_a_vampire`.

**121.14** Bloodlust: X and the minions are chosen as played, any Methuselah's, torpid ones too; X = 0 at [pre] a legal no-op (D1, D11, D14, D28); a pick of another Methuselah's minion directs the action at them, who may block (D12, D19; amber); the Frenzy card is played on each chosen minion, not on its actor: a frenzy-immune minion is not offered as a pick, a frenzy-immune actor may play it choosing others, and Predator's Transformation [abo], Aye and Meditative Grove cancel it for a pick (rules-qa 28.11, digest §3.19, LSJ 20051113; the engine read it as played on the actor, fixed); the rush is a granted Ⓓ action of a card in play, once a turn a copy, needing another Methuselah's ready minion (32.1, 17.3); "an optional press each combat" is every combat of a chosen minion while the card is in play, whoever acts, two copies giving two presses (text, D11; two copies amber: no ruling); burned at the end of the turn. **121.15** Burden the Mind: "this minion's turn" is its controller's turn (rulebook); put on any Methuselah's minion, an ally or a torpid one too (D11); its burn is a Ⓓ action of the bearer, directed at the card's player when another's (D5, D12, 20.1), the minion unlocked as it resolves. The toll is paid in pool by the user of the effect, as a cost of the card or ability (amber; Secure Haven, TOM 19951208-1: "All Methuselahs, including the target vampire's controller, must pay one extra pool when playing Master cards on the target minion"; D3); two copies add their tolls (amber; LSJ 19980225: "The cost is cumulative if they are multiple copies in play"). Green unless noted. Tests: `burden_the_mind_taxes_a_wake_out_of_turn`, `burden_the_mind_is_burned_by_its_minions_action_which_unlocks_it`, `burden_the_mind_two_copies_add_and_an_unpayable_toll_is_not_offered`.

**121.16** Burden the Mind, which effects: every effect that unlocks or wakes its minion, or lets it block as if unlocked, out of its turn costs its user 1 pool more per copy, a group unlock and a vampire's printed self-unlock included (amber, regraded from red: the card's text, "using an effect to unlock this minion", reaches every such effect). The engine classifies every effect exhaustively (the actor, the target, the host, the acting minion, the blocker, the picks), charges the play or ability using it and Baba Yaga's unlock, and never offers it unpayable: a card whose pool cost plus tolls exceeds its user's pool is masked; an ability's toll paying the last pool ousts (D3, 101.3, Batch 118). It taxed only five effect kinds (wake and unlock of the actor, the target, the host), Horrific Countenance's blocker, Strix, Loving Agony, the burn-blood-to-unlock hosts, Baba Yaga, Devil-Channel: Feet and Helena's "as if unlocked" free, and an ability's toll left its payer at 0 unousted (fixed). Open corners (amber): an optional unlock chosen later (Loving Agony [VAL], Paths in Two Worlds, Fiendish Tongue, Revelation of the Serpent [PRE][PRO]) pays when the card is played; a card unlocking a target chosen later is charged but not masked; a group unlock reaching Burden's minion has no pool card (untested). Tests: `burden_the_mind_taxes_a_wake_out_of_turn`, `burden_the_mind_taxes_an_unlock_of_the_blocker`, `burden_the_mind_toll_on_an_ability_can_oust`, `burden_the_mind_two_copies_add_and_an_unpayable_toll_is_not_offered`.

**121.17** Chair of Hades: an imbued is a mortal ally, a Ghouled ally a ghoul, "mortal", "ghoul" and "wraith" read by subtype (113.129, digest §3.6); at [NEC] "non-wraith" qualifies both the ally and the retainer, the vampire's capacity 2 or less read live, torpor included (D13, D30); removed from the game, not burned: no burn rider runs, the cards on the removed minion burn (rulebook); directed at the target's controller, undirected on one's own minion (D12, 17.4). Green. Tests: `chair_of_hades_removes_a_mortal_ally_or_a_ghoul_retainer`, `chair_of_hades_superior_removes_a_small_vampire_not_a_wraith`.

**121.18** Charigger, The Axe: "your prey" is Charigger's controller's prey, read as the burn happens (D11, D13); any minion that prey controls (vampire, ally, imbued, torpid too; a retainer is no minion), whatever burns it (digest §3.6); the replacement takes effect before anything keyed to the burn, no Soul Gem of Etrius draw, no Saatet-ta shuffle (LSJ 20071003-2: "His replacement effect triggers before effects depending on the minion burning (eg. Soul Gem of Etrius)"; advanced rules §5.4.3); the soul counter goes on Charigger, "this Giovanni" the current employer, +1 bleed per counter read live (9.1, D13); "+1 stealth when hunting" on every hunt, no block attempt needed (D15, D17). Green. Tests: `charigger_leaves_other_methuselahs_minions_burned`, `charigger_removes_the_preys_burning_minion_and_feeds_its_giovanni`, `charigger_gives_stealth_on_hunts_only`.

**121.19** Charigger, The Axe: beside another standing burn replacement. Owner (2026-11-14): not a fixed order: the acting Methuselah, or the one whose turn it is outside an action, chooses which replacement applies when two or more apply (the burning minion's own, Byzar's; Book of Going Forth by Night's ally steal; Charigger's), asked only then (advanced rules §2.4.1: "The acting Methuselah decides the order of the effects available in a window"; §5.4.3), the burn waiting for the answer; at a diablerie, the chosen one decides it (121.20). Test: `charigger_or_the_burning_minions_own_replacement_as_the_acting_methuselah_chooses`.

**121.20** Charigger, The Axe: at a diablerie of the prey's vampire. Owner (2026-11-14): the diablerie is unsuccessful, as with Byzar (LSJ 20100228: "His ability will make a diablerie unsuccessful (blood and equipment stay on him)"; 113.9: "the diablerist gets nothing, no blood hunt"): no blood, no equipment, no Discipline, no blood hunt; the victim is removed from the game with its cards, Charigger +1 soul counter; Sacrificial Lamb's victim likewise (no blood, no equipment). Test: `charigger_makes_a_diablerie_of_the_preys_vampire_unsuccessful`.

**121.21** Chavi Oraczko: her stealth once an action, used only when needed (6.6, 79.1, 3.2); +1 hand size while ready, none in torpor (the text, D25). Green. Test: `chavi_buys_stealth_once_and_widens_the_hand`.

**121.22** Chill of Oblivion: any Methuselah's ready vampire, another's directed (D11, D12, 20.1); its bearer burns it any time in its combat, a legal no-op allowed, aggravated damage taken as normal to the end of that combat (text); "only one": not put on a vampire with one (99.1); at [NEC] the acting vampire, not the bearer, unlocks as this turn's discard phase begins, and not after a fizzle (D10, 117.1; amber). Green unless noted. Tests: `chill_of_oblivion_turns_aggravated_damage_normal_for_the_combat`, `chill_of_oblivion_superior_unlocks_the_actor_in_the_discard_phase`, `chill_of_oblivion_superior_fizzled_unlocks_nothing`.

**121.23** Chimerstry, Necromancy, Quietus, Serpentis: on any Methuselah's vampire, +1 level and +1 capacity, not on a vampire with it at superior (111.39, 113.108; the second reader's "controlled vampire" not reopened). Serpentis makes Donnybrook's [ser] mode and Murat's text reachable (76.23 corrected). Green. Tests: `discipline_cards_raise_level_and_capacity`, `donnybrook_serpentis_steals_from_a_minion_with_less_than_two`.

**121.24** Cobra Fangs: "successfully inflicted" is damage neither dodged, prevented nor ignored through an immunity (116.138); the struck ally burned as the action ends (after resolution), or as the combat ends with no action, only if still in play, automatically, with no order among the striker's other after-resolution effects (amber: 23.3 has the player order such effects; no pool interaction found where the order matters; D33); [SER] the vampire goes to torpor at its controller's next unlock phase, an unlock due ordered with the others and read as it resolves (not ready then, nothing), the card to the ash heap either way (amber; 6.1, D13); "their" is the vampire's controller, read live (D5, D13); two landing are two dues and one torpor (advanced rules §1.15: "Each copy triggers and resolves on its own"; 6.1). Green unless noted. Tests: `cobra_fangs_spares_an_ally_when_prevented`, `cobra_fangs_burns_a_struck_ally_after_resolution`, `cobra_fangs_superior_sends_a_struck_vampire_to_torpor_at_its_unlock`, `cobra_fangs_superior_two_landing_give_one_torpor`.

**121.25** Command the Legion: X-1 minions chosen as played, any Methuselah's, X derived from the picks (exactly X-1), X = 1 a legal no-op (D1, D11, D14); "younger" than the acting vampire by capacity, equal excluded, read live, any ally qualifying (26.8, 63.4, D30); at [DOM] one pick may be a same-age or older vampire (text); directed at each other Methuselah whose minion is picked (D12, D19; RTR 20081119 on its twin, Condemn the Sins of the Father; amber); torpid and locked minions may be picked, locking them a no-op, a pick no longer qualifying at resolution skipped, all gone: the action void and unpaid (D28, 117.1, 117.3; amber). **121.26** Community Justice: "since your last turn" counts the current turn, not the last (LSJ 20020429); the victim's clan as it was diablerized, the actor's clan read live, a Laibon no Independent (D13, D31; amber); it requires an Independent or Anarch vampire, re-checked as it resolves (text, D13). Green unless noted. Test: `community_justice_burns_a_diablerist_of_the_actors_clan`.

**121.27** Compel the Spirit: the card is named as played among its player's ally and retainer cards burned from play since their last turn; gone from the ash heap by resolution, the action is void and unpaid (RTR 19980928, LSJ 19980929, LSJ 20020429, 117.3); never a card that represented another (Khazar's Diary's wraith, a Spell of Life mummy, Jake Washington): only cards burned as themselves count (LSJ 20071014, LSJ 20020115, LSJ 20030128); at [NEC] the ally is a new minion: its inferior version, its starting life, X zero, and it may act, even what its old incarnation did (LSJ 20010627, LSJ 20100204, the rulebook's recruit ally); a retainer goes on the acting minion at its starting life (text); Reanimated Corpse with X zero has no pathos and burns at once (LSJ 20071015); undirected, its player's own ash heap (17.2). **121.28** Condemn the Sins of the Father: the clan is chosen as played, only clans with a younger vampire in play offered: the card's only object is "all younger vampires of the chosen clan" (D1, D14, advanced rules §1.6.5: "A card whose text names the object it acts on cannot be played when no such object exists"); directed at every Methuselah controlling a younger vampire of the clan, one's own undirected (RTR 20081119, ANK 20180917); the set read live as it resolves (one become older spared, one become younger added), torpid ones included; at [QUI] 1 blood burned from each, none from an empty one (D13, D20, D28). **121.29** The Crocodile Temple: not used by a Methuselah the action ousted (ANK 20180326: "Cannot be used if the action ousted you."); "successful" includes a fizzled action (amber; ANK 20220218: "can be used if the action 'fizzles'"); 1 environmental damage to the acting minion, an ally too, preventable; once per action, by its lock (D8); "directed at you (or a card you control)" includes an action at a card its controller controls (D12). **121.30** Divine Image: both levels played as the action is announced ("As above" inherits it, digest §4.5); [SER]'s +1 bleed is not limited, a limited bleed modifier playable before or after it (LSJ 20100218, RTR 20180511-2); the −1 stealth applies with no need, below 0 too (D15, D24); "can prevent 1 damage each combat" is one fresh prevention in every combat of the action, a block's, a resolution's or a Psyche! one, in any round of it (30.6 Beast Meld's pool; LSJ 20021213: "The new combat is still part of the current action"; amber); it covers any damage to this vampire in combat, environmental too, none outside combat (30.6; amber). **121.31** Dmitri Borodin: prevents all the damage of one opposing strike, aggravated too, never other damage (digest §3.20, 74.1); not offered without strike damage pending, once each combat (LSJ 20001114, D32); his stealth once an action (79.1). **121.32** Dream World: [pre] is a bleed at +1 bleed, [PRE] a +1 stealth undirected action that is no bleed (digest §3.3); "only one at superior each turn" counts and bars [PRE] plays only (D4, D27); the bonus read live on its player's minions, any blocked bleed action of theirs ending it (D13; amber). **121.33** Durga Syn: her steal strike takes any equipment, not only weapons (the text); close range; with first strike the stolen weapon's strike resolves no more (21.1, 70.11); as often as paid, no "once" (D7); a unique copy her player controls is no target, nor for Fast Hands' and Spider's steal (`Effect::takes_card` now counts the steal strike; digest §3.7, 116.6; fixed); the equipment is named with the strike (D1; 70.11: "the weapon named with the strike moves to the striker as it lands"; amber: the rulebook's "Destroy Equipment" says who chooses, not when); with no opposing equipment the strike is not offered ("Blessing of Durga Syn: Cannot be used if there is no equipment to steal", RTR 19980928; amber); equipment she cannot hold (a second vehicle) stays (70.11; amber); "does not lock for blocking an ally or a vampire with capacity 4 or less" reads the acting minion's kind and capacity in play as the block succeeds (D30, Reverend Adams' read site; amber). **121.34** Ecstasy: "a bleed against you" is the bleed's current target (advanced rules §3.7.1.3: "the new target is the target for every purpose"); [SER] "resolved for 0": the bleed reached its resolution at 0, a blocked, failed or fizzled bleed burning nothing (the card's text, 24.1; amber); the condition read live as the after-resolution window opens (D13); the burn ordered by the acting Methuselah among the after-resolution effects, no pass (ANK 20180206: "The acting player decides the order of (and can play other) 'after resolving the action' effects"; 23.3); "blood or life": an ally burns life (digest §3.14); two [SER] by two vampires each burn 1 (D4, D26; amber); redirected after it, the -1 stays with the bleed and [SER] reads its resolution, whatever its target (116.52; amber). **121.35** Edged Illusion: [chi] any ready minion, directed at its controller, locked and dealt 1 unpreventable damage (D12); [CHI] any Methuselah, oneself included (undirected then): each ready minion the actor may target takes 1, an untargetable one (Secure Haven) or an immune one nothing, none locked (LSJ 20071024, RTR 20080808, advanced rules §4.5.5); the damage all at once, a Dual Form set going to torpor together keeping one (LSJ 20050805, 96.1); no ready minion of the chosen Methuselah left that the actor may target at resolution: the action fizzles, unpaid (117.1 cause 1/, 117.3; LSJ 20071024: each ready minion a target; amber; the engine paid the blood, fixed). **121.36** Enticement: directed at the prey, not a bleed (LSJ 20010807); the Edge lost during the action, no pool is burned and the bleed bar still applies (LSJ 20100527, advanced rules §6.5); the bar from resolution on, every bleed of its player's minions (the rulebook's, a card's, a granted one) to the end of the turn (text). **121.37** Enticement: played by a Methuselah who does not hold the Edge. Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: not offered without the Edge, the Edge being the object the card acts on (advanced rules §1.6.5, D14), as Inside Dirt (77.13) and Shatter the Gate (113.139, "the Edge as declared"); against it, LSJ 20100527 ("If the edge is lost during the action ..., the prey takes no damage but you still cannot bleed anymore this turn") shows the card still acts without the Edge, and the second reader read it a legal play, the bar alone applying. Test: `enticement_is_not_offered_without_the_edge`.

**121.38** The Eternal Mask: at [SER] the ash heap, the unique vampire, then X (0 to the actor's blood less the cost) chosen as played, what is left moved (D1, 38.1, LSJ 20100216); undirected, any heap; not playable with no unique vampire in any ash heap (LSJ 20010627, ANK 20180202, ANK 20200420-3); the vampire is moved, not played: controlled by the player, owned by its heap's Methuselah; a unique vampire whose copy the player already controls is not offered, one another Methuselah controls is offered and contested (rulebook "Contested Cards": "you cannot voluntarily contest cards with yourself"; 7.1; the contest amber, the engine offered one's own copy and contested neither, fixed; Blessed Resilience's raise likewise); the bearer acts, blocks, reacts and votes no more, still unlocks and can be rushed (text); its burn is its controller's, in any Methuselah's unlock phase, only while the same incarnation is in play (LSJ 20070928-1, LSJ 20040726, ANK 20191031); the Mask burned otherwise (its bearer burned), the vampire stays (LSJ 20071206, LSJ 20090725). **121.39** The Eternals of Sirius: playable at 4 pool, ending at 5 (LSJ 20020620, LSJ 20080612: "Pay the cost at the same time you gain pool from it, you do not get ousted in between (ie. can be played when at 4 pool)."), not at 3 (ANK 20210714-2); the branch chosen as played (D1); the first branch playable with no ready Ministry, a legal no-op: it pays 4 pool and gains 5 only if a ready Ministry is controlled, read as it resolves (amber; D14, 17.4: the condition is the gain's, not the play's; built masked, graded red, fixed); the second branch masked without a target; "capacity 9 or more" printed, in the uncontrolled region (D30, 60.18); canceled as played by Sudden Reversal ("Cancel a master card as it is played by another Methuselah, and its cost is not paid") at 4 pool: nothing paid, nothing gained, not ousted (the canceling card's text; the readings' "cost paid, ousted" corrected). Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: every opposing strike at the vampire has no effect on it or its non-retainer cards this round, and the retainers' damage too, so an opponent with no retainer striking with a hand strike for 2 deals nothing and Heroic Might's strike burns none of its Baseball Bat ("cannot strike" is the price of a full strike shield; the second reader read retainers' strikes and retainers' damage only, D4 narrow, no ruling). **121.50** Forearm Block: up to 2 damage of the next opposing hand strike this round, fewer all (RTR 20041202); it waits for a hand strike this round (a weapon strike first: kept for an additional hand strike) and is lost with the round (ANK 20200318, LSJ 20001114); set as the strike is chosen, so a first-strike opposing hand strike card (Quick Jab) resolving before it is covered, "including any currently-resolving" (amber, text); usable at any range (rulebook: "a defensive strike such as dodge or combat ends"); an opposing dodge undoes neither the shield nor the first strike (amber, advanced rules §4.6.1); first strike on the initial strike of the next round (text); hand strike cards (Cobra Fangs) are hand strikes. Green unless noted. Tests: `forearm_block_prevents_two_of_the_next_hand_strike`, `forearm_block_waits_for_the_next_hand_strike_this_round`, `forearm_block_covers_a_first_strike_hand_strike_card_at_any_range`, `forearm_block_first_strike_survives_a_dodge`, `forearm_block_gives_first_strike_next_round`.

**121.51** Forger's Hammer: "Only usable when an action to equip … is successful" is played by the acting minion once blocks are declined, before resolution pays the weapon's cost, so [POT]'s reduction applies (36.1: reductions "on resolution (just before the cost is paid)"; the would-be-successful window opens only for a bleed and a political action, 10.1; amber); the card itself goes from the ash heap onto the weapon as it enters play, nothing if it has left the ash heap by then (D21, 47.1, 111.16 Ghouled, ANK 20220805; amber); a fizzle or a swap that puts no weapon in play from hand leaves the Hammer in the ash heap (111.16; amber); only a non-unique melee weapon from the equipping minion's hand, never one taken from another minion, never a gun (the card text, D5); +1 damage on each strike with that weapon whoever bears it, in the strike's own damage kind, a strike dealing none still none (RTR 19970630, TOM 19960225: "Additional damage inherits all of the properties of the base damage"; RTR 19960221); "inflicts +1 damage" an amount, no "inflicted" trigger (116.44, 116.138 not engaged). Green unless noted. Tests: `forgers_hammer_goes_on_the_equipped_weapon`, `forgers_hammer_stays_in_the_ash_heap_with_no_weapon_from_hand`, `forgers_hammer_needs_a_non_unique_melee_weapon_from_hand`, `forgers_hammer_is_not_played_on_a_gun`, `forgers_hammer_follows_its_weapon_and_inherits_its_kind`.

**121.52** Foul Blood: "would successfully hunt" is an unblocked hunt, a card's hunt too, whose vampire would gain at least 1 blood: at capacity yes, a vampire that cannot gain blood by hunting no (D17; 89.1, 89.26, 59.4; amber); its window after blocks are declined, before the blood, not the would-be-successful window (no pool card tells the two apart for a hunt; amber); a hunt brought to 0 gains nothing and is not a successful hunt, the action still successful (LSJ 20050720-2: "A hunt is not successful if no blood is gained, even if the hunt action is still successful"); a second Foul Blood after the first brought a 1-hunt to 0 is not playable (D13 with LSJ 20050720-2); -1 hunt on a hunt stealing blood takes 1 less (digest §3.1, 64.1, RTR 20030519; amber); the damage 1 normal, unpreventable, environmental, after resolution, even at 0 gained, ordered by the acting Methuselah (the card's text, 23.3; amber); [qui]'s lock an effect, [QUI] none. Green unless noted. Tests: `foul_blood_makes_a_hunt_gain_nothing_and_damages_after`, `foul_blood_superior_leaves_the_reactor_unlocked`, `foul_blood_takes_one_less_from_a_stealing_hunt`.

**121.53** Francis Milliner: Toreador and Toreador antitribu, the clan read live (the text, 13.2). Green. Test: `francis_milliner_hits_harder_on_toreador`.

**121.55** Garrote: strength damage, close range only (melee, D23); the burn is no part of the strike (ANK 20210612, LSJ 20020416-2: "It can be used if the opponent dodges the strike but is still going to torpor"); "this strike" is the weapon whose strike resolved, kept until its damage is applied, dodged or not (Sword of the Archangel's precedent; amber); "the bearer remains ready" is not going to torpor or burned by the damage pending in that strike resolution (Decapitate's precedent, RTR 20010711; amber); a Ghoul Retainer using it is no bearer striking: no burn (TOM 19951114, LSJ 20080210: "using {Garrote} does not allow the bearer to burn the opponent"); not diablerie: burned, no blood hunt, nothing gained; optional (the text). Green unless noted. Tests: `garrote_burns_the_vampire_its_strike_sends_to_torpor`, `garrote_burns_even_when_its_strike_was_dodged`, `garrote_is_not_offered_to_a_bearer_going_down`, `garrote_used_by_a_ghoul_retainer_burns_no_one`.

**121.56** Gemini's Mirror: [obf] the maneuver commits the dodge, not usable while strikes are barred (LSJ 20021028); the vampire and its blood only, not its equipment, retainers or cards: a burn-equipment strike is not flipped (LSJ 20031118); one superior play per vampire each combat (text, D27); a coin for each opposing strike at the vampire, a combat ends one included, tails voiding it (the combat goes on), heads ending the mirror (amber, the design's red downgraded: 113.120, an opposing combat ends strike with no effect on the vampire: "the combat does not end"; both reads count combat ends as "made against"); undodgeable strikes flip too (amber, advanced rules §4.6.4); the striker's own environmental damage (Burst of Sunlight) still taken, a strike's environmental damage on the vampire voided with it (amber; no pool strike in reach carries both, untested). Green unless noted. **121.57** Go-getter [OBF], Shadow Boxing [OBF][POT]: no continuation once the acting vampire went to torpor during the action, even brought back by Warsaw Station (ANK 20220218, LSJ 20090325-2: "the action ends and cannot be continued (eg. with Ambulance)", the ruling general, not Ambulance's alone); they were offered with the actor ready only (fixed; Momentary Delay's "from torpor too", LSJ 20030130, is an actor left in torpor). **121.87** Lorenzo Detuono: used at Amaranth's moment (the going-down step after preventions), not after Amaranth resolves, the first replacement averting the others both ways (ANK 20210705: "Cannot use his ability after Amaranth resolves"; RTR 20010711, the Decapitate loop's precedent; amber); "would not [be ready]" takes an ally about to burn, given 1 life ("with 1 life for an ally with no life"), a vampire with no blood added; "your ready region" is Lorenzo's controller's, for good (a Diamond Thunderbolt may stop it), cards following, its lock unchanged, the combat over, the opposing strike's damage landing first (D11; LSJ 20020618: "If used to steal a vampire in combat, combat ends. Test: `lorenzo_takes_a_vampire_aggravated_damage_would_burn`.

Owner (2026-11-14): any library card name is legal; it must be a single parametric decision (the interface offering e.g. name completion), not ~1,500 options — built for now as the FindCard enumeration; the engine board's "A card named by a single parametric decision" (Z-engine) switches it. Test: `precision_makes_the_prey_discard_the_named_card` (asserts the count).

**121.123** Pseudo-Blindness: "a blocking vampire" is the one attempting to block (the rulebook's blocking minion; amber); "requiring Chimerstry or Obfuscate" the mode played (D29; PIB 20130704); the canceled card's cost paid (ANK 20260216: "The cost for a canceled non-action-card is still paid"); immediately, only wakes before it, the cancel group's rows (LSJ 20061207, LSJ 20021011, LSJ 20080630, ANK 20190104, LSJ 20030224); a modifier played "as if from hand" (The Erciyes Fragments) canceled (LSJ 20060106); [CHI] in the block's combat only, the acting minion's cards played in a mode requiring [chi] or [obf] barred (D29; LSJ 20010813; amber); a dual action modifier/reaction a third party plays as a reaction would still count as a modifier, the printed types read (D18; no pool card is such a dual requiring [chi] or [obf], Ophidian Gaze shares the gap; amber, untested). Green unless noted. Tests: `pseudo_blindness_needs_the_blocker_and_its_disciplines`, `pseudo_blindness_cancels_an_obfuscate_modifier_its_cost_paid`, `no_replacement_in_the_as_played_window`, `a_canceled_wash_is_replaced_at_once`, `a_canceled_modifier_cannot_be_played_again_by_the_same_minion`, `a_canceled_limited_bleed_leaves_the_limit_unused`, `a_wake_in_an_action_cards_as_played_window_lets_it_be_canceled`, `pseudo_blindness_cancels_a_modifier_played_from_the_erciyes_fragments`, `pseudo_blindness_superior_bars_chimerstry_in_the_block_combat`.

**121.124** Quick Jab: one damage inflicted, one to prevent, first strike (LSJ 20071117); the cap applies after every addition (an aim's, Target Vitals' landing +2 too), the kind kept (text). Green. Tests: `quick_jab_strikes_first_for_one`, `quick_jab_caps_an_aimed_strike`.

**121.125** Resume the Coil: no blood paid in either mode (ANK 20181017: "Is not the default action, no blood is paid"); [nec] blocked: no combat, the blocker may diablerize; the vampire out of torpor before resolution: a fizzle, both modes (ANK 20220218, ANK 20210124); [NEC] by a ready vampire at any Methuselah's torpid vampire, another's directed (D11, 16.4). Green. Tests: `resume_the_coil_leaves_torpor_for_free`, `resume_the_coil_superior_rescues_for_free`, `resume_the_coil_blocked_offers_the_diablerie`, `resume_the_coil_fizzles_once_its_vampire_left_torpor`.

**121.126** Retain the Quick Blood: "spends to play" is a card's paid cost, surcharges included, the mode played requiring Celerity or Quietus at either level, never a burn effect (D29, D20; amber); [CEL][QUI] "move 2" is up to 2 (D4; amber); once each unlock phase (D7, 6.6); "this vampire": not played by an ally (63.5, D16). Green unless noted. Tests: `retain_the_quick_blood_stores_celerity_and_quietus_costs`, `retain_the_quick_blood_returns_blood_in_the_unlock_phase`.

**121.127** Retain the Quick Blood: "can be put on this card". Owner (2026-11-14): with one copy on the vampire, the blood spent always goes on the card, nothing asked (storing it is never worse than sending it to the bank); with two or more, its controller picks the copy taking each payment (`Interrupt::KeepSpentBlood`, `choose_card`), so split payments let each copy return blood in the unlock phase. Tests: `retain_the_quick_blood_stores_celerity_and_quietus_costs`, `retain_the_quick_blood_two_copies_take_the_payments_chosen`.

**121.128** Revelation of Despair: "if this vampire blocks, before block resolution" is the blocked window, the blocker locked (advanced rules §5.2.3; Diabolic Lure, Voice of Madness); [ser] burns 1 blood or life (digest §3.14); the acting minion gone before block resolution ends the action, the blocker not locked (ledger, "Damage before block resolution": "the action ends immediately and the blocking minion is not locked"; the engine left the blocker locked, fixed); [SER] "the target minion" is the acting minion (the only minion named; amber); [SER]'s steal optional, at once, the reactor's own counters only, burned, for good, an ally by its printed cost (35.2); stolen, the block stands and the blocker locks (ANK 20220127: "When the acting minion gets stolen, the block still happens and the blocking minion locks"; advanced rules §6.2.2). Green unless noted; the combat after the steal, 121.129. Tests: `revelation_of_despair_burns_an_acting_ally_and_leaves_the_blocker_unlocked`, `revelation_of_despair_steals_the_acting_minion_and_the_block_stands`.

**121.129** Revelation of Despair [SER]: the block's combat after the steal. Owner (2026-11-14): the acting minion stolen before block resolution, the block stands and the blocker locks (ANK 20220127), but no combat begins between the two, now the same Methuselah's (as a canceled block combat, advanced rules §3.5.4; §6.2.2: "Stealing a minion in combat ends the combat"); the action is blocked. Test: `revelation_of_despair_steals_the_acting_minion_and_the_block_stands`.

**121.130** Revelation of Ecstasy: a locked minion is a target (ANK 20170303: "Can target a locked minion"); the steal ignores the card's requirements, optional, for good (TOM 19960226-1); "capacity or cost" is a vampire's capacity, an ally's printed cost, a card become an ally 0 (35.2; LSJ 20080804: "Once in play, is an ally with a cost of zero"). Green. Tests: `revelation_of_ecstasy_locks_a_prey_minion_even_locked`, `revelation_of_ecstasy_superior_steals_at_capacity_without_requirements`, `revelation_of_ecstasy_steals_a_sets_curse_ally_at_once`.

**121.131** Revelation of Ecstasy [SER]: "their capacity or cost" of a represented ally (a Spell of Life mummy, a Khazar's Diary wraith). Owner (2026-11-14): a represented crypt card's capacity (a crypt card's cost is its capacity, 71.9; 35.2): the steal comes once the counters reach it; a represented library card (the Diary's wraith) reads its printed cost as an ally card does, 0 if it has none (`GameState::ally_cost`, every "cost" reading of a represented ally). Test: `revelation_of_ecstasy_steals_a_mummy_at_its_capacity`.

**121.132** Revelation of Wrath: played after the presses, a round ended early too (RTR 20030519); by a vampire going to torpor (ANK 20201122, ANK 20200926); the counters are its player's, no control taken (ANK 20200925, ANK 20211105, 25.1); [SER]'s second counter on 3 damage or more, or 2 aggravated or more, this round (text); "a vampire can play only one … each round" counts per minion, an ally's too (amber, text, D4). What damage counts: 121.133. Green unless noted. Tests: `revelation_of_wrath_corrupts_after_a_round_of_two_damage`, `revelation_of_wrath_by_a_vampire_going_to_torpor`, `revelation_of_wrath_superior_adds_one_for_three`, `revelation_of_wrath_once_each_round`.

**121.133** Revelation of Wrath: which damage "the opposing minion inflicted (even if prevented)" counts. Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: only the opposing minion's strikes' damage on this vampire this round, prevented or immune damage counting, dodged not, never damage on its retainers nor environmental damage, so an opponent burning its Ghoul Retainer with a 2-damage hand strike gives no Revelation (as Haymaker, 116.44: "the damage each minion's strikes inflicted on the other this round"; 116.138; ANK 20200925; the second reader counted any damage the opposing minion inflicts, on any target, strike or not, digest §3.20). Test: `revelation_of_wrath_ignores_retainer_and_environmental_damage`.

**121.134** Rooftop Shadow: "has Celerity" any level at [cel], superior at [CEL], read as played, the intercept kept if the acting minion gains Celerity later (The Mole [cel]; amber); the intercept only when needed (D15). **121.135** Sajid al Misbah: [qui] cards cost him 1 fewer blood, the cost only (D29, D20, 38.4); cannot block older vampires, by capacity, read live, allies neither (D30). Green. Test: `sajid_pays_less_for_quietus_and_cannot_block_older_vampires`.

**121.137** Selective Silence: [qui] a maneuver in the first round only (text); [QUI] the blood is an effect, no reduction applies, not playable without it (ANK 20210226, D20); once set the range is not reset and Determine Range is skipped (RTR 19970630, ANK 20180720); an earlier setter has priority, so it is not offered then (PIB 20120214, 84.1); not offered to an ally, which has no blood (63.5, D16). **121.139** Seterpenre: before any contest, the contests staged after the picks (LSJ 20080526); up to four, each from hand, ash heap or library, a Pass stopping, put (no as-played window, requirements ignored, the level cap kept), the library shuffled after the last (`DisciplineSearch`'s reading, LSJ 20051116-2, D2; amber); a hand card replaced at once (advanced rules §6.8: "Whenever an effect ... adds or removes cards from your hand, you immediately discard down or draw up to match"); the Discipline cards add no capacity to any capacity reader, the Disciplines still count (the words). **121.141** Shambling Hordes: its controller may decline the removal and burn it (LSJ 20030701-2); its strength read as strike resolution begins: a simultaneous steal does not lower it, first-strike damage does (LSJ 20030307, LSJ 20100119); a life paid for its strike lowers it first (LSJ 20090703); its entry effect however it enters (Pressing Flesh; ANK 20170309-2); life it would gain goes to the bank; [NEC] 4 life and 4 strength; put in play by another card, its basic version (85.30). **121.142** Sheepdog: no stealth; "this vampire" a vampire only (63.5); blood over capacity drains; its next own unlock phase skipped, the card burned in it, the one after unlocking (106.12, Serpent's Numbing Kiss's shape; amber); an infernal vampire keeps its paid unlock (LSJ 20050114). **121.143** Shell Break: any Methuselah's ready unique non-wraith non-zombie minion, locked too, one's own undirected (D11, D12, D28); "about to be burned" is any burn of the bearer, the move mandatory; its Giovanni out of play: it burns with the bearer, in torpor: it moves (amber); played on its own Giovanni, it burns with him (amber); from then it is a retainer and a wraith for every card, its +1 bleed and +1 intercept only after the move, the employer's; immune to non-aggravated damage, which is not inflicted (LSJ 20010627, LSJ 20010629, LSJ 20090304-1: "Immune means damage is not inflicted, even if non-preventable or outside combat"; advanced rules §4.5.5). Every retainer-wide read of a card in play sees it: combat damage to a minion's retainers, Weather Control, retainers moved with a minion, Stanislava's and Midget's text loss, Can't Take it with You's count, a referendum's chosen card, War Ghoul's entry burn (the card's "it becomes a wraith retainer"); Weather Control's damage to the combatants' retainers is non-aggravated, so it takes none (LSJ 20010627). Burned, the card in the ash heap is an action card, not a retainer: Compel the Spirit ("an ally or retainer that has been burned from play") cannot take it back, and it is not counted among the retainers burned from play ("becomes" lasts while in play; amber, untested). Green unless noted. Tests: `shell_break_needs_a_unique_non_wraith_minion_without_one`, `shell_break_moves_to_its_giovanni_as_a_wraith_retainer`, `shell_break_burns_with_its_bearer_once_its_giovanni_is_gone`, `shell_break_on_its_giovanni_burns_and_moves_to_a_torpid_one`, `shell_break_retainer_is_immune_to_non_aggravated_damage`, `shell_break_retainer_counts_for_cant_take_it_with_you`.

**121.144** Shemti: his damage environmental, normal, out of combat (RTR 19970630); ordered by the acting Methuselah among the after-resolution effects (ANK 20180206, 23.3); "successful" includes a fizzle (D1, digest §3.2); "ready and unlocked" read as the after-resolution window opens (D13; amber); directed at his controller or a card they control, as resolved, a hunt nothing (D12, 17.2, 50.3; amber); his title kept through a sect change (LSJ 20010714). **121.145** Shroudsight: the action must require Necromancy or Giovanni through its card, never a rulebook action (41.12); "you" is its controller, the acting Methuselah (D11); "you can" is optional, one move (D8); the action card, out of play until the action ends, goes to the library's top unshuffled as it would reach the ash heap (76.22 Epikasta Rigatos; amber); [NEC] moves one card chosen among those its player played during the action and now in their ash heap, the action card, Shroudsight, modifiers, combat cards, canceled ones too ("As above, but" keeps the single move, digest §4.5; D4; amber); a card removed from the game as it resolves never reaches the ash heap (ANK 20170331).
