**121.146** Shroudsight after a blocked action ("After action resolution (successful or not)"). Owner (2026-11-14): offered after a blocked action too, after its combat; 55.5 corrected. Test: `shroudsight_returns_the_action_card_to_the_library_top`.

**121.147** Soul Feasting: [NEC]'s +1 on either hunt (amber); the wraith chosen at declaration, gone before resolution: the action fizzles with no hunt, neither the +3 nor the base hunt, and Loki's Gift's [PRO] mode, its vampire chosen, likewise (D1, 117.1 cause 1/, 117.3; Loki's Gift untested); "a wraith" is an ally or a retainer, a Khazar's Diary wraith and a Shell Break retainer included (amber); directed only at another's wraith, a special hunt once each turn, vampires only (the rulebook: "A minion can only perform this special hunt once per turn"). **121.148** Spell of Life: "only one each turn" counts every Methuselah's play (D27); five copies anyone's, this one included; with more in play its user picks the five, anyone's, one at a time, then the mummies (LSJ 20090428: "The copies you burn need not be ones you control"; D1; the engine chose them, fixed); the burn untimed, whenever its controller holds the impulse (digest §5.1); "up to five" chosen one at a time, a pass stops (D1, D14; amber); life = printed capacity, the slot's blood to the bank, cards on the slot follow (LSJ 20100716; amber); unique by name, the name in play, contesting unique minions of that name, not non-unique vampires (LSJ 20070928-2, LSJ 20071001-2); put in play, they act this turn, their rush at another Methuselah's ready minion (119.1, 17.3); burned, a vampire in the ash heap, not burned from play (LSJ 20071014); the ability is its copy's controller's (D5, D11). Green unless noted. **121.149** Strike at the True Flesh: played after strikes are chosen, on its player's own melee weapon (RTR 19990105, LSJ 20020425); +1 damage of the strike's kind, none on a strike dealing none (RTR 19970630, TOM 19960225, RTR 19960221; ledger n/a: every pool melee weapon strike deals damage); aggravated damage from the weapon stays preventable (text); a dodge is not prevention, unaffected (rulebook). Who may prevent: 121.150. Green. Tests: `strike_at_the_true_flesh_bars_card_prevention`, `strike_at_the_true_flesh_spares_dodges_and_aggravated_prevention`.

**121.150** Strike at the True Flesh: "cannot be prevented except by equipment or by other strikes" against prevention set up earlier in the combat. Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: an equipment's prevention (Leather Jacket) and a strike's own (Stunt Cycle, Forearm Block) reach the damage; prevention cards and allowances from cards played earlier (Apparition [chi], Bear's Skin) do not, and a prevention card is not offered when only that damage is pending (Apparition is neither equipment nor a strike; no precedent; both reads agree in substance). **121.151** Sundervere, The Devil Brahmin: "During an action ... +1 bleed" a bleed's only, before resolution, once (group G00117 on Virginie's identical "During an action, Virginie can burn 1 blood to get +1 bleed": "can only be used during a bleed action before Action Resolution", ANK 20231124, LSJ 19970718; amber, Sundervere not in the group); "After Sundervere is burned, you burn 1 pool": any burn while in play, ready or torpid, his controller as he burns, an oust handled (The Capuchin's burn site, D25; amber); contested, he gives nothing (engine.md §6.12's convention; amber). **121.161** Target Leg: a press or maneuver granted earlier by a card requiring Obfuscate, Blood Sorcery or Flight. Owner (2026-11-14): it stays usable: Hunter's Mark [cel][tha] requires Blood Sorcery, so its press does (D29); each banked maneuver or press records its card's Disciplines (`combat::Side::banked_from`, an ability's the mode its card in play was played with), and under Target Leg only those requiring one of the three are offered and spent; a card played then for its own maneuver or press (Swallowed by the Night [OBF]) still is. **121.162** Temptation: on one's own vampire undirected, its steal unlocking it (LSJ 20010828, ANK 20180917); a counter once each master phase, no master phase action (D7); the steal optional, once in the minion phase at any point of it, counters at least the blood read live, 0 against 0 too (LSJ 20001201, LSJ 20010828-2); each copy apart, the card staying (LSJ 20010227); temporary control, removed if a Methuselah is ousted, returning in torpor (ANK 20181110, LSJ 20081105, LSJ 20010119); a blocker, a combatant or an action's target stolen (LSJ 20080326, LSJ 20020618, LSJ 20020725); the attached vampire in torpor still steals, no "ready" printed (D25; amber). Green unless noted. Tests: `temptation_on_ones_own_vampire_is_undirected_and_unlocks_it`, `temptation_counts_up_and_steals_until_the_end_of_the_turn`, `temptation_with_no_counters_steals_an_empty_vampire`, `temptation_superior_enters_with_a_counter`, `temptation_copies_count_their_counters_apart`, `temptation_temporary_control_and_ousts`, `temptation_steals_a_blocker_or_a_combatant`, `temptation_stealing_the_target_fizzles_the_action`, `temptation_steals_a_vampire_in_torpor`.

**121.163** Trophy: Library, Trophy: No Questions, Trophy: Revered: no effect until moved to a vampire (rulebook "Trophy": "It has no effect until it is moved to a vampire"); controlled by its vampire's controller, following a steal (rulebook "Trophy"); caps ("no more than two", "only one") masked at the award; several moved at one award, one searched, Trophy: Chosen's take-or-burn only for a trophy with blood (rulebook "Red List"); No Questions' 2 counters as it enters play, played or searched (amber; text); one counter as its vampire's action is announced, no block attempt, once per action, burned at 0 (66.1, D8); Revered's +2 bleed not limited (amber; digest §3.3). Green unless noted. Tests: `trophy_library_counts_only_once_awarded_and_follows_its_vampire`, `trophies_awarded_together_and_capped`, `trophy_no_questions_awarded_with_its_counters`, `trophy_no_questions_makes_an_action_unblockable`, `trophy_revered_gives_two_bleed`.

**121.164** Truth of a Thousand Lies: [SER] played after resolution of a successful action, a fizzle included, only after-resolution plays following it (3.6; ANK 20190425: "Cards and effects that can be played 'after resolution' (and only them) can be played after it"); still offered on an already-unlocked vampire, a legal no-op (D14). Green. Tests: `truth_of_a_thousand_lies_bleeds_and_unlocks_after_success`, `truth_of_a_thousand_lies_superior_on_an_unlocked_vampire`.

**121.165** Truth of Blood: blocked, the blocker's Methuselah discards 2 at random before block resolution, first, held to the action's end, through Change of Target too (the "Triggered by block" group, ANK 20220116; RTR 19991206); with fewer than 2 in hand, all there are (D4). Green. Tests: `truth_of_blood_blocked_costs_the_blocker_two_cards_until_the_action_ends`, `truth_of_blood_blocked_discards_what_the_hand_holds`.

**121.166** Tutu the Doubly Evil One: his dodge once each combat (D32); his unlock once in his minion phase, any time in it, never while unlocked (D7, 26.5, 48.7); his steal takes another Methuselah's torpid vampire's equipment (never his own Methuselah's), directed at its controller, a move checking no requirement but "can hold" (one vehicle, a unique copy) (90.4, 116.6, digest §3.7; amber); burned, shuffled into the library and still burned (ANK 20200813), discarded, not (RTR 20010711). Green unless noted. Tests: `tutu_dodges_once_each_combat`, `tutu_steals_equipment_from_a_torpid_vampire_and_unlocks_once`, `nephren_ka_and_tutu_shuffle_into_the_library_when_burned`.

**121.167** Unholy Penance: put on after combat ends (RTR 19970630); lost if dodged, the combat continuing or a new one beginning (RTR 20041202, RTR 20020501, LSJ 19980109); at any range (rulebook: combat ends at any range); "+1 bleed" is the striking vampire's against the host's controller, read live, an ally striker none (D13, D16); burned by its host as a +1 stealth action, undirected (D12); a second still played, not put on (99.2). Green. Tests: `unholy_penance_goes_on_after_combat_at_any_range`, `unholy_penance_is_lost_if_dodged_or_the_combat_continues`, `unholy_penance_is_burned_by_its_minion`, `unholy_penance_second_is_played_not_put_on`.

**121.168** Urban Jungle: the 2 votes are no Methuselah's, so no bribe, Scorn of Adonis, Marcos Belegrad or Carlak reader charges or counts its player (Carlak and the bribes now skip National Guard Support's counters too) (amber; 116.62); every blood hunt referendum, a political action's too (amber; text); once a game across the table (D27, 40.1); "Inconnu" has no engine meaning (no reader; untested). Green unless noted. Tests: `urban_jungle_adds_two_votes_against_every_blood_hunt`, `urban_jungle_once_a_game`.

**121.169** Vasiliy Vasilevich: his unlock once after the bleed, not offered while unlocked (16.1, D8, 48.7); "your prey" his controller's prey as the bleed's target resolved, a bleed redirected away (Deflection to his predator) giving nothing (50.3; amber). Green unless noted. Test: `vasiliy_unlocks_after_bleeding_his_prey`.

**121.170** Vassily Taltos: any Methuselah's hunting ground or Blood Doll, directed at its controller, undirected at his own, 0 stealth (D11, D12, 17.2, 20.1). Green. Test: `vassily_taltos_burns_a_hunting_ground_or_a_blood_doll`.

**121.171** Wall Street Night, Financial Newspaper ("A minion you control can lock this location to attempt to move 1 counter from an investment card to your pool as a +1 stealth Ⓓ action"; its "no investment card is in the pool, inert" false since Secret Horde, implemented this round): any investment card in play, another Methuselah's making the action directed at its controller (17.2, a controlled card), one's own undirected (20.1, Ⓓ only indicative); the location locks as the action is declared, even if the action fails (amber; Mobile HQ's precedent, ANK 20221102); the card emptied burns by its own text (121.1). Green unless noted. Test: `wall_street_night_takes_a_counter_from_an_investment_as_an_action`.

**121.172** Warrant: no younger ready vampire to choose, not played (LSJ 20100129, ANK 20191228); younger than the acting vampire, chosen in the terms (D6, D30); any other Methuselah's ready vampire rushes the bearer, 1 blood paid at resolution (17.3, D20, 3.6); the bearer no longer ready at resolution: successful with no effect, the blood unpaid (17.3). Green. Tests: `warrant_is_not_played_without_a_younger_ready_vampire`, `warrant_lets_any_vampire_rush_its_bearer_for_a_blood`, `warrant_rush_at_a_bearer_no_longer_ready_has_no_effect`.

**121.173** Whispers from the Dead: the card named as declared (LSJ 20010627: "The card to retrieve must be announced when declaring the action"), gone by resolution: a fizzle (117.3); the discard down after, its discards not replaced; [NEC] +1 stealth from declaration. Green. Tests: `whispers_from_the_dead_fetches_the_card_named_as_declared`, `whispers_from_the_dead_superior_has_stealth`.

**121.174** Will-o'-the-Wisp: [chi] any time before resolution, no attempt needed (LSJ 20020612); X = 0 allowed at [chi], one point, nothing paid (LSJ 20030502: "X can be zero, but not less than zero"); points and picks chosen as played, among any Methuselah's minions, a minion taking several points (D1, D11); choosing is targeting at both levels: a minion the acting Methuselah's actions cannot target (Secure Haven) gets no [chi] point, as it is never one of [CHI]'s picks (35.1, LSJ 20031010; the [chi] points were not checked, fixed); [CHI] gives no points (no "As above", digest §4.5); its "cannot play reaction cards" bars cards only, not [REACTION] powers (the text); its picks younger than the acting vampire (D30). Green; [CHI]'s cost with no pick, 121.175. Tests: `will_o_the_wisp_spreads_intercept_points`, `will_o_the_wisp_gives_no_point_to_a_secure_haven_vampire`, `will_o_the_wisp_superior_bars_chosen_minions`, `will_o_the_wisp_superior_with_no_pick_costs_one_and_gives_no_points`.

**121.175** Will-o'-the-Wisp [CHI] with no minion chosen. Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: it is played at X = 1, 1 blood, choosing X−1 = 0 minions; X = 0 is never offered at [CHI], X−1 ≥ 0 bounding X, as Command the Legion's "X-1", which shares the reading (LSJ 20030502's "X can be zero" read as not reaching a choice of −1 minions). Test: `will_o_the_wisp_superior_with_no_pick_costs_one_and_gives_no_points`.

**121.176** Zapaderin: played by the acting Methuselah's ready unlocked Ravnos, copies by different Ravnos stacking (LSJ 19990425, ANK 20200515); any time before resolution, no attempt needed (LSJ 20020612); any Methuselah's allies and younger vampires (D11); "younger than this modifying Ravnos" read live, both capacities as intercept is computed (D13; it was a snapshot taken as played, Perfect Paragon's primitive too, fixed). Green. Test: `zapaderin_lowers_younger_intercept_by_each_ravnos`.

**121.177** Text-only vampires (Ahmose Chambers, Anjalika Underwood, Belle Equitone, Bertrand d'Anjou, Brian Thompson, Clea Auguste d'Holford, Evan Rogers, Kashan, Kay Polerno): their printed traits generated, no definition; Bertrand d'Anjou, Evan Rogers and Kashan blood cursed, Kashan 1 vote titled (the cards). **121.178** Text-only vampires (Layla bint-Nadr, Marla Kenyon, Neel Ramanathan, Renenet, Sisocharis, Stephen Milliner, Sukainah): no definition; Layla and Sukainah blood cursed, generated (the cards). Green. Test: `the_round_30_text_only_vampires_carry_their_printed_traits`.

**121.179** An ally playing a card as a vampire (73.6; Herald of Topheth, Infernal Servitor, Vivienne Géroux, Grey Thorne, Alia, God's Messenger): for the card it so plays it meets that card's "this vampire" (`Requirement::ActorVampire`: Shadow Feint, Sympathetic Agony, Eyes of Blades) and is younger-compared by its granted capacity (Herald of Topheth's Inspire Greatness, "The acting ally or younger vampire", on an acting vampire below 5); any other reader still sees an ally (Powerbase: Zürich, as Powerbase: Munich, 73.6). Green (73.6). Test: `an_ally_playing_as_a_vampire_is_one_for_that_card_only`.

**121.180** Meditative Grove canceling an action card as it is played on a Salubri (Bloodlust's frenzy, played on each chosen minion): what the canceled action card costs. Owner (2026-11-14): confirmed as built: the action card's cost is not paid, its blood being paid at resolution (77.4) and a canceled action never resolving, though Grove prints "(cost is still paid)": its ruling names only non-action cards ("The cost for a canceled non-action-card is still paid", ANK 20260216; rulebook 2024; 77.4). Test: `bloodlust_is_played_on_its_chosen_minions`.
